# DEOS Actors Package Architecture

> Package: `pallet-deos-actors`; Rust crate: `pallet_deos_actors`

This document maps the independently reusable crate implementation. A host supplies `PalletId`, account derivation context, origins, adapters, bounds, fees, genesis actors, and production weights. Concrete DEOS namespace, System accounts, TMCTOL plans, runtime adapters, and operational evidence belong in [`docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md`](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md).

## Executive Summary

> This document records the shipped package implementation; the standalone specification owns normative semantics and executable tests own conformance.

After a committed completion, StopCycle or terminal failure, an Idle Actor with no signal or Run leaves Service. `place_idle_service_after_cycle` reuses the fresh-publication plan: Protocol Disabled without a window, or the independent Block Deadline for window expiry. Temporal detector authority remains independent; ParkedBalance restores its bounded current-state review. Semantic identity, custody and holds survive. A new useful Trigger returns through ordinary paid readiness and B+1; this is passive waiting, not owner pause or deactivation.

Four storage/API fixtures now use a shared canonical passive witness after direct or mandatory zero-Step completion, permanent retry failure and funded retry completion. They retain exact generation/identity/certificate and full Contract/body commitments, Idle with no latch/Run or temporal/Service carrier, Protocol Disabled/no residence and the completion block. This replaces obsolete Service-only reads, not canonical state: those readers correctly refuse passive sources. Resource admission/refusal/settlement, retry Deadline/B+1 chronology, one committed transfer and unchanged custody remainder stay asserted. Focused default/all-feature checks pass; reference pricing and public policy admission remain separate.

The pre-correction native witness remains sealed in `target/idle-inspection-evidence/qualification.json`. One paid zero-Step Manual Cycle formerly retained Service(Pending); two quiet turns charged positive declared work without another payment. Its conditional source invariant permitted N admitted turns under unchanged authority/input and increasing representable blocks. This historical result established a missing payment owner, not a physical maximum, public waiting or an infinite-clock claim.

The owner selected passive completion. Current native checks cover no-signal quiet blocks, new paid B+1 readiness, zero/nonempty completion, abort, retry recovery, temporal sources and window expiry. Zero-Step window placement adds the existing Service-to-Deadline allowance to its complete envelope; policy host control remains separately authenticated. Fresh diagnostic branch profiles and physical qualification remain distinct: the new placement does not close collector/invariant-refusal backing, production maxima, tariffs or generated binding.

`passive_window_placement_refusal_restores_paid_source_and_committed_prefix` checks the shared passive-window destination after User/System zero-Step Opening and final Running Transfer. A synthetic malformed full Deadline page rejects late placement with `CorruptCarrier`; complete-root equality preserves the paid latch or committed prefix, fees, events, Run, process and peers. Repair admits the same source without another Trigger, completes once and publishes only the independent window Deadline. Default and benchmark/try-runtime checks pass. This is native transaction/authority conformance, not public reachability of the malformed carrier, physical capacity, caller containment, tariff sufficiency or Wasm acceptance.

The scoped correction passes 92 default and 98 benchmark/try-runtime pallet checks, 28 all-feature embedding checks and eleven reference-runtime checks. One fresh diagnostic Wasm (`b64ac02a7ca32e18177235a536d5466e7fda73703dc569c3185e5a7aea283aca`, 6,453,713 bytes) runs the ordinary System/User zero-Step mains and raw-only `scheduler_inner_zero_step_passive_window`, with three timing rows and one database row each. The window point checks exact fee/hold and expiry residence; the other points check Protocol Disabled. `target/passive-idle-evidence/qualification.json` binds these sources and artifacts. Selected singleton geometry does not certify complete physical costs or production coefficients.

The reference integration now also qualifies finite complete-block quiet/Session and Governance-cleanup inputs for passive residence and the two-Step Fee Sink. Named preset registrations separate bounded cleanup checks from full diagnostic construction; exact completed-preset reuse avoids rerunning a terminal prefix solely because the composite timer expired. The [integration owner](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#current-distribution-and-evidence-domain) owns source identities, conditional clock recurrence and finite replay scope. Historical resident-Service coordinates remain historical; physical placement/cleanup and finite PVF acceptance stay open.

`ensure_contract_fits_service_budget` now checks independent fresh Control/effect domains in addition to the combined idle envelope. Its conservative Contract maximum includes common inspection, ring suffix, terminal cleanup, exact zero-Step/Step geometry, Parked completion and authenticated optional financial/policy prices; StopCycle's allowance belongs to Control. `mandatory_actor_control_envelopes` shares the Prepass/Deadline/review/finalization declaration with execution. Creation, replacement, activation, genesis and try-state use the same guard; Prepass rejects a host allocation below any configured minimum domain. The reference authority commits its configured budget. This closes a declaration-admission defect, not physical qualification, collector-refusal backing or production binding; earlier replay/Wasm results retain their source scopes.

A capacity-only refusal leaves the eligible cursor unchanged and ends that pass. The block protocol permits one Base pass and one Drain pass; insertion appends behind the existing cursor, and the next round does not reset it. Under sound complete declarations, a conforming fresh allocation, positive execution/scan limits, unchanged eligible authority and successful funding/collection, the refused Step is admitted first in the next Prepass: at most two preceding capacity-refusal inspections belong to that turn.

The existing sixteen-case native boundary test now recovers through public `actor_prepass`, including mandatory work and protected finalization, then checks Q1 through `on_idle`. Synthetic prior usage does not prove a reachable whole-block load. The conditional count neither prices the inspections nor supplies their payer, and excludes freeze, invariant, collector and financial-wait failures. The [integration owner](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#fee-composition) retains exact evidence and qualification limits.

Canonical Service terminal selection loads the complete authored Active state only after admitting the complete Close envelope, then calls `remove_actor_publication_and_finalize`. Its bounded current-Step view may repeat the selected Step across the declared count and is not full-Contract equality authority. Mixed-Step Suspended insolvency tests cover terminal commit, consumed work under rollback, custody-neutral hold/slot release and missing-price refusal; they use existing native model prices, not production coefficients.

The public Contract now authors `close_when` and `on_unfunded`; legacy completion types, fields and productive-Cycle authority are removed from Rust. Header and current-Step view carry only `has_close_when`, keeping variable expression width out of their common geometry. Present policy admission requires available host Control, inspection and Close declarations plus ordinary capacity/certificate checks; unavailable policy resources return `BehaviorPolicyUnavailable`. The cold authored-policy companion is installed, while complete production resource qualification remains open. The internal Service transaction now reduces authenticated candidates and commits monitor/Close atomically; prepared native sources exercise this path. The configured four-clause/four-atom codec bounds are preparation limits, not a physically accepted production policy domain. Only OnUnfunded Close is admitted; waiting and complete policy qualification remain Gate B work in [`BACKLOG.md`](../../../../BACKLOG.md).

The cold owner stores canonical expression, derived plan and the lifecycle generation that started the episode. Full reconstruction authenticates the Active lifecycle/certificate, plan and generation range. Episode identity hashes Actor ID, start generation and semantic ID; re-certification rebinds the plan without resetting values, while semantic replacement starts at the successor generation. Destruction removes only required owners. Absent policy performs no optional lookup/removal; try-state detects orphan or inconsistent records. Raw-geometry tests cover missing/forged scope, another Actor, replacement, re-certification, rollback and destruction. Prepared policy current-Step loading now requires authenticated cold/certificate scope and available Step/zero-Step/Close maxima. Public policy admission depends on host declarations; native witnesses are not physical Weight qualification.

`behavior_source_snapshot` measures `load_step_behavior_policy` with the caller's loaded header. Six sources cover absent policy, threshold-one current outcome, sparse accumulation and a full four-by-four policy in Idle, Running and Suspended. The full-policy cases reuse configured-length Cadenced witnesses: completed early return, committed Transfer followed by ContinueNextStep failure, and committed Transfer followed by Temporary suspension. Their real Run records retain committed-block and host-error payloads; staged monitor state follows those prefix observations. Required monitor and clean-Cycle Run bindings use the actual loaders; the maximum expression spans all ten metrics. Snapshot loading preserves the complete storage root. Optional authority remains prepared component state: these profiles do not qualify public policy admission, every legal semantic/Run width or the host's maximum inspection price.

`behavior_service_policy_context` isolates the existing cold policy authentication and resource-context derivation used during Service inspection. It reuses the same six source shapes through a benchmark-only facade, receives the caller's header and preserves the complete root. Native recorded-key checks find no reads for absent policy and exactly the policy/semantic records when present; head, monitor and Run are excluded even for Running/Suspended sources. The profile is raw-only and excluded from generation/merge. Host-price checks, ordinary caller inspection and complete Service admission remain separate; the larger Step snapshot is not this owner's exact read set. This is component qualification, not public policy admission or a complete inspection coefficient.

`behavior_policy_installation` measures first-installation policy preparation and publication through the same `prepare_contract_behavior_state` / `replace_contract_behavior_policy` owners used by lifecycle installation. Four categorical sources cover absence, stateless current outcome, sparse accumulation and the configured four-by-four policy with full temporal Contract length. Commit initializes a fresh episode; rollback preserves the full root. No host resource price is supplied. Ordinary semantic/head/body publication, public caller validation, holds, Creation backing and complete installation bounds remain outside this raw-only component; reconfiguration is not first installation.

`behavior_candidate_publish` measures the actual reducer, source comparison and candidate publication in an enclosing transaction, with source loading outside the measured body. The same six cases cover a passing predicate, successful Action and normal plan-end observations; `r=0` commits and `r=1` restores the exact root and monitor. The threshold-one case reports satisfaction without allocating a monitor; accumulated cases update counters and clear clean-Cycle flags. Hot/Run epilogue, Task effects, fees and Close remain separate owners. The script admits raw samples only and excludes both profiles from default Weight generation. Database write counters after rollback describe committed backend I/O, not the provisional work's absence.

`OnUnfunded::{Close, AwaitFunding}` has one-byte SCALE encoding. The field adds one byte to authored Contract, stored header and header metadata ceiling, changing semantic/admission identities while preserving the Steps-only body commitment. Actual User head backing adds one state-byte tariff; reserved Run and Hot widths are unchanged. Creation, activation, replacement, genesis and certificate construction refuse unavailable waiting before publication; Current-Step geometry and full reconstruction accept prepared waiting headers only with explicit entry/cleanup host prices; prepared Service also needs its inspection price; public admission remains closed. Native public-path tests preserve complete roots on refusal. Admitted Close retains §10.7's existing source/payment dispositions, including Running non-invoked funding continuation; it does not promise destruction at every shortfall. AwaitFunding has no fallback to Close. Generated metadata, quote vectors, Weight and Wasm qualification remain open.

The storage-free `financial_wait_expiry_tick` helper first adds exactly 2,419,200,000 ms to consensus time, then rounds that absolute endpoint upward to a Tick. Its checked horizon includes phase relative to the current floor tick, and its rounded endpoint must remain reachable by the `u64` millisecond clock. Native tests cover 500 ms and non-divisor periods, exact/insufficient delay ceilings and overflow. This prepares the specification §10.7 clock owner; no financial wait, Deadline publication, backing or expiry worker is installed by this helper.

The storage-free `FinancialWaitRecord` distinguishes owed AtTime Trigger, Pipeline Opening, Running Action and Suspended Action, including the retained retry position. It binds immutable User scope, complete Contract/paid-machine authority, optional behavior episode, liability, original entry time, Tick duration and expiry. Binding rejects a changed Tick duration even when the rounded Tick endpoint collides. Its borrowed witness authenticates a caller-owned episode identity before current-spendability classification; it never writes state or changes the deadline. Funded classification precedes expired insolvency. There is no payment-ticket or credit ledger in this component. Its codec ceiling and component tests do not install canonical wait residence, establish funding, or qualify physical costs.

The optional `ActorFinancialWaits` companion retains the record, original admission/Pipeline bindings and original identity; its `u128` codec ceiling is 331 bytes. Reconstructed original binding authenticates the unchanged episode independently of renewed execution authority. A unit `ProcessStatus::AwaitingFunding` owns one Tick Deadline while parked, or ordinary Service during tentative funded restoration. It adds no field or encoded maximum to shared semantic/process, head or Run owners. User AwaitFunding body backing reserves the full companion before entry; Close takes no companion read/reservation. The loader and TryRuntime authenticate original scope/debt/expiry and current Contract, Run, behavior episode and residence. Enlarged hold/price and physical inputs remain unqualified; native ports are model prices.

`financial_wait_runtime_clock_change_preserves_deadline_and_tentative_service` qualifies prepared canonical source preservation for all four unpaid boundaries in parked Deadline and tentative funded Service. With valid AtTime debt under both clocks, 60,000/60,001 ms periods share expiry Tick 40,323. The changed clock rejects canonical loading, producer ingress, the applicable Service/Deadline worker and current-certificate renewal. Complete roots, native/local custody, fees, producer backing, process and original episode remain; performed model work is retained. Restoring the original clock re-authenticates the same state and passes try-state. The mock keeps its default 500 ms period and restores it after each case. This is native refusal qualification, not public waiting, an upgrade/migration disposition, physical backing or arbitrary clock-transition acceptance.

The canonical `publish_financial_wait` transaction authenticates the unpaid boundary, current spendability and checked 28-day clock, then publishes process/companion/Deadline together after detaching Service/retry and temporal Trigger authority. It preserves paid authority, counters and committed effects without moving custody or collecting the unpaid fee. Prepared temporal dispatch now owns unpaid AtTime entry before one-shot consumption; Service owns Opening, Running and Suspended entry. Entry work and every reachable cleanup branch require admission; native model bounds are not physical or economic qualification. Prepared source and joint monitor/wait witnesses remain native models; public conformance and complete cost qualification remain open.

Temporal dispatch loads authenticated current state before source extraction and transfers an insolvent AtTime source into the original-boundary wait transaction. Independent terminals retain precedence. With optional wait qualification, the common temporal admission maximum additionally reserves entry and the complete current lifecycle maximum; ordinary successful occurrences settle only their unchanged occurrence owner. Refusal preserves the exact source and retains conservatively admitted entry/cleanup work. Without optional host prices, the ordinary envelope is unchanged and waiting remains unavailable. Native witnesses cover both resource dimensions, late hold rollback, Immutable and zero-Step entry, funded recovery and custody-neutral expiry. Physical selector/loading/control composition and backing remain unqualified.

The Tick worker dispatches financial expiry separately from retry. It admits optional due-control plus the current host lifecycle maximum before Actor-specific inspection and verifies actual consensus time, so grace never expires early. Due inspection authenticates the full retained Contract and applies ordinary stored-state terminals before solvency; an expired Window keeps `WindowExpired` even when funded. Otherwise insolvency selects atomic `FundingGraceExpired`, while funded expiry tentatively publishes B+1 Service without a fee or Action. Original episode/debt/expiry and paid progress remain until the boundary commits or retires. Failures restore the source and retain incurred model work. Native witnesses cover all four boundaries, Window precedence through direct/Tick paths, funded missing-tail refusal and late cleanup/hold rollback. Full loading/classification adds optional due work; physical and economic qualification remain open.

Native witnesses use admitted post-movement hints for all four unpaid boundaries. Duplicate hints retain the B+1 member; two solvent returns followed by unavailable funds repark the same episode without a new receipt, Actor fee, observation or expiry. Every producer and Service visit retains its model Weight. Native Prepass/Drain/finalization also preserve overdue Deadline and tentative Service waiting through freeze; same-block thaw cannot resume work. After thaw, funded work obeys B+1 and insolvency closes custody unchanged. A joint two-Step witness preserves one committed transfer, monitor counters and clean flags through freeze, then reaches authored Close or grace expiry. The mock lifecycle maximum includes configured policy/monitor and financial cleanup together; the former financial-only bound refused joint expiry. These witnesses establish no reference admission or physical tariff.

`FinancialWaitReceipt` retains the generation-bound Actor, original episode identity and unpaid boundary, including after Close removes the companion. Due work returns it transiently; committed entry emits `FinancialWaitEntered` and recovery emits `FinancialWaitRecovered` with the same scope. These events add bounded ABI/proof work, not stored history or encoded Contract/Run capacity. Metadata, descriptors and physical event/control bounds still need coordinated qualification.

Prepared Running/Suspended Action recovery uses ordinary Service and its fail-closed financial inspection/retirement price. Episode retirement and current-time Cadenced rearm occur in the owning Step transaction; the next anchored point is strictly future, with no catch-up or second Pipeline fee. Effect, fee, placement and recovery receipt commit together. Collector or late control refusal restores the exact wait with cadence still unarmed, the paid prefix and native effects while retaining incurred work. Cadenced AwaitFunding Steps also reserve reachable namespace cleanup before effects. Native witnesses cover all three Cadenced recoveries, future-cycle liveness, late refusal and namespace cleanup/refusal in both Weight dimensions. A real retry failure retires the paid boundary and increments its actual retry. Lost funds repark at the original expiry or select custody-neutral expiry Close. Model bounds remain physically and economically unqualified.

Prepared AtTime recovery reuses the canonical temporal occurrence owner after provisional retirement of its original financial episode. Classification accepts the authenticated owed point because financial entry removed the Trigger member; it installs no duplicate deadline or pointer. The Service quantum admits the occurrence owner plus complete cleanup before mutation, pays only the original Trigger, marks the one-shot source consumed and publishes Pending B+1. No Opening, Step or Action occurs. Collector refusal restores the exact wait, custody and occurrence; the Service error retains the collector category. Native witnesses include Immutable and zero-Step Contracts and Base/Drain control-only settlement. Model owners still require physical and economic qualification.

Prepared Opening recovery pays its original machine fee and processes Step 0 in the same transaction. A false predicate skips normally; a payable Action executes normally. A matching predicate with unavailable Action liability instead commits a pristine Running cursor 0 with no Step outcome, observation or retry increment and enters a distinct Action wait. The Opening recovery receipt precedes the new entry receipt; later Action recovery uses paid Run authority without another Pipeline charge or Cycle. Pristine coherence rejects fabricated progress, outcomes, retries and committed blocks. Late control/collector refusal restores the original latch, wait, native effects and fees. Zero-Step recovery completes its control cycle without an Action fee. Native Base/Drain models settle the committed first-Action wait with zero effects; production resource/economic qualification remains open.

For prepared User AwaitFunding, `derive_pipeline_machine_envelope` excludes the first additional Opening inspection already backed by useful readiness. Pipeline backs later Step/retry financial inspections, including StopCycle, and each Action's distinct wait: another ordinary control visit, entry, due inspection and financial Service recovery. Initial inspection cannot spend recovery backing. Zero-Step has no Action-wait premium; Close/System prices stay unchanged. Missing required prices refuse. Native one-Action/StopCycle models yield 500/100 units against unchanged Close 100. A first-Step three-attempt witness retains both retry inspections. A two-Action ledger collects readiness 600 and Pipeline 1000 once, then only original Action liability after recovery. These are declared-price models, not physical bounds or production tariffs; arbitrary hints and noncommitting visits remain unqualified.

Prepared User AwaitFunding Trigger pricing adds initial Opening Service inspection and a separate fixed Opening-wait reserve to useful readiness. It uses admitted Step-0 control plus policy inspection, or the zero-Step machine fee, then entry, due inspection and recovery Service components. Manual, AddressEvent, temporal capacity, AtTime debt and quotes use this tariff before payment. Close/System return original prices without another head lookup. Zero-Step still needs Opening backing. Native ledger witnesses collect the composed Trigger fee once, nothing on wait entry/duplicate readiness, then only original Pipeline liability on recovery into a pristine Action wait. AtTime installation keeps its distinct one-occurrence control and fixed wait backing, not an initial Service visit. Native model Trigger/Pipeline tariff changes establish no production coefficient; repeated producer returns, tariff sufficiency, installation composition and physical qualification remain open. Public waiting stays unavailable.

Financial Service selects its additional owner before companion authentication; shallow ring discovery performs carrier checks only. The admitted process snapshot is reused by canonical loading, avoiding another ordinary process read. No-policy Close service takes no financial companion lookup/reservation. The additional optional owner and receipt add CPU/event work that still requires physical qualification; native model prices are not coefficients.

`FinancialWaitBackingProvider` supplies installation backing for one unpaid User AwaitFunding AtTime occurrence. `secure_at_time_installation_backing` composes one extra occurrence-control visit and the fixed entry/due/Service fees, then passes the owner, prospective generation, semantic Contract and minimum fee to the host. Active creation/activation and qualifying replacement place the hook in their enclosing transaction; exact no-ops, consumed unchanged AtTime sources, technical geometry re-certification, Close, other Triggers and System paths do not invoke it. The unit provider refuses. A prepared native provider pays from owner to Fee Sink and records only a mock scope key; tests prove passive entry, original-Trigger recovery and whole-root payment rollback. Public installation remains refused by certificate admission. Reference transaction composition, public source conformance, producer/refusal bounds and physical tariffs remain unqualified.

The common `insert_active_actor_with_admission` transition checks supplied certificate identity, Contract/body/wake commitments and current host authority before backing or state mutation, and rejects System AwaitFunding independently. `ensure_current_contract_admission` binds the supplied hash, semantic/body/wake commitments and current host authority, then requires full policy Close coverage in both Weight dimensions. Initial installation and policy-bearing `store_actor_contract_with_admission` call it before publication, so replacement/re-certification cannot evade installation checks. The ordinary `insert_active_actor` wrapper keeps its fail-closed certificate builder. Geometry storage reuses the accepted certificate instead of rebuilding it after semantic publication.

A prepared native Dormant-to-Active witness installs the real head/hold/Tick residence, then enters passive AtTime waiting at its owed point. Missing backing/price, malformed scope, wrong wake qualification, stale authority and late refusal preserve the whole root and payer ledger. Public activation still rejects AwaitFunding with all model ports available. The native transition does not qualify public admission or physical coefficients; changed validation CPU and certificate-reuse work need refreshed installation evidence.

`behavior_runtime_installation_requires_complete_lifecycle_authority_before_publication` exercises supplied-certificate Dormant activation for both classes. Missing policy Close authority and a one-unit lifecycle shortfall in either dimension preserve the whole root before caller rollback. Exact-bound installation publishes policy/monitor/holds, then canonical Trigger and two Transfer rounds reach one Action-plus-Cycle Close with exact fee/custody deltas. A late caller refusal restores Dormant state. Public activation and certificate construction still refuse policy even with these model ports; no reference coefficient or public admission is supplied.

The common ingress preflight/consequence now selects waiting through `ActorProcesses` before cold wait/Contract authentication. Its separate optional `financial_wait_ingress_weight` and `financial_wait_ingress_weight_upper` require complete producer inspection/return and reachable lifecycle capacity; availability alone proves no concrete producer admission, backing or numerical binding. The new common selector adds bounded reads to ordinary ingress too and requires qualification in its existing producer owner. Final destruction retains Creation backing. Native recorded-key witnesses prove that missing optional qualification precedes cold reads; positive controls authenticate them.

Authorized fee-native hints use current spendability, not credited amount. Before Deadline → B+1 Service, `secure_funded_ingress_return` must secure one future financial inspection/repark through producer backing; `()` refuses. `financial_wait_return_fee` combines common Service inspection, optional policy inspection and the additional financial owner, excluding Step effects and Creation-backed destruction. A post-hook check preserves fee-native custody. Due return uses its fixed allowance. Negative or already-Service hints invoke no backing hook. Original debt, expiry, Run and counters survive until the owed boundary commits. Native signed-producer ledger tests pay each fresh return, charge no duplicate Service hint, and roll back payment/credit/placement on late refusal. A mock debit from waiting custody is rejected. Complete producer, noncommitting/refusal, physical and economic qualification remain open.

Canonical financial Service now loads ordinary semantic/current-Step geometry once, checks current certificate/header authority and admits a complete reachable Control/effect envelope before financial or policy companion reads. The upper composes existing inspection/entry owners, current-Step resources, ring suffix and Creation-backed cleanup. Optional-policy cleanup uses the current lifecycle certificate; policy-bearing zero-Step recovery additionally requires `maximum_behavior_zero_step_service_weight` over the complete configured policy domain. AtTime recovery, unlatched Idle and zero-Step/StopCycle control use no economic effect allowance.

A cloned frozen resource ledger checks paired capacity; actual branches keep their existing reservations and settle once, including caller-owned Control. Missing qualification, arithmetic overflow or capacity refusal preserves the root/head/progress and retains only performed common inspection. This changes neither episode/debt/expiry nor its fee fields; fresh-turn fit and physical prices remain unqualified.

For every canonical Service Step, `current_step_service_resources` checks and folds the stored StopCycle Task allowance into Control before admission; its effect requirement becomes zero. Stored geometry, publication authority and fee envelopes remain unchanged. The inner Task port still authenticates actual work against its original maximum; Service assigns that performed StopCycle work to Control for one settlement. Failed StopCycle work retains the admitted Control upper without booking an economic effect. The public native User/System witness covers exhausted Base/Drain effect pools, exact model work conservation, unchanged Pipeline pricing, no Action fee and root-preserving overflow refusal in either dimension. This changes no-policy StopCycle resource ownership; fresh production measurement and guaranteed-turn qualification remain open.

`financial_wait_runtime_retained_capacity_refusals_preserve_return_backing` revisits one paid AtTime return before, at and after its original expiry. Every insufficient-capacity visit now refuses before additional financial inspection, with exact root, Service node, episode and payment/custody ledgers preserved. Recorded-key witnesses cover all four unpaid sources, independent Control/effect dimensions in Base/Drain, and missing zero-Step policy qualification; positive controls cover effect-exhausted AtTime/zero-Step recovery and caller-owned Control without duplicate booking. The earlier charged-inspection counterexample remains archived evidence, not current behavior. Common discovery/classification is protocol service overhead under specification §8.8; its conservative physical and aggregate block qualification remains open.

`financial_wait_runtime_admitted_collector_refusals_need_separate_backing_qualification` instead fully admits recovery, then refuses original Trigger collection. Repeated visits, including solvent service after grace, retain the exact consensus root and one producer backing receipt while charging financial inspection and incurred work. The mock collection spy records each failed invocation without a committed debit. Early capacity admission does not establish physical or aggregate operating-cost bounds for collector/invariant refusals. Specification §8.8 assigns these uncommitted autonomous encounters to protocol overhead, not another Actor payment. Complete refusal/production qualification and public waiting remain open; no recurring rent, retry counter, queue or authored episode payment is introduced.

Owner controls distinguish retained wait integrity from current execution authority. A stale carrier validates its stored scope, original liability/identity, clock, body, Run and required monitor without repricing debt; an unchanged Contract remains an exact no-op. Entry, funded ingress, due inspection and Service recovery retain current-authority checks. Replacement uses authenticated control state, retires the old financial companion/expiry and publishes a fresh generation. AwaitFunding cancellation retains Run and clean flags until Service/Deadline removal has authenticated the old residence, then clears them before Idle publication. Ordinary non-wait cancellation retains its loaded-Run reconciliation. No public wait/policy admission opens. Single/batch Sweep retain owed AtTime and WindowExpired precedence. Fresh-authority checks, stale full-body authentication and replacement/cancellation CPU require complete physical qualification.

`financial_wait_runtime_owner_replacement_retires_wait_and_noop_preserves_it` covers all four unpaid boundaries and a joint clean-policy Running source, under current/newer host authority and Deadline/tentative Service residence. Real funded hints supply tentative returns before any owed work commits. Stale hints, due inspection and Service recovery refuse without mutation; exact no-op preserves the whole root. Late replacement refusal restores Run, residence, monitor, original wait, fees and custody; successful public removal rotates generation and rejects old expiry authority without recovery credit. The companion/process corruption witness also rejects missing or forged records under newer authority. These prepared native models do not qualify public creation, concrete producer prices, incompatible clock/schema upgrades or complete resource/economic bounds.

Technical financial-wait re-certification now has one internal transactional owner. It retains generation, residence, original debt/expiry/identity and behavior episode while rebinding certificate, head/tails, Run and policy plan. Acceptance requires equal authored Contract, body schema, lifecycle maximum and stored/current Step/Pipeline resources, with current certificate authority and unchanged exact unpaid liability. A clock or resource incompatibility refuses instead of renewing waiting. It pays no new Trigger, Pipeline, AtTime installation or episode fee. Ordinary geometry replacement still refuses this case; its storage wrapper selects the preservation owner. This prepared transition opens no public admission or upgrade dispatch and has no production resource binding. Close/no-policy adds no companion read.

`financial_wait_runtime_recertification_preserves_owed_boundary_and_paid_progress` covers four unpaid sources plus joint monitored Running, each on Deadline and tentative Service. Forced rollback restores the whole root; commit preserves custody, holds, topology, counters and clean flags with no fee/event. Funded service commits the original receipt and one remaining native Transfer; unfunded due service closes at the original expiry without custody movement. Stale certificates, unavailable resources and incompatible stored Step geometry refuse; late hold refusal restores the complete root. Component rebind tests preserve original identity through repeated renewals and reject actor/generation/body/episode replacement and forged origin. These are native owner witnesses, not public/reference-adapter, upgrade, economic or complete physical qualification.

`financial_wait_runtime_current_incompatible_authority_cannot_recertify_wait` supplies identity-valid certificates from a current mock host with changed body schema or lifecycle maximum (one unit higher/lower in each Weight dimension). Deadline and tentative Service both reject with whole-root, original wait and fee/backing-trace preservation. The ordinary compatible path remains separate. Component rebind rejects shorter/longer tick durations and a temporal-delay ceiling below the original phased grace; AtTime also rejects an owed point made future by the changed clock. These fail-closed guard witnesses do not implement incompatible upgrades, migrate clocks or qualify concrete runtime resources. No production schema, codec, fee or provider changed.

Optional entry, due, Service, ingress and cleanup prices default to unavailable; their presence alone does not establish complete economic backing. Prepared User policy/wait service requires both financial and policy qualification; System AwaitFunding remains rejected. Joint native witnesses preserve actual success counters and clean-Cycle flags through waiting, hints and late terminal-fee rollback, then close on the authored condition. Empty clean flags are valid only for an authenticated pristine paid Running cursor 0 under AwaitFunding; the first committed Step initializes them, while missing flags after progress refuse. Repeated first/later-Action waits preserve one behavior episode and paid Pipeline. Wait entry emits no Attempt or monitor observation. Qualification adds optional-source loading work without changing shared encoded layouts. Public admission, complete source/lifecycle conformance, producer, physical/economic and metadata/Wasm gates remain open.

The prepared joint failure/wait trace compares all eight outcome-stream metrics and both Cycle metrics with the independent exact-history oracle. A real temporary Swap failure rolls back its native input while collecting the actual Action fee; its retry count and unclean flags survive fee insolvency and a funded hint unchanged. Successful recovery pays only the original Action liability and completes an unclean Cycle. A later false predicate resets the opposite precondition streak, preserves Action streaks and completes another unclean Cycle; the first genuinely clean Cycle then closes atomically. Ordinary native Service, monitor, Deadline and fee owners execute this trace. It qualifies neither public creation/reference adapters nor physical coefficients, and adds no encoded state or production binding.

Prepared waiting cleanup uses stored admission/full body/current host authority rather than fabricating a new admission certificate. It requires the additional financial cleanup price, removes the companion through ordinary Contract destruction, and releases the exact Tick or tentative Service owner without custody effects. Generated public-call/selector/cleanup coefficients, complete finite work backing and final metadata/Wasm remain unqualified; native preparation cannot establish those gates.

Current Step execution does distinguish `FundingUnavailableReason::{ServiceFee, TaskInput}` in the latest outcome and funding-skip receipt. After a matching precondition, User Action-liability validation precedes input resolution, including a Percent amount that would otherwise collapse to a zero-input skip. System Actors bypass this Actors-fee check. Continuation retains the unit `SuspensionReason::FundingUnavailable`; the Step outcome alone owns source provenance. Admitted Close retains its existing continuation/global-failure rules; prepared AwaitFunding consumes fee-shortfall provenance at the financial owner described above. Metadata, generated consumers, applicable Wasm and resource qualification must be rebound with the final Gate B ABI.

Funding provenance adds one encoded byte to the funding outcome and skip receipt, but remains below the existing `Failed(TaskFailure)` maximum outcome width; the unit suspension tag remains one encoded byte. The schema guard protects both owners, so this split does not increase maximum Run capacity/hold through a duplicated source field. It does add a fee-native spendability read to each matching nonzero-liability User Task before input resolution. That read is current-Step control work requiring physical qualification, including the protected no-policy workload; source bounds or native conformance do not certify its RefTime/ProofSize cost.

The shared Running-progress benchmark preparation funds the actual admitted User Step's maximum Action liability before measuring the zero-input resolution branch. It leaves that Task's input asset empty, preserving the intended `ResolutionSkipped` witness instead of measuring `ServiceFee` insolvency. This preparation supplies no production Weight coefficient.

The common current-Step execution entry returns transient `BehaviorObservation` stream facts in its plan. Opening, Running and Suspended record the complete present precondition; absence and evaluation error record none. Action success/failure comes only from actual invocation and its current outcome, excluding `StopCycle` and pre-invocation failure. Caller-supplied observations are cleared. Contract geometry owns authenticated episode/monitor records. The Service wrapper loads them before effects, derives a candidate, revalidates its source and commits it in the current-Step transaction; no new receipt/history owner is added. Native tests cover real predecessor/successor Runs, predicate results, logical Actions and exact-root rollback. Transient fields and CPU work affect the no-policy path but add no encoded storage/hold width; physical cost still requires qualification.

`NormalCycleCompletion::{PlanEnd, EarlyReturn}` now accompanies the actual completed current-Step candidate and its internal attempt evidence. Opening/Running/Suspended distinguish reaching the last Step from a successful earlier `StopCycle`; false return predicates advance normally. Zero-Step service supplies PlanEnd before an independent nonce terminal, while failures, suspension and nonterminal progress supply none. Caller-supplied completion is cleared, and exact-root rollback discards the candidate. This is a transaction-local boundary fact, not a clean-Cycle observation or monitor commit. Existing cumulative skip totals conflate economic and control Steps, so they cannot substitute for policy-required clean-qualification flags. Public policy admission and complete resource-qualified completion reduction remain open.

The same entry supplies `CleanCycleStep::{Neutral, EconomicSuccess, Disqualified}` to its plan and internal attempt evidence. Invoked economic success qualifies; any failed result or skipped economic Step disqualifies. Funding unavailability without progress while Suspended is neutral; advancing past an unfunded economic Step disqualifies. False control predicates are neutral, but control predicate errors disqualify. Caller facts are cleared. `CleanCycleCandidate` folds these facts without repairing disqualification and requires PlanEnd plus at least one economic success; early return and zero/control-only Cycles are unclean. Its two-byte flags have a Cycle-nonce-scoped optional carrier in the installed monitor; the Service wrapper carries flags across committed Steps, qualifies completion and clears them on completion/cancellation. Native transactions cover clean completion and economic-skip rejection. Step/rollback tests and candidate traces cover this owner; no-policy CPU qualification remains open.

`pallet-deos-actors` provides a deterministic scheduler, bounded execution model, typed trigger system, lifecycle state machine, and adapter-driven task runtime for User and System actors.

The crate assigns no economic roles, assets, recipients, routes, actor IDs, or chain policy. Host behavior enters through typed adapters, origins, account derivation, weight/fee conversion, explicit bounds, and genesis actor specifications. External obligations live in the [package-owned embedding guide](./embedding.md).


## Architecture Overview

### Design Principles

1. `Deterministic scheduling`: one persistent generation-bound Service ring plus exact paged Deadline carriers, deterministic encounter order, and explicit per-block caps
2. `Execution safety`: exact current-Step control/effect Weight admission, complete Pipeline charging at Opening, and Action-only fee settlement around invocation
3. `Lifecycle correctness`: pause/close transitions are deterministic and reasoned (`CycleAdmissionInsufficient`, `WindowExpired`, etc.)
4. `Adapter isolation`: pallet never embeds DEX pricing logic or asset implementation specifics
5. `State decomposition`: semantic, certified Contract, process, Service/Deadline/Parked residence, and optional run owners remain independently bounded.

Canonical writes target one generation-bound `ActorSemanticStates` record, `ActorProcesses` record, exact process residence, and certified Contract geometry. Funding authorization remains in Contract policy. Ordinary `Percent` amounts read current preservable sovereign balance at each Attempt; Transfer-only cycle-balance forms instead use the bounded Run snapshot described under Amount Resolution. `ActorStateHolds` records a refundable User geometry quote for installed-lifetime capacity; Dormant Actors release that component, while System Actors remain host-capacity-backed and hold-exempt.

Detector backing uses `TriggerWakeupPointer::max_encoded_len()` while the initialized Trigger is Cadenced or an unconsumed AtTime, even when paid readiness or financial waiting has removed its member. Both full hold derivation and Hot-only Service publication use the same helper, without another storage read. Consumed AtTime releases that capacity; deactivation and Close release the remaining Active hold. Stateless Triggers retain their actual pointer geometry. The maximum financial episode and Run capacities remain reserved at installation, separately from fees and finite wait-work backing.

`prepare_zero_step_temporal` now authenticates its exact generation and invokes the existing direct due-worker fixture seam, requiring `TemporalTriggerProcessed` and a paid latch before the measured inner Opening. The two-frontier wrapper's outer success alone does not establish either worker result or selection of this Actor. Clock advancement and actual occurrence remain outside measurement; production selector admission/order are unchanged. Four simple User AtTime/Cadenced completion/close benchmark tests and the Cadenced occurrence test cover preparation natively. This isolated setup does not qualify global frontier service.

One crate-private loader reconstructs the certified Contract from those partitions, reads optional `ActorRunState`, and classifies the actor as `NotRegistered`, identity-only `Dormant`, exact `Active`, or `Corrupt`; exact Active requires coherent Identity, Hot, C6 Contract geometry, generation-bound process residence, and run state exactly while `cycle_state` is `Running` or `Suspended`. Its loaded state has no write-back path, and the public `ActiveActorState` returns the canonical partitions without flattening. No derived context owns authored equality or mutation.

### Cycle Balance Distribution

One optional `CycleBalanceSnapshot { asset, balance }` belongs to Run after its cycle nonce. The first `Transfer(CaptureCycleBalance { percent, share })` captures preservable volume at its Attempt. Later `Transfer(CycleBalancePercent(share))` Steps use that volume and check current capacity independently. Retry preserves the snapshot; a skipped capture leaves reuse skipped. Run destruction clears it without another storage key or cleanup walk. Each recipient receives one independently admitted Step with committed-prefix preservation; User Steps pay their Action fees. New ingress does not alter shares; rounding remainder stays in custody.

### Host Composition Boundary

Actors executes declarative plans against host-provided adapters. Ledger, market, liquidity, staking, fee, ingress, governance, and genesis policy remain outside the crate. The package never identifies a concrete pallet, asset, actor role, route, or recipient as canonical.

### Type Ownership

`src/types.rs` is the canonical package facade: it declares private type-owner modules and publicly re-exports their families through the `pallet_deos_actors::types::*` namespace and crate root. `src/lib.rs` exposes the facade; no compatibility alias or second semantic owner exists.

| Module | Owned type families |
| --- | --- |
| `src/types/behavior.rs` | Outcome metric catalog, canonical expressions, bound requirement compiler and pure sparse/capped episode reducer |
| `src/types/contract.rs` | Actor Contract, triggers, Steps, tasks, predicates, funding policy, and certified address ingress |
| `src/types/lifecycle.rs` | Identity/class, lifecycle, Continuation, outcomes, classification, simulation, and active read views |
| `src/types/plan.rs` | Transient Step execution carrier (`StepExecutionPlan`) |
| `src/types/resource.rs` | Simulation, admission, block budget/reservation/accounting, fee and host-resource authority records |
| `src/types/scheduler.rs` | Service ring, process and Trigger Deadline topology, compatibility carriers, drain statistics, and starvation phase |
`CloseWhen` is now the optional authored Contract field and appears in native runtime metadata and semantic hashing. Present policies require canonical validation and available complete Control, inspection and compiled policy-Close declarations; missing qualification fails closed.

Raw Contract geometry now stores `ActorBehaviorPolicies` separately from the fixed head, verifies its expression/plan against semantic and admission identity, and deletes it with the Contract. Validation rejects noncanonical or redundant clauses; its borrowed witness evaluates every atom without short-circuiting. `behavior_policy` tests compare DNF interpretation with the independent oracle and attain the 85-byte expression / 86-byte option ceiling for four-clause/four-atom bounds. These are codec bounds, not production-domain acceptance or measured trie-proof/Weight. Positive policy fixtures declare Action success plus normal Cycle completion explicitly. Finite native success does not qualify every host geometry or physical resource bound.

For host limits `C` clauses and `A` atoms per clause, validation first rejects zero/overflowed limits and values exceeding the current limits, including previously constructed values after a limit shrink. Ordering and threshold checks traverse bounded values; absorption checks at most `C(C−1)A²` atom pairs through a helper whose inputs are also `BoundedVec`. The immutable evaluation witness visits at most `CA` atoms. These are algorithmic bounds, not measured RefTime, DB or trie-proof coefficients, and none of the helpers enumerates consensus storage.

`ValidatedCloseWhen::compile` captures the validated limit snapshot and derives a sorted `BoundedVec<BehaviorAtom, ConstU32<10>>` of per-metric maximum thresholds. Each authored atom searches at most ten entries; final sorting handles at most ten entries. `CurrentOutcome` is selected only for singleton threshold-1 clauses, otherwise `Accumulated`; `requires_clean_cycle` remains independent of that choice. This is the policy-expression part of the authenticated monitor plan, not Contract-Step geometry or the state-transition reducer.

`BehaviorPolicyBinding` supplies semantic/admission IDs to the same fixed Blake2 owner used by package authority digests. The compiler binds these IDs, canonical expression and captured limits; `BehaviorPolicyPlan::verify` revalidates current source limits and recompiles the entire plan. Decodable forged caps, mode or identity are rejected. Re-certification may change the bound plan but does not itself reset the behavior episode. `prepare_contract_behavior_state` supplies the source-bound IDs and invokes compilation during installation; the standalone compiler performs no pallet storage lookup.

`behavior_plan` tests attain the 84-byte ten-metric plan ceiling and the 181-byte digest input for the four-by-four expression fixture, including fixed-width maximum thresholds. The dictionary is sparse for common policies; capped projections preserve DNF truth against the independent exact-history oracle. These are standalone representation/binding witnesses, not installed-state/hold/ProofSize bounds. No Actor Contract/head/hash change, runtime metadata change or generated Weight binding is installed by this library layer.

`BehaviorPolicyPlan::bind` returns an immutable source/plan witness only after revalidation and full recompilation agree. `BoundBehaviorPolicy` initializes, projects, reduces and technically rebinds `BehaviorMonitorState`: two 32-byte plan/episode references plus a `BoundedVec<u32, ConstU32<10>>` in plan metric order. Accumulated mode allocates only referenced counters; CurrentOutcome mode allocates none. The caller supplies the authenticated episode authority; no pallet lifecycle or storage lookup is implemented here.

`reduce` checks plan/episode/cardinality/cap coherence before indexed access, rejects contradictory current-Step facts, and returns a cloned candidate plus satisfaction. It updates at most ten slots, then evaluates every authored atom with at most ten-entry structural lookup per atom. Capped increments saturate at the selected threshold; opposite streaks reset, while absent observations preserve both streaks. The input completion flag is an already qualified normal/clean summary; actual Run flag collection, Step geometry and transition admission are not implemented by this reducer.

`rebind_episode` preserves counters only for equal semantic identity, canonical expression, cap dictionary and mode; a changed certificate may rebind the plan reference without creating a new episode. `behavior_state` tests compare 8,640 capped projections with independent exact-history streams, distinguish normal/clean completion maturation, discard candidate state on rollback, reject scope/shape/cap forgery and distinguish re-certification from replacement. They establish component behavior, not real fee/effect/lifecycle transaction conformance.

The monitor state codec remains 65 bytes without counters, 69 with one and 105 with all ten; its slots are exactly the referenced capped metrics. The installed mutable companion adds an optional Cycle carrier: a nonce plus two flags encode to ten bytes, giving a 116-byte record ceiling. CurrentOutcome without clean qualification allocates no mutable record. Clean qualification reserves these ten future bytes while Idle. Actual cold/counter bytes and required clean capacity enter body backing; common head and reserved Run holds are unchanged. Native required-only lookup/hold tests cover User shapes, but complete physical trie-proof/Weight and terminal/resource qualification remain separate from finite host admission. Financial waiting additionally requires its unavailable-by-default resource/backing ports.

EXP-001 O15 selects optional Actor-keyed authored-policy and mutable monitor/current-Cycle companions behind a fixed head presence bit. The cold expression/plan owner is now installed; the mutable monitor is installed only where accumulated tracking or clean qualification requires it. `behavior_storage_geometry_*` compares the same current mock head: both absent-policy payloads encode to 149 bytes, but inline policy raises FRAME head `max_size` by 85 bytes. Inline optional monitor raises shared Run capacity from 204 to 310 bytes under the existing maximum-backed hold. The companion selector records one head key while absent and three head/policy/monitor keys while present. The four-by-four expression is diagnostic, not a host limit; recorded key counts are not trie ProofSize or RefTime. Preserve canonical reconstruction/binding and lifecycle transactions during implementation; final payload/scope/clean-flag codecs, holds, cost owners and public ABI remain Gate B work.

`BehaviorControlWeightContext` carries selected cold/monitor encoded bytes, clause/atom/metric counts, counter slots and clean qualification into `StepControlWeightProvider`. Policy-bearing Step maximum/actual methods return the complete control price, never a second surcharge, and default to unavailable. Contract resource derivation uses provisional fixed-width binding values only for geometry; execution derives the same shape from its authenticated cold source and verifies host maximum against stored resources before Opening or Action. Absent policy uses the original port with unchanged context codec and no policy geometry lookup; new selector CPU work still needs qualification. Native tests compare shape bytes with actual holds and verify missing authority refusal in Idle/Running. The native mock is a model; its declaration availability does not establish complete lifecycle/call coverage or production qualification.

Zero-Step policy control uses separate fail-closed maximum/actual host methods over the same geometry. `zero_step_resource_envelope` supplies one control-only owner to Pipeline pricing, canonical Service admission and full-Contract publication/reconfiguration paths. Opening authenticates the cold source before fees, and actual settlement must fit its admitted maximum after candidate commit and the single finalizer; late refusal rolls back the fee, monitor and Close together. The returned Step commit evidence reports zero effect Weight. Native User witnesses cover the header fee derived from this envelope, missing maximum before Opening and invalid actual after Close. No policy price falls back to the ordinary zero-Step price; public policy admission and physical qualification remain open.

Public creation, activation and replacement preserve resource refusal causes through `try_zero_step_resource_envelope`, `try_derive_step_resource_envelopes` and Result-bearing `contract_service_resources_upper`. Missing policy Control/inspection returns `BehaviorPolicyUnavailable`; checked composition/conversion or ordinary bound failure retains `AdmissionBoundOverflow`. Internal Option consumers reuse `.ok()` wrappers, with no second pricing lookup. The `policy_admission_*` family checks exact no-op/no-fee refusal for User/System creation and existing-source controls, independent RefTime/ProofSize overflow, invalid syntax, absent-policy isolation and retained complete-declaration native admission. The independent embedding's original unsupported-policy assertion remains unchanged. This correction adds no host price, error variant or production capability.

`lib.rs::close_control_weight_for_contract` prices policy/monitor destruction through the required compiled `WeightInfo::close_actor_policy_bearing` owner and the component-wise maximum with ordinary Close. It does not consult `AdmissionCertificateAuthorityProvider::behavior_close_additional_weight`; that optional port is not the current Close qualification boundary. Financial waiting retains its independently checked supplement. `ensure_current_contract_admission` requires the complete Close bound to fit the certificate's lifecycle maximum on both axes. Creation economically backs destruction, but does not waive current-block admission or establish conservative physical coefficients.

Policy-bearing canonical detachment reuses stored admission only after full geometry/body equality, current host authority, exact generation/identity/hot/Run and complete Close availability checks. Ordinary detachment retains certificate rebuilding. Non-resident terminal simulation receives the authenticated full source, admits selector plus complete Close and invokes the same detachment/finalizer inside outer rollback. Native User witnesses cover body/generation/host refusals, one-unit resource boundaries, successful rollback and late hold-release failure; absent-policy simulation has its own hold-failure witness. Native preparation does not establish a concrete host's public policy or physical-pricing qualification.

`preopening_policy_prefix_does_not_bypass_close_detachment_price_gate` retains three component sources with policy staged while Idle before the real paid prefix. Qualified native lifecycle coverage admits detachment despite absent old optional ports. One-unit RefTime-only and ProofSize-only shortfalls instead produce `BehaviorPolicyUnavailable` at fresh public admission; the existing paid source refuses changed authority with `ActorInvariant`. Whole roots, process, Run, custody and holds survive refusal. Qualified detachment executes under explicit outer rollback, preserving the source rather than leaving half-closed state. These host overrides are native boundary falsifiers, not healthy production history.

Refused public creation may attempt its collector before publication, inside the enclosing transaction. `MockFeeCollector` records that call in a thread-local diagnostic, while its ledger transfer rolls back with publication refusal. The regression checks exact attempted amounts separately from unchanged creator/sink balances and storage roots; the diagnostic is not committed fee history. Detachment itself makes no collector call.

`load_service_behavior_policy_with_price` authenticates the stored policy and current host certificate before optional service reconstruction. Current-Step geometry requires the policy control maximum to fit its stored envelope, while zero-Step requires its distinct maximum; both require the independent Close maximum. An absent policy uses the original ports without companion reads. The transient reconstructed Contract preserves the authenticated expression while retaining only current-Step materialization. Prepared native canonical rounds now execute distinct successive Tasks, accumulated closure, User zero-Step and strict clean completion; Running service ignores an unreached tail chunk. Missing policy prices refuse reconstruction without widening ordinary prices. Public creation/replacement and certificate construction require available policy resources; complete lifecycle/call, financial, certificate-wide maximum and physical qualification remain open.

Policy-bearing `update_contract` retains its authenticated control source for cancellation instead of requiring the old policy to pass current execution pricing. `ContractReplaced` retires the old Run and Service/Deadline residence inside the enclosing transaction; its temporary Idle publication authenticates stored body/certificate/resources without authorizing an Attempt. New geometry rotates the generation and publishes current prices before commit. Other cancellation and execution paths retain current-authority checks. Cancellation commits the nonce, clears only current-Cycle flags and emits one cancellation/summary pair; it supplies no completion observation. Compatible re-certification preserves counters, while semantic replacement resets the episode. The shared publication committer retains its previous mutations. Absent policy adds no companion reads; the policy selector/branch and helper-call CPU, complete replacement admission and physical prices remain unqualified.

Policy Service inspection now has a separate fail-closed maximum port over configured encoded/clause/atom ceilings. Canonical Service reads the common head under its ordinary probe, then reserves this additional Actor Control before any optional companion read. Both direct and pass-owned control retain the inspection cost after subsequent capacity, scope or transaction refusal, without outstanding reservation debt. Trie-recorded native cases prove a one-unit ProofSize refusal reads neither companion. Pipeline pricing includes the inspection bound per reachable Attempt, or once for zero-Step; System settlement stays zero and final destruction remains excluded. These source/native owners do not certify physical coefficients, repeated pre-Opening refusal economics, temporal/public-call composition or the certificate-wide lifecycle maximum.

Execution logic remains in `src/execution.rs`, `src/scheduler.rs`, and `src/reactions.rs`; the type split does not move algorithms or create a mirrored model. `src/contract.rs` remains the semantic classifier and is distinct from the Actor Contract type owner at `src/types/contract.rs`.

## Execution Model

### Actor Classes

| Class | Ownership | Mint task allowed | Typical usage |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `User` | Signed owner + slot namespace | No | User automation |
| `System` | Governance origin | Yes | Protocol automation |

User recovery has an explicit slot-targeted surface: the default `create_user_actor` path allocates the lowest free slot, while `create_user_actor_at_slot` recreates fresh Mutable control for a released slot and therefore derives the same sovereign account. Close never moves custody, and the recovery Contract can use residual native or asset balances without a rescue subsystem.

Current owner-slot representation is fixed-width and runtime-shaped:

- `OwnerSlotBitmaps` stores one `[u8; 32]` bitmap per User owner; System Actors never consume it
- `MaxOwnerSlots` is nonzero and at most `255`; every bit at or above the configured bound remains zero
- Slot `s` maps to byte `s / 8` and little-endian bit `s % 8`
- Default allocation scans the 32 bytes in ascending order for the lowest valid free bit, while exact-slot admission changes one validated bit
- Closing the final User actor deletes its all-zero bitmap

### Current Actor-State Shape

The package stores each actor identity once and decomposes each active epoch into bounded semantic, certified Contract, process, residence, and optional run owners:

- `ActorSemanticStates`: canonical lifecycle semantic and Contract-generation owner; stores dormant identity plus last generation or active Identity/generation/Hot/admission independently of physical placement. Initial Contract publication uses generation one, replacement checked-increments it, dormancy preserves it, and ordinary Hot updates do not change it
- `ActorIdentities`: dormant physical identity partition only; it must equal the dormant semantic state and cannot coexist with active partitions
- `ActorProcesses`: canonical generation-bound process status and primary-residence owner
- `ServiceHeader` / `ServiceNodes`: canonical persistent service ring with one generation-bound Pending or Live node per resident Actor; the persistent cursor, reciprocal links, eligibility, and same-round consideration markers determine class-neutral encounter order
- `DeadlineHeaders` / `DeadlinePages` / `DeadlineHandles`: canonical C32 Block/Tick deadline buckets, linked pages, and exact generation-bound reverse handles for suspended processes and independent temporal Triggers; vacancy authority reuses fragmented slots and empty-page removal updates bounded key indexes transactionally
- `ActorControlCell` / `ActorControlLocators` and paged Ready/Waiting stores: retained pre-cutover compatibility and benchmark fixtures only; fresh-genesis production publication, block service, simulation, and TryRuntime reject them as scheduler authority
- `ActorContractHeads`: schedule, fixed policy presence and unpaid-service disposition header, optional canonical parked-balance activation, semantic/body/admission commitments, Step count, optional inline Step 0, and its optional control/effect envelope
- `ActorBehaviorPolicies` / `ActorBehaviorMonitors`: optional canonical expression/plan/episode anchor and required-only capped state/Cycle flags; their lookup and backing stay outside common head/Run geometry
- `ActorControlCell.admission`: compact runtime semantics/layout/Weight and lifecycle identity, independent of the configured Step/resource ceiling
- `ActorContractTailChunks`: authority-bound gap-free Steps 1..N in chunks of at most four, with one aligned control/effect envelope per Step
- `ActorRunState`: sparse bounded semantic/body/admission authority, current Step cursor, cumulative outcomes, eligibility, retry, and suspension facts retained only while a multi-block Cycle is open

`ActorCreated` carries `actor_id`, owner, `actor_class`, mutability, sovereign account, and `initial_lifecycle`; User slot or System custody locator lives inside `ActorClass`. `actor_id` identifies the semantic owner and custody locator; dormant identity retains its last control-mutation block. Public creation admits Dormant only as Mutable, while host genesis may install a sealed Immutable Dormant System identity that generic activation and owner-close control reject. Activation or schedule replacement derives block eligibility from `schedule_anchor` and window start, while canonical Active semantic state owns the optional timestamp-ceiled cadence anchor. Replacement releases the superseded Trigger deadline before planning its successor, so no replaced cadence or AtTime tick survives. The typed lifecycle forbids contradictory pause state.

Package internals reconstruct execution state from the canonical semantic owner, process residence, certified Contract geometry, current sovereign ledger state, and optional `ActorRunState`. `active_actor_state` exposes canonical semantic partitions without synchronized mirrors.

`mutate_actor_semantic_state` freshly loads the bounded record and applies complete Publish/Replace/Remove validation before deciding storage submission. It writes only when the validated replacement differs from current state; an identical Replace returns the same value without a redundant Put. Publish still rejects an existing record, Remove requires the exact present value, and stale/missing expected state fails even when requested bytes equal the caller's old snapshot or current storage. No cached authority, placement update or economic side effect is introduced. `tests/storage_and_api.rs::semantic_storage_mutation_preserves_fresh_validation_and_rollback` covers both stale-equal cases, changed writes, publication/removal and outer rollback.

This is intentionally more concrete than the paired specification: the spec (§4.5) defines each canonical fact and its owner, while this document records the current package storage realization.

- `Hot record residue`: `ActorHotState` still carries `queue_ticket` and `wakeup_pointer` from the pre-cutover carrier. Canonical Actors keep both `None`; they have no semantic authority and are removal candidates together with the compatibility stores below.
- `Pipeline fee envelope`: the stored envelope retains one machine-fee upper bound; Opening charges that amount without a final-destruction surcharge. Creation economically backs lifetime cleanup under specification §§4.4/8; physical close admission remains independent.
- `Compatibility absence reads`: canonical transition paths still test `ActorControlLocators` and `ActorUnsignaledControlCells` for absence (about seventy production call sites) before choosing the canonical branch. Fresh-genesis state never populates them, so each test is a proof of absence that costs a storage read and ProofSize without deciding anything; removing the compatibility stores removes these reads.

### Contract Steps Structure

Each actor admits `0..=MaxContractSteps` ordered Steps and stores them as C6 geometry: optional Step 0 and its optional resource envelope live in the head, while Steps 1..N and aligned envelopes occupy gap-free authority-bound chunks of at most four. A zero-Step Contract has neither inline body nor tail fragments and reconstructs from its certified header alone. One host-configured `MaxContractSteps` in `1..=255` applies identically to User and System actors across creation, activation, replacement, simulation, genesis, and benchmark construction. For a nonempty N-Step Contract, execution loads only the current Step head or one exact tail chunk; unreached tail fragments remain cold. Mutable `RetryLater` admits only `2..=MaxRetryAttempts`, with the protocol-fixed metadata constant set to 10.

- `precondition: Option<Precondition<Predicate, MaxPreconditionClauses, MaxPredicatesPerClause>>`
- `task: Task`
- `on_error: StepErrorPolicy` (`AbortCycle` / `ContinueNextStep` / Mutable-only `RetryLater { max_attempts }`)

`None` is the sole unconditional Step form. `Some(Precondition { clauses })` stores one bounded DNF: outer clauses are OR and each inner clause is AND. Admission rejects empty outer and inner vectors. Raw codec input is bounded by `MaxPreconditionClauses × MaxPredicatesPerClause`; the total `MaxPredicatesPerStep` bound is checked after deduplication and absorption, against the canonical result. Evaluation visits every admitted predicate without short-circuit and executes or skips the Step exactly once.

Each admitted predicate is current-state only. The evaluator reads it immediately before its Step, so it observes committed earlier-Step effects from the same logical multi-block cycle plus intervening authoritative state. No predicate result is captured at cycle admission or retained in `ActorRunState`.

Admission sorts predicates and clauses by canonical typed SCALE, removes repeated predicates within a clause, rejects clauses that become semantically identical, absorbs exact predicate-superset clauses, and stores only the canonical form. `update_contract` canonicalizes before equality and returns an exact no-op before rate limiting, cancellation, writes, placement reconstruction, or events when the resulting contract is unchanged.

`Precondition::evaluation_units` supplies the total predicate count to the host's `StepControlWeight` resource-envelope provider. It is a pricing hint and equals the bounded count of current predicate evaluations. `evaluate_step_precondition` visits each predicate once. Pipeline pricing consumes that Step-control input through the Fee Collection Boundary below; predicate capture adds no separate component.

A false DNF expression emits `StepSkipped(PreconditionFalse)` and advances one fixed cursor; evaluation errors remain task-independent failures routed through the authored Step policy.

`ObservationProvider<FeedId, BlockNumber>` is the generic current-scalar boundary. The host receives `feed`, `now`, and `max_age_blocks`; `Fresh` returns both `value` and `observed_at`. Actors accepts Fresh only when `observed_at <= now` and checked age stays within the authored maximum. Future or over-age Fresh maps to `PredicateError::InvalidObservation`, while explicit Unavailable, Uninitialized, and Stale states produce ordinary false results.

Plan validation rejects zero `max_age_blocks`, fixed-zero and percentage-zero amount resolutions, self-directed Transfer recipients, zero absolute input ceilings, zero liquidity minima, and identical SwapIn/SwapOut/AddLiquidity/DonateLiquidity asset pairs. RemoveLiquidity admission checks amount/minima; its host effect owns LP/pair membership. Creation predicts User custody from the first available or explicitly requested slot before fee collection; update and activation validate against the stored sovereign account.

Task set in implementation:

- `Transfer`
- `SwapIn`
- `SwapOut`
- `AddLiquidity`
- `RemoveLiquidity`
- `Burn`
- `Mint` (System only)
- `Stake`
- `DonateLiquidity`
- `Unstake`
- `StopCycle` (User/System, fieldless, adapter-free)

### Public Inventory Evidence

Prepared `ActorEligibilityApi` v7 replaces legacy placement with `ActorActivationResidence`: Service kind, Deadline key, Parked reason/review block, or Disabled. The strict canonical loader authenticates generation and carrier coherence before projection; physical page/slot addresses and independent Trigger deadlines are excluded. Lifecycle classification authenticates an owed AtTime point from the financial companion after detector removal. A separate optional `FinancialWaitProjection` preserves typed original unpaid service, entry time and absolute grace Tick; absence certifies no future solvency. Native fixtures cover both Service kinds, retry Deadline, disabled System and Park with a separate review Deadline, original financial debt/expiry and read-only roots. Metadata/descriptors remain behind the coordinated binding gate.

`public_reachability_inventory_is_closed_and_canonical` freezes the reviewed SCALE names and order for the listed Actors public families. Semantic-contract tests exhaustively interpret Task, Predicate, amount, and policy families, while focused production, simulation, embedding, metadata, and ABI tests cover the named seams below. This evidence does not claim universal call-graph reachability from every public variant to a production constructor.

`public_api_error_signatures_use_shared_typed_cores` compiler-checks that the eligibility runtime API returns `ActorClassificationError` directly and simulation wraps that core once in `SimulationError`. Its exhaustive classification-to-dispatch match fails compilation when the shared core grows without a mapping. Focused eligibility and simulation tests cover the cited public roots; metadata/spec drift checks freeze Event and pallet Error inventories without asserting universal constructor reachability.

| Family | Retained variants | Reviewed executable evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Predicate | `BalanceAbove`, `BalanceBelow`, `BalanceEquals`, `BalanceNotEquals`, `BlockNumberAbove`, `BlockNumberBelow`, `ObservationAbove`, `ObservationBelow`, `ObservationEquals`, `ObservationNotEquals` | Active contract calls; `every_predicate_is_pure_and_bounded`, predicate evaluator and observation tests |
| Task | `Transfer`, `SwapIn`, `SwapOut`, `AddLiquidity`, `RemoveLiquidity`, `Burn`, `Mint`, `Stake`, `DonateLiquidity`, `Unstake`, `StopCycle` | Active contract calls; `every_task_has_one_exhaustive_semantic_contract`, task tests, and independent runtime profiles |
| Amount and exact-output bound | `Fixed`, `Percent`, `CaptureCycleBalance`, `CycleBalancePercent`; `LiveQuote`, `Absolute` | Task constructors; amount classifier/resolution tests, removed-form decoding tests and independent exact-output evidence |
| Trigger | Exactly one of `Manual`, `AddressEvent`, `AtTime`, or `Cadenced`; source `Any`, `OwnerOnly`, `Whitelist`; asset `Any`, `Whitelist` | Actor Contract constructors plus raw typed calls; exhaustive Active replacement and Dormant lifecycle matrices; manual, certified-ingress, timestamp-cadence, and embedding tests |
| Funding | `OwnerOnly`, `SignedAllowlist`, `RuntimePolicy`, `AnyVerifiedIngress`; provenance `Signed`, `InternalProtocol`, `Xcm` | Active contract and certified producer constructors; funding-policy package tests and DEOS producer inventory |
