| Outcome and Step policy | Authored `close_when` (qualified host resources required); `AbortCycle`, `ContinueNextStep`, `RetryLater` | Contract/hash, admission/refusal and shared reducer/transaction boundaries; finite success is not full resource qualification |
| Attempt and step views | `AttemptDisposition::{Completed, Continued, Failed, Suspended, Closed}`; `StepOutcome::{Executed, Stopped, Skipped, FundingUnavailable, Failed}` | Shared production evaluator; production/simulation parity tests |
| Eligibility view | `NotRegistered`, `Dormant`; Active residence, temporal phase/terminal reason, original unpaid grace and authenticated behavior episode/capped monitor | `actor_eligibility`; `eligibility_projection_*`, `financial_wait_runtime_eligibility_*`, `behavior_eligibility_*` tests |
| Cost quote | Named Creation, family-specific Trigger, upfront Pipeline Machine, current maximum Action, and refundable state-hold components with independent Weight/admission identities | `actor_cost_quote`; `actor_cost_quote_keeps_fee_boundaries_and_state_hold_provenance_separate` |
| Simulation | `FreshCurrentPlan`, `CurrentRun`; every `SimulationError` in specification Section 7.2 | `simulate_current_contract`; package simulation and independent runtime tests |
| Adapter result | `RetryClass::{Permanent, Temporary}`; scalar observation `Unavailable`, `Uninitialized`, `Fresh`, `Stale` | Host adapters and fail-closed unit implementations; runtime Oracle mapping, retry matrix, and embedding tests |
| Event | Every variant in specification Section 8 and runtime metadata | Production `deposit_event` sites; event-order, task, lifecycle, ingress, scheduler, and generated ABI tests |
| Error | Every variant in specification Section 9.2 and runtime metadata | Production `ensure!`/error branches; rejection, rollback, exact metadata/spec, package, and embedding tests |

`CancellationReason::RuntimeUpgrade` and semantic-manifest `ContextDependency::None` were removed because neither had a production constructor. Runtime upgrades remain migration-specific work under specification Section 9.4 rather than a permanently encoded placeholder. Every amount classifier now reports its actual task-policy dependency.

`SwapOut` groups authored output before explicit `InputLimit::{LiveQuote, Absolute(Balance)}` protection. `Absolute(0)` fails before storage; `Absolute(nonzero)` composes its ceiling with live preservable input capacity, while `LiveQuote` intentionally uses that capacity without an authored long-horizon ceiling. `DexOps::swap_exact_out` always receives the resulting finite bound.

Liquidity tasks also carry fixed non-zero outputs. `AddLiquidity.min_lp_out` reaches `LiquidityOps::add_liquidity`; the host adapter must reject a measured LP output below that bound.

`RemoveLiquidity.min_amount_a` and `min_amount_b` pass directly into Asset Conversion. Its two exact withdrawal-minimum errors classify as Temporary; malformed pair identity, missing indexed topology, and unknown downstream failures remain Permanent. The outer adapter transaction retains post-call balance-delta checks as defense in depth, so no success event or partial liquidity mutation survives either enforcement layer.

`StopCycle` executes after its precondition passes. Its admission and actual work belong to Control, prepaid through the User Pipeline Machine owner; it reserves zero economic effect capacity and charges no Action fee. The canonical evaluator produces `StepOutcome::Stopped`, production emits `CycleStopped { actor_id, cycle_nonce, step_index }`, and the shared interpreter normally completes the Cycle at that cursor.

The shared completion path emits the cumulative summary, supplies one normal-completion observation and applies the common terminal precedence. Prepared policy service reduces `close_when` in the current transition's transaction; public present-policy admission depends on complete host declarations and ordinary admission. A surviving Actor clears its Run and derives Idle residence from current state, leaving the suffix unreachable. Independent nonce/lifecycle terminals remain effective without an authored outcome policy.

The instruction resolves no amount and invokes no runtime adapter. It increments `executed_steps`, leaves `committed_effectful_tasks` unchanged and supplies no Action observation. Successful stop contributes to `CycleCompletedTotal`; an unvisited suffix or a control-only Cycle cannot contribute to `CycleCleanTotal`. Authored Action thresholds and normal/clean Cycle thresholds therefore remain distinct. A false precondition advances normally; evaluation failure follows the authored Step error policy. There is no legacy productive-Cycle terminal authority.

### Amount Resolution

The pallet resolves amounts through `AmountResolution`:

- `Fixed`: an authored absolute amount, checked against current task capacity.
- `Percent`: a fraction of the current task-specific available balance at each Attempt.
- `CaptureCycleBalance { percent, share }`: Transfer-only; capture a bounded asset/volume basis on the first attempted capture Step, then resolve the requested share against it.
- `CycleBalancePercent(share)`: Transfer-only; reuse that basis without recapturing after later ingress or a skipped capture.

`execution.rs::resolve_transfer_amount` owns capture/reuse and still checks each resolved Transfer against current spendability and fee reservations. One optional `ActorRunState.balance_snapshot` survives retry and disappears with Run on completion, abort, reset or Close. The seven `cycle_balance_*` tests in `src/tests/market_tasks.rs` falsify ingress independence, first-attempt retry retention, skipped-capture behavior, admission geometry, committed-prefix preservation and custody-neutral Close.

Resolution policy is task-bound in code:

- `PreserveSpend`: applies to Transfer, Burn, exact-input swap, liquidity add/remove, Stake, and DonateLiquidity; computes one spend ceiling as adapter-visible balance minus reserved future User fees for the native fee asset and, for User fee-native direct debits, `max(MinUserBalance, asset minimum)`; other assets retain their adapter minimum.
- `DonateLiquidity` resolves only declared `asset_a` as `max_amount_a` and passes the current preservable `asset_b` balance as the cap on pre-existing B debit; the host adapter must enforce both debit caps and report total donated amounts. An adapter may atomically acquire and donate new B above that debit cap, so generic Actors verifies returned A spend while adapter tests falsify B-cap violations. `Fixed`, every percentage basis, and each resolved Transfer amount must stay within their applicable ceiling.
- `ExpendableSpend`: consume available amount where task allows
- `Mint`: amount interpreted in mint context
- `Unstake share spend`: `Fixed` and `Percent` resolve against `StakingOps::share_balance(position_asset)`; 100% of current shares permits full withdrawal.

Resolution outcomes are deterministic:

- `Resolved(value)`
- `Skipped`
- `FundingUnavailable`

`FundingUnavailable` is a deterministic current-capacity resolution outcome for both actor classes. It advances as a non-terminal skip under `AbortCycle` and `ContinueNextStep`, while valid Mutable `RetryLater { max_attempts }` suspends at the current cursor. It covers current source/share overspend and preserve-spend resolution that would cross the minimum-balance ceiling. Actors retain no funding accumulator or Opening snapshot.

`PipelineMachineEnvelope` stores the bounded User machine fee derived from current host Weight bindings in the certified Contract head, including prepared optional wait components and excluding final destruction. Scheduler admission reads that O(1) authority only when Idle paid readiness is consumable. Generated owners distinguish zero-Step, Opening, retry, continuation, completion and cleanup work; the benchmark ownership qualifications below separate priced fixtures from complete physical containment. Mutually exclusive owners combine by component-wise maximum rather than subtraction or additive overlap.

For absent policy, `zero_step_resource_envelope` selects `WeightInfo::scheduler_inner_zero_step_complete` plus window placement and accepts no Actor-class parameter. Its benchmark source is System; the separate complete User benchmark includes Pipeline collection/receipt and User hold work. The configured common port must contain every supported class, but diagnostic existence or settlement against the same declaration is not independent physical containment. `idle_window_placement_weight` adds `service_member_to_deadline_new_key` only for a window. Full-window diagnostics already execute placement, so their whole-body maximum plus that owner does not establish a non-overlapping base. Policy-bearing zero-Step retains separate maximum/actual ports.

The four persistent minimal-Manual/full-AddressEvent profiles resolve ActorRef and Service kind through `prepare_zero_step_inner_context` in setup. Their `#[block]` calls `execute_prepared_zero_step_inner`, retaining the original typed enclosing transaction around the actual worker's own attempt transaction. `execute_zero_step_on_service` still authenticates the supplied source inside measurement. The legacy `execute_zero_step_inner` signature delegates through the same ordered preparation and byte-identical transaction/worker block; other temporal/terminal callers keep their original measured boundary. This is not a transaction-exclusive body or a production optimization, and no invented transaction-price subtraction is justified.

`zero_step_whole_worker_owns_placement_and_matches_canonical_carrier` compares prepared, bare-worker and canonical paths on persistent User/System minimal Manual and full AddressEvent sources. Matching diagnostic rollback depth preserves equal committed roots, fees, custody and placement. User collector depths are ambient+3, ambient+2 and ambient+3 respectively; System is collector-exempt. Each worker has already consumed Service: another advance refuses, as does another window transfer, without mutation. Omitting only the prepared carrier fails this witness; equal outcomes do not license its removal. The prepared round is already begun, and declared meter equality supplies no physical bound or broader caller/refusal coverage.

For these persistent successes, the canonical caller books inner plus `service_turn_control_weight_upper` but performs no post-worker ring mutation. That common declaration includes begin/probe/state inspection plus a maximum admit/retire suffix. The inner base measures a complete Disabled branch, while window Weight measures a complete transfer including Service removal. These are overlapping branch declarations, not source-disjoint phases. A future price needs one complete branch/common allocation or qualified actual subphases with one carrier/placement owner; neither coefficient/sample subtraction nor removing an allowance establishes containment. Current production bindings remain unchanged.

Four raw-only `scheduler_service_zero_step_{user,system}_{complete,address_event}` extras instead measure the complete canonical caller. `prepare_canonical_zero_step_service` uses the existing prefunded minimal Manual/full AddressEvent sources; categorical `r=0/1` leaves the round unbegun or begins it through the real setup transition. Meter/resource state enters Drain outside recording. `execute_canonical_zero_step_service` adds no transaction: it delegates to `service_canonical_round_head_with_resources`, so selector/classification, actual worker, canonical carrier, placement and resource reconciliation stay inside. No terminal destruction is reached; snapshots and verification remain outside.

`canonical_zero_step_service_uses_only_actual_caller_transaction` checks eight class/header/round contexts and the real User collector's two-layer caller/worker depth, with no System collection. `canonical_zero_step_sdk_profiles_execute_with_and_without_verification` executes all four SDK profiles at both `r` values with verification enabled/disabled, then independently checks completion, holders, custody and passive residence. The script excludes these categorical extras from generation/merge and admits exact extra/raw-only collection only. Their existence supplies no consumed Weight binding or physical class maximum; complete economic tariff and physical authority retain separate contracts.

The extra `scheduler_user_pipeline_capacity(c)` is a raw-only owner for the complete cached-Head `pipeline_capacity_sufficient` call, not a complete I/K bound. `c/2` selects minimal zero-Step Manual or full-header/max-Step no-policy AddressEvent; parity selects exact Pipeline capacity or a one-unit shortfall after paid readiness. Setup reuses the public creation Contract factory without its unrelated owner-slot prefill, projects cached prices through existing admission/geometry producers, funds before creation, then performs real Manual or signed AddressEvent ingress. No post-readiness topup, fabricated source or extra carrier is used. The body includes Head loading/decoding, cached fee/floor arithmetic and fee-native spendability; it performs no collection, current-authority lookup, Task or lifecycle transition.

`pipeline_capacity_inspection_owner_covers_public_funding_and_head_boundaries` runs all four real SDK bodies with verification on/off, checking one sovereign `AssetOps::balance` call, no live authority lookup and unchanged whole root in each body window. The large public source retains maximum header width without parked activation, maximum admitted Task encoding and configured predicate capacity; nonempty tails are not read by this helper. Returning only the expected boolean without executing the helper fails the occurrence assertion. Script self-tests exclude this categorical owner from default generation/regression/merge. Native functional coverage is not reference-Wasm applicability, maximum ledger/trie proof or a conservative resource coefficient.

The extra `scheduler_service_user_inspection_refusal(c)` records the canonical local-meter inspection/refusal for a full no-parked User AddressEvent Contract with a loaded first Step; it does not cover the later paired-resource reservation refusal. It reuses `prepare_user_pipeline_capacity(2/3)`, the common `CanonicalServiceFixture`/refusal-meter preparation and actual resource-aware canonical caller. `c/4` selects exact/one-short paid Pipeline capacity, `(c/2)%2` unbegun/prepared round and parity the local RefTime/ProofSize axis. Public creation/funding/readiness precedes recording; no Head/Run/certificate injection or post-readiness funding occurs. The old inspection sum selects a refusal boundary, not a sound price for this complete body.

`canonical_user_inspection_refusal_covers_paid_head_round_and_axis` uses actual SDK bodies in both verification modes, existing mock balance/current Recording and a test-only collector-attempt wrapper. Ordinary bodies observe one native spendability call and no current-provider call; root/source neutrality and no collection are independently checked even with SDK verification disabled. An empty canonical body fails the first occurrence assertion. The DEOS integration owner retains the separately restored Idle-current diagnostic, artifact applicability and physical qualification gates; no new production guard, tariff or Weight follows. This categorical extra is excluded from default generation, regression and merge.

`canonical_inspection_distinguishes_local_and_shared_refusal_work` pairs fully funded native sources over both round states and meter/resource axes. Local InsufficientWeight reaches zero Action-fee quotes; later shared-budget ResourceUnavailable reaches one. A test-only scoped fee-converter trace records calls without changing its constant return or consensus state. Both keep the whole root/custody/paid latch unchanged, collect no fee and settle only the inspection declaration with no effect booking or outstanding reservation. Equal declaration/root results therefore do not establish equal incurred work.

For valid Idle/no-Run/cursor0 and AbortCycle, the later path quotes the Action fee, performs a second stored-integrity hash and one current Number read, clones identity/hot/None-Run plus two plain certificates, and moves the loaded Step into the canonical plan. Its PhantomData does not clone resources; no extra Step/Contract clone or current-provider check occurs. Four suffix WeightInfo getter invocations precede paired reservation. Failed effect reservation restores its provisional Control booking before the common after-inspection restore, four-slot settlement and storage rollback. These source owners cannot be discarded merely because the final root/declaration agrees; host fee conversion and emitted copies/heap/executor costs require independent qualification.

This native mock comparison constructs ledger pressure only in its fixture, not in a public block producer, and supplies no physical price, exact runtime geometry or numerical underfit. The existing raw local-meter owner remains narrower; the separate shared-refusal owner below closes that native body boundary, not conservative complete I/K qualification.

The separate raw-only `scheduler_service_user_shared_refusal(c)` covers the later paired-reservation failure. Setup reuses the fully funded full-Step User source, constructs one valid frozen-budget User-dispatch booking and enters Drain before recording; no consensus ledger or policy override is injected. `c=0..3` selects unbegun/prepared round and shared RefTime/ProofSize exhaustion; the local meter remains unlimited. Its body delegates to the existing canonical caller, including quote/plan, failed reservation, inspection settlement and rollback. Verification preserves user usage/custody/holds/latch/root with no effect booking or outstanding reservation.

`canonical_shared_refusal_sdk_owner_covers_round_axis_and_quote` executes all four SDK categories with verification off/on. The body window observes one native spendability call and exactly one fee quote, no collection/current-provider call and unchanged whole root. Quote indices exclude setup/verification. SDK metadata marks the selector extra-only; project routing self-tests exclude it from generation/regression/merge and require exact extra/raw-only selection. Native body coverage supplies neither physical host equivalence nor an I/K upper, slope or production price; no benchmark artifact is implicitly rebound.

The raw-only extra `scheduler_service_zero_step_admission_refusal(c)` reuses those four sources with sixteen categorical values: `c/4` selects System Manual, User Manual, User AddressEvent or System AddressEvent; `(c/2)%2` selects the ordinary round state, and parity selects RefTime/ProofSize. Setup limits only that meter axis to the declared begin/probe/state-inspection sum `I`, leaving the other axis and resource budget unchanged. `execute_canonical_zero_step_refusal` delegates to the same canonical caller with no added carrier. Recording ends at actual complete admission refusal, before Opening; setup snapshots and verification remain outside. This is a meter-shortage boundary, not a physical price or resource-ledger-axis shortage.

`zero_step_canonical_refusal_preserves_paid_source_and_retries_without_retrigger` checks all sixteen categories: `InsufficientWeight`, incurred meter/Control equality, zero effect/reservations, no optional halt, exact root/hold/custody and retained Idle paid readiness. An ordinary next-block retry completes without another Trigger and collects only the applicable Pipeline fee. `zero_step_canonical_refusal_sdk_verification_on_and_off` runs all sixteen SDK values with both verification modes and independently checks source state. Refusal must follow actual canonical inspection, not a synthetic error. Retained production authority remains unchanged; the reference integration owns reversible fresh-current qualification and its separate physical/economic gates.

`zero_step_prepared_inner_context_preserves_source_and_legacy_completion` checks readonly setup and matching whole-state roots/collector attempts for both classes and header shapes at matched diagnostic rollback depth, then commits through the existing fee/hold/custody/placement verifiers. `zero_step_prepared_inner_profiles_execute_with_and_without_verification` runs all four actual SDK profiles with verification enabled/disabled and independently checks completion, native custody and User hold presence. Snapshots/observers remain test-only outside benchmark recording; diagnostics distinguish attempted collection from committed ledger fees. No physical timing or complete class bound follows from these native checks.

`execute_zero_step_control` derives the Opening view, rearms its Trigger, calls `charge_pipeline_opening`, then advances nonce and completion. Collection is a no-op for System but invokes the host collector for Idle User. `execute_zero_step_on_service` authenticates the source before that call and owns atomic publication/rollback; the current worker has no explicit post-collector current-authority check. Any correction must price the complete actual branch and preserve independent lifecycle backing rather than treating callback authentication as free or adding overlapping complete owners.

The native Mock's `set_admission_authority_override(None)` clears its override; normal no-policy `MockAdmissionCertificateAuthority::current` still returns `Some`. `set_admission_authority_unavailable` instead returns `None` from the actual provider before any override, without optional-mode activation or a stubbed guard result. New externalities reset this test-only fault. `admission_authority_unavailability_is_explicit_and_resets` covers availability priority, independent override clearing/preservation and reset in both feature modes. The semantics setter writes test storage and rolls back with the attempt; off-chain fault/override/callback diagnostics require separate assertions/reset. This adds no production state and supplies no reference CPU/proof price.

Breaker refusal preserves exact Service residence. Temporal Opening charges no second Trigger fee.

Nonempty geometry checked-sums each Step's generated maximum control owner across its authored total attempt count; `StopCycle` folds its control-only effect into machine work and remains Action-fee-free. Physical terminal admission separately reserves generated `close_actor` plus applicable optional-state Close components; none is added to the Pipeline fee. The production Weight identity commits zero-Step, Opening, retry/error, continuation/RunFrame, completion, placement, and cleanup owners.

Insufficient `MinUserBalance + Pipeline total` selects `CycleAdmissionInsufficient` before Opening; Running/Suspended reuse prepaid machine authority. A Suspended User separately checks current Action liability before effect-capacity deferral: insolvency closes fee-free with custody unchanged, while a solvent deferred retry retains its complete Run and Service or Deadline authority (`user_retry_insolvency_closes_before_effect_capacity_deferral`). `StepFeeBreakdown` owns Action effect fees only: non-invoked effects, false Precondition, skipped resolution, and `FundingUnavailable` charge zero; every invoked success or typed failure settles valid actual effect Weight. System settlement stays zero.

Prepared payment witnesses separate persistent monitoring from recurring rent. Scalar and fixed-seed oracle traces span User/System Cycle boundaries, retaining capped monitors while each committed User Opening pays one current Pipeline quote; later Steps pay only invoked Action effects. Joint clean-Cycle sources retain their Run, monitor and one paid Pipeline across repeated Action waits and funded returns. Zero-Step and wait-backed nonempty geometry recompute the same Pipeline price while the Close port is unavailable; actual execution still refuses before payment without destruction authority. These are source/native payment boundaries, not physical price certification or backing for repeated capacity refusals.

User `StopCycle` Pipeline pricing folds its non-Action effect Weight into machine control through checked addition. RefTime or ProofSize overflow returns `AdmissionBoundOverflow`; exact representable boundaries remain usable. `pipeline_stop_cycle_control_overflow_refuses_each_weight_dimension` covers both dimensions alongside ordinary StopCycle pricing. This arithmetic rejection changes no valid quote and supplies no generated coefficient.

`attempt_fee_envelope` and `settle_attempt_fee_step` remain bounded forecast-vector utilities for comparative client evidence; they do not authorize runtime admission or settlement. The package-owned `fee_envelope_vectors` example emits deterministic User/System forecast, release, rollback-pricing, and protected-floor vectors consumed by browser tests.

The User state hold is paid by the identity owner through the runtime's `StateHoldCurrency` under the aggregate `RuntimeHoldReason::Actors(ActorState)` reason. Each present component prices one configured base plus a configured per-byte rate. Identity uses its concrete locator authority; the Active header prices bounded hot/process capacity with the actual Contract head and admission certificate; body uses only present tail chunks; detector uses actor-owned temporal Trigger records without shared-page slack; run reserves type-derived maximum capacity throughout Active installation, including Idle and zero-Step Actors. Create, update, Opening/progress/suspension, cancellation, deactivation, and close reconcile exact per-Actor deltas in the owning storage transaction. Positive-delta failure rolls back semantic state, while close releases the record without touching sovereign custody. TryRuntime rederives every record and compares each owner's aggregate dedicated currency hold.

`user_lifecycle_reconciles_exact_state_hold_and_preserves_exact_slot_custody` falsifies dormant/active hold deltas, deactivation shrinkage, close release, custody neutrality, and exact-slot reuse through real User lifecycle calls.

Resolution and charging follow these rules:

- A User run releases the skipped step's unused execution-fee reservation before resolving later steps, matching every non-executable cycle path.
- A multi-amount task resolves every field before dispatch and selects `FundingUnavailable > Skipped > Executable` independently of field order.

Pallet boundary tests cover fixed and current percentages across native, sufficient-asset and staking-share surfaces, plus the Transfer-only captured cycle-balance distribution. Decoding regressions reject retired amount discriminants rather than reinterpreting them. The embedding fixture binds unrelated host position keys to share assets without DEOS types.

Task execution is wrapped in a task-scoped storage transaction. If an adapter fails after an intermediate mutation, the task-local storage effects and success event are rolled back before `StepErrorPolicy` handling decides whether the cycle aborts or continues to the next step. Successful earlier Steps in the same Actor Contract remain committed.

`src/contract.rs` is the package-owned semantic classification surface. Exhaustive matches derive task adapter/assets/recipients/effects/availability/weight ownership/bounded algorithms, typed task amount roles with dependency and retry behavior, predicate observations and purity, and error-policy controls.

`TaskWeightOwner::weight` selects the corresponding method on the runtime's single `WeightInfo`; the module adds no codec, storage, runtime API, or parallel numeric weight authority. Package tests instantiate every current primitive, while a new enum variant makes its owning match non-exhaustive. The package example `semantic_manifest` verifies ordered task and amount coverage against SCALE metadata and emits one deterministic format-neutral contract projection.

The control-flow firewall combines closed types with adversarial evidence. `Step` metadata exposes exactly `precondition`, `task`, and `on_error`; exhaustive contracts admit no successor, nested contract, callback, generic `RuntimeCall`, or opaque dispatch field. Optional `Precondition` classification fixes full bounded visitation, whole-expression error, one admitted task, and false advance. Predicates and amount classifiers expose read dependencies and never a control target.

Service discovery validates the exact header cursor, reciprocal node links, Actor generation, process residence, semantic record, admission certificate, and current Step before execution. Future eligibility, insufficient RefTime or ProofSize, effect-capacity refusal, fee insolvency, breaker deferral, and terminal precedence retain their distinct owners. Negative resource preflight cannot authorize execution and leaves the complete Service head unchanged.

Suspended service validates the Run head, current certified Step, retry bound, and current Action liability before another effect attempt. Solvent refusal preserves the same cursor and exact Service or Deadline authority; insolvency selects custody-neutral cleanup. Current-state amount checks need no funding partition. Ordinary `Percent` retries reread current capacity; captured cycle-balance Transfers retain their original bounded Run basis and check current capacity independently. Execution loads only the certificate-gated current Step rather than reconstructing unreached tails.

Destination planning receives the loaded Identity, Hot state, optional coherent Run, admission certificate, and current Step resources. It selects Disabled, Service, or Deadline before mutation. The effectful core returns the exact successor Run for progress/suspension or no Run for a terminal outcome; publication consumes that result without reloading Run backing.

Opening prepares Trigger rearm in the same transaction that consumes Pending Service. Cadenced installs a fresh Tick `TriggerDeadlineHandle`; AtTime marks its one-shot source consumed; Manual and AddressEvent require no rearm mutation. Any rearm, hold, fee, or effect failure restores the paid latch and all source authority. Rearm reads no observation, so observation availability cannot refuse Opening.

`try_store_service_control_state` commits canonical semantic state, in-place Pending-to-Live promotion, and the User state-hold delta together. Promotion changes neither generation nor ring links. The dedicated regressions prove peer-order preservation, stale-kind refusal, complete rollback on hold refusal, and one committed effect after recovery.

Activation, cancellation, Contract replacement, pause/resume, and occurrence publication carry the validated admission identity and exact source residence into one transactional publication owner. A surviving destination is fully planned before source detachment; no scalar/canonical mode selector or fallback carrier exists.

Fresh multi-Step Opening loads only Step 0. One-Step Opening and Running/Suspended service likewise load only the head/current fragment, so unreached-tail corruption cannot block an authoritative committed prefix. The execution kernel visits at most one current Step per Actor per block after generation, process, Service, admission, resource, fee, and same-block revalidation. A nonterminal commit persists the advanced Run and retains Live Service with `eligible_at >= now + 1`; retry may move to Deadline, while completion, abort, and close commit atomically without replaying the prefix.

The execution kernel produces one `StepOutcome` for the current Step after canonical precondition, amount, fee, and task evaluation. `StepOutcome::Failed(TaskFailure)` retains the concrete `DispatchError` cause and orthogonal `RetryClass`; `execute_loaded_single_step_core` applies the authored policy without a simulation-only failure vocabulary.

One `AttemptDisposition::{Completed, Continued, Failed, Suspended, Closed}` owns production and simulation meaning; `Continued` commits exactly one non-terminal Step and persists the causal successor cursor. Production emits events and commits state from it; simulation returns the same disposition and final counters while its transaction rolls back. Bounded trace records wrap shared Step outcomes and do not reconstruct task, predicate, amount, fee, failure, or finalization semantics.

Production and simulation share current-Step transition owners, with the exhaustive policy/mutability/failure matrix and same-cursor Continuation regressions pinning retry identity. Task-local transactions forbid callback-visible partial mutation. Bounded vectors, adapter capability contracts, generated worst-case weights, maximum-plan tests, and circular scheduler stress bound adapter and cross-actor work without interpreting local plans recursively.

### Market Adapter Boundary

`DexOps` owns swap-only host execution; `LiquidityOps` owns add, remove, and donation operations. Actors supplies `ExecutionContext { actor, actor_type }`, resolved amounts, and authored spend/output bounds without knowing route topology, market identity, or price-source policy.

DEX adapters return `DexSwapOutcome { total_amount_in, recipient_amount_out }`. Liquidity adapters return actual add debits and LP output, remove outputs, or donation debits. Actors validates every returned committed fact against the authored input cap or output minimum before emitting its task event; an out-of-bound success is a Permanent failure inside the task-local rollback boundary.

The DEOS runtime swap success fixtures prepare two Local/Native pools, a thinner direct pool and a losing TMC candidate so Router selects the Native-anchored route. Both main DEX profiles invoke the complete prepared Task executor as System, retaining its reference-deviation guard; this selected route does not establish a universal Task maximum.

Adapters return typed task failures. Only explicitly classified Temporary failures may enter Mutable `RetryLater`; unknown downstream errors remain Permanent. Task-local transactions roll back adapter mutations before step policy runs.

Host-specific quotes, route selection, fees, oracle guards, slippage policy, and failure mapping belong in the integration architecture and embedding evidence.

Under `runtime-benchmarks`, the opaque `MaximumContextInherent` helper fixture is prepared outside measurement, dispatched inside `maximum_context_inherent`, and verified afterward. This gives the host runtime one complete maximum-context falsification owner without importing Cumulus payload or relay-proof types into the reusable package contract.

## Scheduler Architecture

### Hook Separation

- `on_initialize` performs no Actor work; the mandatory inherent is the sole pre-external phase owner.
- `actor_prepass` inherent:
  - is payload-free, versioned at the provider-data boundary, Mandatory, fee-free, and required under the runtime inherent contract; private `CanonicalActorPrepassInherentData` decodes the complete marker slice and rejects trailing bytes without changing the public codec type
  - consults the host `PrepassContext` and rejects before mutation unless required Timestamp and parachain consensus context are present
  - admits the complete fixed Prepass, independent Block/Tick Deadline, dependency-scan and finalization envelope before mutation
  - reserves and settles one Block-then-Tick Deadline quantum before the bounded parked-balance dependency scan
  - runs the canonical Service base pass against remaining Actor Control and `ActorBaseTurn`, retaining the idle-base/finalization envelope
  - advances to `ExternalPhase` and rejects duplicate or stale execution without publishing alternate scheduler authority
- `on_idle`:
  - derives finalization and Drain capacity from current-block Actor Control remaining after Prepass
  - pre-reserves the remaining Drain-control maximum and settles generated actual control before final reconciliation
  - rejects stale, wrong-phase, or optional-halted resource state before housekeeping mutation
  - advances `ExternalPhase -> FreshDrain`, services the canonical Service ring against remaining Actor Control and Shared Economic effect capacity without another Deadline quantum, writes the latest non-authoritative finalized snapshot, and finishes successful reconciliation in `Finalizable`
- `on_finalize`:
  - consumes and requires the current block's one-pass `Finalizable` marker, zero outstanding reservations, and matching telemetry block tag
  - makes any incomplete resource protocol consensus-invalid rather than silently carrying state into the next block

### Ordinary Drain Authority

The canonical persistent Service ring is the sole ordinary execution drain. Prepass and `on_idle` share its persistent next-encounter cursor and current round identity; an empty ring stops immediately, while an authoritative blocked head prevents bypass.

Mixed System/User and lower-id regressions compare each Actor's first serviced attempt in ring order. Recurring later Cycles remain independently legal, and current `Percent` resolution or fees may change later effects. Mandatory-prepass and finalized-frontier tests derive eligibility and next-attempt capacity only from `ServiceHeader`, `ServiceNodes`, `ActorProcesses`, and current semantic state.

### Admission Gates

A cycle is admitted only when all checks pass:

1. actor is ready (`trigger`, cooldown, pause/breaker/window checks)
2. per-block execution cap (`MaxExecutionsPerBlock`) not exceeded
3. a two-dimensional `WeightMeter` can consume the complete attempt plus measured pure-cleanup weight without exceeding RefTime or ProofSize
4. For an Idle User Actor consuming paid readiness: complete Pipeline Machine payment preserves `MinUserBalance`; each invoked Action independently admits its current effect fee

The certified Contract head stores the producer's declared `PipelineMachineEnvelope`; Idle admission reads that authority without rescanning the full Contract. Storage/certificate integrity does not establish phase-disjoint pricing or complete physical qualification. Opening and every later Attempt read declared predicates and current task capacity from authoritative state. Ordinary percentage amounts use that current capacity; Transfer-only cycle-balance amounts use the retained Run basis without recapturing. Running/Suspended service reuses paid machine authority, loads only the current Step, and independently admits its control, Task effect, and current Action liability.

Weight or scan deferral remains silent and state-preserving: no identity, event, nonce, cursor, Run, residence, or Task effect changes. Persistent head Weight blockage, fee-collection failure, or invariant refusal becomes observable through sparse starvation transitions; starvation does not itself identify the cause.

Deferral/terminal paths:

- insufficient Weight or scan budget → silent state-preserving deferral; actor remains active
- Pure terminal cleanup prechecks every fallible identity, count, hold, residence, reverse-index, and User-slot invariant before mutation; no close retry or requeue state exists
- After circuit-breaker refusal, admission handles the classified terminal reason before testing capacity for a new User Pipeline. Already-due nonce/failure/lifecycle closure therefore cannot be relabelled as `CycleAdmissionInsufficient`; terminal cleanup retains its own Weight admission.
- Paid readiness that cannot preserve `MinUserBalance` while paying complete Pipeline Machine → terminal `CycleAdmissionInsufficient` process cleanup with custody untouched
- `CycleResult::Completed` means authored control reached terminal without an abort: skip-only and all-failed-`ContinueNextStep` runs remain Completed, reset `unsuccessful_attempt_streak`, and may satisfy nonce auto-close. Their counters remain factual; authored `close_when` alone owns outcome-driven closure. Abort emits `Failed`; explicit invalidation emits `Cancelled`. Nonce auto-close applies on Completed or Failed Cycle boundaries, after higher-priority retry/global failure and policy terminals, without treating Failed as normal completion.
- Post-failure close selects retry-local exhaustion before the inclusive global bound `unsuccessful_attempt_streak >= MaxConsecutiveFailures` when both are reached by one Attempt. The shared finalizer emits the authoritative `CycleSummary` before pure cleanup emits `ActorClosed`, matching `AutoCloseNonceReached` ordering at Completed and Failed boundaries. `cycle_boundaries_drive_failure_reset_auto_close_and_event_order` checks the public no-policy abort boundary, custody preservation and same-attempt closure.
- Explicit, automatic, lifecycle-touch, dormant, and sweep paths share one pure cleanup routine: no Task, Precondition, fee, funding restoration, sovereign-balance movement, or population-wide scheduler scan occurs
- Owner-initiated Active or Dormant close requires `Mutable`. User Immutable rejects pause, resume, semantic Contract replacement, auto-close replacement, deactivation, cancellation, and close while still admitting its authored Manual source. A host-genesis Immutable Dormant System identity has no authored source and rejects activation. Scheduler, sweep, authored completion, auto-close, window, and Pipeline-admission terminals remain runtime-owned.
- Active close prevalidates identity, generation, process residence, counts, reverse ownership, slot ownership, and hold authority, then commits cancellation, Actor-state deletion, counter/slot release, and the close event in one storage transaction; any residual late error rolls back the complete terminal mutation
- Package and runtime tests preserve native and non-native residual custody across Mutable owner close, User Immutable zero-Step AtTime auto-close and Pipeline-admission apoptosis; exact-slot recreation receives a fresh actor id and nonce but executes against the same sovereign account. `user_lifecycle_reconciles_exact_state_hold_and_preserves_exact_slot_custody` covers real dormant/active User hold deltas, and `user_close_reclaims_park_and_pending_without_custody_or_hold_leak` closes real User Parked/Pending residence without retained authority. Prepared policy-terminal tests retain committed custody; their public counterparts remain required behind the policy admission gate. Retired productive-close behavior is not current public conformance.
- Pure close paths, including insufficient Pipeline admission, reserve `close_dispatch_weight_upper()` through `close_cleanup_weight_upper()` and the generated `close_actor` owner. `pipeline_admission_apoptosis` remains a comparative extra for the direct minimal-finalizer fixture, with no separate production Weight consumer. It accepts raw samples only and is excluded from default/all-pallet generation and candidate merge; its unused Weight trait/generated port awaits retirement in the authorized binding cohort. The complete Service-admission refusal is measured by `scheduler_service_minimal_apoptosis` and belongs to the outer Service selector; adding the standalone finalizer Weight would double-count cleanup. These owners do not establish effectful-Step terminal containment; Service admits Step and lifecycle control separately.
- Non-attempt close removes exact Service/Deadline/Parked and Trigger authority transactionally through `remove_actor_publication_and_finalize`, releases the generation-bound process exactly once, and converges on the custody-neutral finalizer.
- Post-Attempt Service close uses `finalize_actor_from_consumed_state` followed by `retire_service_member` inside the same attempt transaction. Retirement validates generation and ring authority, relinks peers, then deletes the process rather than publishing a tombstone. Late refusal restores finalization and the exact source publication.
- Deactivation uses `detach_actor_publication` to release Service/Deadline/Parked and Trigger authority and remove `ActorProcesses` while preserving the durable Dormant identity. `remove_actor_publication_and_finalize` reuses the same bounded detach owner before finalizing.
- Every bounded window validates checked `end + 1` representability and stores that exact terminal block in primary hot state. Block service/terminal reasons share one exact Block pointer and retain the earlier target; AtTime/Cadenced detection has its independent Tick pointer.
- Paused actors remain hot-only before terminal time and load `ActorContract` only when closure is due
- With `GlobalCircuitBreaker` active, normal cycles and scheduler-owned terminal cleanup defer; explicit lifecycle controls and sweep now refuse before no-op, expiry substitution, payment or publication

Creation admission remains independent of machine freeze; physical control/resource qualification remains open. `UserCreationEnabled` is a fail-closed scalar chain value and getter, explicitly installed by `GenesisConfig.user_creation_enabled` (default false). The privileged public `set_user_creation_enabled` authenticates `GlobalBreakerOrigin`, writes only that flag and emits `UserCreationEnabledSet`, including a same-value request. It remains available during freeze and neither freezes existing Actors nor bypasses creation's independent breaker guard.

Prepared `ActorResourceApi` v2 projects `ActorMachineControlFlags { user_creation_enabled, machine_frozen }` through two bounded scalar reads. These values do not describe individual System pause or the resource authority's sticky block-local halt. `class_control_machine_flags_project_independently_without_writes` exercises all four combinations through real controls, preserving exact read-only roots while an Actor stays paused and the block stays halted. The concrete runtime resource fixture and isolated native SCALE contract verify the reference binding; production descriptors/rendered conformance remain gated.

Both scalar controls currently share the stale `set_global_circuit_breaker` Weight declaration. Freeze now additionally reads current-block resource authority and conditionally records its halt, so scalar-shape equivalence does not qualify that owner. Both storage keys, the conditional resource read/write and complete public/Governance dispatch need physical qualification; no numeric coefficient changes. Public extrinsics authenticate `GlobalBreakerOrigin` before their trusted Rust hooks; the embedding caller authenticates any bounded delegation. The reference runtime's exact L2 decoder selects those hooks without Root dispatch. The integration owner records named-preset signed conformance and remaining resource/artifact prerequisites.

Runtime conformance separates seeded Actor-topology fixtures, whose test genesis explicitly admits Users, from named runtime presets, which begin with admission disabled. Named-preset tests exercise privileged flag transitions after genesis and preserve the declared System topology; the seeded fixture separately proves enabling creation and disabling it again without removing an existing User identity.

Public pause/resume, Manual occurrence, replacement, Active/Dormant close, activation/deactivation, cancellation, active-limit changes and single/batch sweep now check `ensure_machine_unfrozen`. Replacement checks before Contract validation, so expired or identical input cannot bypass the freeze refusal. Other controls retain origin authentication before refusal; both privileged scalar setters remain available. `frozen_public_controls_reject_before_noop_expiry_payment_or_publication` covers Mutable User/System, live/expired windows, Dormant authority, empty/mixed sweep and unchanged/invalid limits with exact-root and fee preservation. The added scalar reads require refreshed physical owners; no generated coefficients change.

Frozen `execute_mandatory_prepass` reads checked consensus time and opens the resource protocol, then records `optional_actor_work_halted` and opens ExternalPhase without Deadline extraction, dependency scans or Service round work. Freeze committed after Prepass records that same current-block halt; thaw never clears it. Drain still reconciles and finalizes resources, while the next block opens fresh authority. `machine_freeze_*` witnesses retain due Block/Tick handles and Service header, preserve a committed Step prefix through freeze/thaw and resume once in the next block without a second Cycle. This adds no field, queue, population scan or deadline extension. Complete producer rollback, runtime Full Executive and physical qualification remain open.

Certified ingress now checks freeze in both read-only preflight and the consequence owner. Positive Active ingress returns `GlobalCircuitBreakerActive` as Temporary before expiry, funding or Trigger matching, including source-less claimed ingress. Zero/self, absent and Dormant destinations retain balance-only classification. The consequence rechecks authority, so preflight accepted before freeze cannot authorize later mutation. Registered balance-dependency publication also refuses before revision or scan publication; unregistered assets publish nothing. `frozen_ingress_*` and `frozen_balance_dependency_publication_*` verify exact-root refusal and native-ledger producer rollback, including expired Active identity preservation. Those package witnesses are not the complete host-producer inventory or physical qualification of the additional scalar reads.

Full-freeze closure still needs maximum producer geometry, remaining Full Executive path coverage, bounded projections and complete resource owners. The integration document owns native reference-producer refusal/thaw, named-preset complete-block Governance thaw and rejected signed Native candidate rollback through the declared host import transaction. Shared guards, existing RuntimePolicy/source matching and timed-review coverage alone do not certify those blocks, thaw latency or new Weight owners; the integration owner records prepared-prefix, native-only and producer-scope limits.

The reference implementation selects complete synchronous reclamation rather than incremental retirement. `do_deactivate_actor`, `finalize_actor_loaded_inner`, and `close_inactive_actor` preflight exact generation-bound references and reclaim every bounded Contract, Run, detector, residence, hold, slot, and identity owner in one storage transaction. Production writes no `Retired` process and stores no cleanup debt or reclamation cursor; replacement-during-cleanup and debt-progress states therefore do not exist. The maximum User `close_actor` benchmark owns retained Run, maximum Contract, and state-hold release; integrity checks require that Weight to fit guaranteed Actor service. Full Deadline removal fixtures prove carrier occupancy cannot block removal. Introducing incremental cleanup would be a new specification, storage, hold, and Weight contract.

`terminal_service_reserves_cleanup_before_any_step_mutation` checks absent process/Service/Run authority after User/System terminal outcomes under both Control-ownership seams. `service_retirement_atomically_unlinks_each_topology_and_reclaims_the_process` covers interior/cursor/pair/singleton removal and exact-root refusal for stale generations, mismatched residence and corrupt rings. Try-state validates every process key against semantic identity ownership; `try_state_rejects_orphan_canonical_processes` rejects orphan tombstones that forward-only checks cannot see.

`ActorContract.close_when` replaces the former completion owner. Absent policy adds no outcome terminal; existing failure, lifetime and nonce terminals remain. `BehaviorPolicySatisfied` is the sole outcome-policy close reason. Qualified policy sources produce it through the existing synchronous finalizer after their permitted Action or control completion. Public creation, activation and replacement require the configured complete policy resources before publication. Full Contract reconstruction authenticates the selected companion; current-Step loading authenticates `has_close_when` and requires policy-specific maximum prices, never turning it into absent policy. Positive fixtures explicitly author Action success and normal Cycle thresholds; those conditions are not generally equivalent to the removed productive-Cycle rule. Pure Close still preserves custody.

Code anchors: `src/execution.rs::execute_loaded_single_step_core` produces Step facts; `src/lib.rs::{load_step_behavior_policy, reduce_step_behavior_policy, commit_step_behavior_candidate}` authenticates and reduces candidates; `src/scheduler.rs` settles monitor, permitted Action, fees and the single Close owner atomically. `behavior_runtime_*` uses prepared policy geometry to verify pass-threshold closure after Action, accumulated-count commit, clean multi-Step completion/skip rejection, zero-Step completion and exact-root rollback after late control or User Action-collector refusal with incurred effect Weight retained. Missing required host prices returns `BehaviorPolicyUnavailable`; successful native witnesses do not certify every public caller or physical bound.

Lifecycle lease-by-cycles is authored by `ActorContract.auto_close_at_cycle_nonce`: after a successful cycle reaches the configured target, the actor closes with `AutoCloseNonceReached`. Complete Mutable Contract replacement may set, shorten, extend, or clear the target; every non-empty target must remain strictly ahead of current `cycle_nonce` and within `MaxAutoCloseNonceHorizon`. No field-specific setter or increment call exists.

### Fee Collection Boundary

`execution.rs::collect_user_step_fee` revalidates current fee-native spendability against checked `MinUserBalance + actual fee` immediately before nonzero User Action collection. Pre-Task maximum reservation and amount resolution remain independent protections. A late auxiliary native debit that erodes the floor returns the existing typed Attempt invariant, independently of collector rejection. The owning Service Step transaction restores effects, Pipeline payment, progress and events; performed work remains metered. The native staking-adapter regression includes a valid debit leaving the exact floor and a one-unit excess with complete root/custody/issuance rollback. The mock's invocation log is outside consensus and records the provisional Pipeline call only. The balance read remains; the newly consumed minimum provider and checked arithmetic require complete physical qualification in the collection owner. The reference integration owns the scoped fresh-Wasm result and real pool-deposit refusal witness.

`lib.rs::derive_pipeline_machine_envelope` checked-aggregates declared per-visit Control Weight and converts once, without adding `WeightInfo::close_actor()`. Dedicated optional wait/recovery fees are checked-added separately; `pipeline_fee_breakdown` exposes the stored Balance as the machine fee. Repeated attempts still reuse the same Step maximum; this interface does not independently distinguish Opening-only, retry and finalization economic work. `charge_creation_fee` collects the configured fixed fee once at User creation; its economic obligation includes eventual complete state destruction, but the constant alone does not establish a measured lifetime-cleanup tariff. Close remains atomic, custody-neutral and subject to current-block Weight admission; fee prepayment introduces no deferred-cleanup lifecycle or second scheduler.

Pipeline derivation prices one `StopCycle` visit: this control Task cannot produce a Temporary failure or Action-liability wait, and predicate errors are Permanent. An absent precondition ends the pricing visit at that Step; a conditional return retains reachable suffix attempts. The producer preserves complete authored Steps/resources, holds and Close admission. Canonical Opening uses authenticated header/current-Step geometry and the stored Pipeline envelope without reloading unreachable tails. `pipeline_machine_envelope_prices_only_reachable_stop_cycle_visits` covers head/interior returns, conditional fall-through, economic retry bounds and prepared wait tariffs. The User/System `stop_cycle_commits_prefix_and_completes_before_unreachable_suffix` witness checks actual public creation, fees, held geometry, committed credit, skipped tail and one completion. Model prices establish this reachability correction, not complete physical or phase-disjoint bounds.

`service_turn_control_weight_upper` checked-adds common discovery/state inspection and the maximum outer ring suffix. Pipeline derivation adds this existing declared owner once per bounded Step attempt, or once for zero-Step, before fee conversion. Contract admission uses the same sum without changing its bound. Optional inspection, inner control, financial backing and StopCycle keep their separate inputs; System fees stay zero. The independent embedding's linear-price zero-Step regression compares an empty Prepass with one publicly admitted completed Cycle and catches the former omitted common owner. This qualifies arithmetic and native payment, not physical coefficients, bounded protocol refusal overhead or the complete reachable Opening/retry/completion partition. The integration owner retains red/green identities.

| Cost input | Current consumer | Ownership boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Step `control`, including optional `complete_cycle_to_parked_balance` | `derive_pipeline_machine_envelope` | Checked-adds host Weight per counted attempt before one conversion; one StopCycle visit and no unconditional-return suffix; phase-disjoint economic composition remains unestablished |
| Common discovery/state inspection and outer ring suffix | Same Pipeline derivation and fresh Contract admission | One committed admitted-turn owner per allowed attempt, or once for zero-Step; uncommitted refusals follow specification §8.8, not an extra Pipeline charge |
| `StopCycle` effect Weight | Same Pipeline derivation | Absorbed into machine pricing because this adapter-free control Task has no Action fee |
| `scheduler_inner_zero_step_complete` | Empty Pipeline derivation | Opening and retained-Actor Cycle completion, not state destruction |
| `close_actor` | `close_cleanup_weight_upper` | Physical final-destruction owner, excluded from the Pipeline fee envelope |
| `close_cleanup_weight_upper` and complete outer Service maximum | Physical Contract admission and scheduler | Resource owners remain required regardless of which fee paid for cleanup |
| `PipelineControlWeightProvider<Contract>` | Declaration-only `Config::PipelineControlWeight`; no live consumer | Economic interface distinct from physical/actual inputs; unit backend returns unavailable, not a qualified tariff |

[Specification §8.8](./specification.en.md#88-bounded-protocol-service-overhead) separates accepted protocol service overhead from the four Actor fee boundaries. Pre-Opening work in a turn whose Opening commits retains its Pipeline owner. Existing Service accounting retains incurred work on rollback; this contract clarification installs no new fee, escrow, counter, reserve or allocator. Conservative per-encounter and block-wide bounds, useful-service/fairness impact and production binding remain unqualified by the native declaration checks.

`user_pipeline_machine_envelope_prices_control_retries_without_destruction`, `zero_step_pipeline_machine_envelope_prices_generated_control_without_destruction` and `pipeline_machine_envelope_absorbs_stop_cycle_control_effect` witness the fee partition. `persistent_pipelines_pay_only_cycle_control_before_fee_free_close` checks two paid Cycles and final custody-neutral destruction with no additional Actors fee. `contract_admission_uses_class_neutral_canonical_step_resources` separately preserves close and complete-outer capacity.

The constant nonzero mock conversion rejects the former per-attempt monetary sum. `pipeline_machine_envelope_checks_both_cumulative_weight_dimensions` rejects cumulative overflow across Steps and retries in either dimension and accepts an exact maximum aggregate. These pure arithmetic inputs are not admitted physical prices. Host-specific coefficient soundness and phase-disjoint reachable composition remain separate obligations; generic `StepControlWeightProvider` does not prove arbitrary host composition excludes destruction.

For a committed nonempty Close-only Pipeline, ordinary source phases form the following finite control graph. `i` is the cursor, `k` the current cursor's unsuccessful count and `g` the carried global streak. A Running successor requires `i + 1 < N`; a final advancement exits instead. Every edge still requires ordinary authority, admission, predicates, Task preparation and policy reduction; this is not permission to fabricate a source or skip those checks.

```text
Opening(0,g)     -> Running(1,g)   | Suspended(0,1,g+1)   | Exit
Running(i,g)     -> Running(i+1,g) | Suspended(i,1,g+1)   | Exit
Suspended(i,k,g) -> Running(i+1,g) | Suspended(i,k+1,g+1) | Exit
```

Running starts beyond cursor zero in this Close-only graph, but a Running Attempt can suspend. Suspended return retains the cursor; advancement resets only its local count. `prepare_loaded_resuspension` increments both local and global unsuccessful counters and selects terminal failure at either limit. An ordinary successful prefix does not reset `g`; completed Cycle does. `RetryLater` is Mutable-only, Temporary/funding-limited; Permanent errors terminate or advance through their authored policy, never suspend. StopCycle has no retry/Action-liability edge: false predicate may advance, success completes and predicate errors are Permanent. An unconditional StopCycle excludes its suffix.

`Exit` denotes one selected Completed/Failed boundary or an independent terminal Close, not multiple additive finalizers. `execute_effectful_step_control` applies failure, policy and nonce precedence; publication/placement follows only the selected successor. Ordinary reduction/monitor/publication work remains chargeable control, while actual final state destruction is separately Creation-backed and currently admitted. Zero-Step has one Opening/completion encounter, not this cursor graph. Pre-Opening terminal paths, uncommitted collector/invariant refusals and arbitrary repeated inspections do not become prepaid Pipeline visits. AwaitFunding has its own recovery overlay, including first-Action Running cursor zero; it must not inherit the narrower Close-only exclusion.

If the reachable prefix has `N` Steps, effective local ceilings `r_i` (one for StopCycle or no retry) and host global cutoff `G > 0`, ordinary visits are bounded by `N + min(G - 1, sum(r_i - 1))` from the least restrictive starting streak zero. Every revisit needs an earlier nonterminal unsuccessful Attempt; those increments persist across cursors and at most `G - 1` can precede another visit. A terminal failure does not buy another visit. This is a control-flow upper, not a Weight, tariff or guarantee of any Task outcome. Window, policy and other terminals can only shorten this graph; they do not justify ignoring their work owners.

The fee producer adds one declared first visit for every Step in the reachable prefix, then assigns at most `MaxConsecutiveFailures - 1` extra visits under each local ceiling. `maximum_pipeline_repeat_cost` sorts bounded `(cost, capacity)` choices and takes the highest-cost available units. An exchange of a cheaper chosen unit for a more expensive available unit cannot lower the bound. The scalar owner is reused independently for RefTime, ProofSize and dedicated wait backing; every multiply/add is checked. Its three coordinate maxima may describe different paths, not one jointly observed physical maximum or three charges for one visit.

Greedy traversal also depends on capacity distribution: larger local ceilings can consume the shared budget in fewer choices, so a maximum-ceiling input need not maximize source-level helper work. `pipeline_repeat_cost_checked_work_is_not_maximized_by_retry_ceiling` in `src/tests/storage_and_api.rs` instantiates the real helper with a test-only counted amount: twelve choices and nine units select one versus nine choices at capacities nine versus one, for both zero and positive costs. Checked additions/multiplications are observed without changing production code. This diagnostic instantiation does not measure optimized integer/sort instructions, physical RefTime maxima or justify altering retry semantics.

The compiler's direct callers are `decompose_admitted_contract_geometry`, `user_pipeline_machine_capacity_requirement` and diagnostic `do_try_state`. Decomposition reaches fresh insertion, legacy/canonical replacement and technical financial-wait recertification through Contract storage. `ensure_current_contract_admission` authenticates hashes/wake/current authority and optional Close containment; it does not compile the Pipeline fee. Ordinary `try_store_service_control_state` publishes semantic/hot state without rebuilding Contract geometry. Repeated Service visits therefore do not acquire this three-sort installation cost merely because they authenticate a certificate.

| Compiler caller | Invocation scope | Physical coverage consequence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fresh/activated Contract storage | One full decomposition during installation | User nonempty retry bodies exercise all repeat choices; System returns economic zero before allocation/sorting |
| Contract replacement | New authored geometry, including certificate renewal for financial waiting | Mutable User target retry payload matters independently of the old Run/custody source |
| Public Rust capacity helper | Constructs ordinary no-policy Close quote from Steps | Host-callable utility, not an automatic Service refresh; caller must own its cost if used on-chain |
| `do_try_state` | Diagnostic whole-state reconciliation | Repeated recompilation is verifier work, not production hot-path tariff evidence |

The producer uses at most one repeat choice per Step and three bounded sorts: `O(N log N)` time and `O(N)` temporary memory, without a loop or allocation proportional to the host global cutoff. This is installation/reconfiguration arithmetic, not stored remaining machine budget, rent or new consensus state. Current Hot streak is not reset or debited to obtain a quote; the cached envelope covers the least restrictive admissible starting streak. Complete path-priced declaration/consumer qualification remains separate; the prepared tariff interface below is not yet consumed.

`pipeline_repeat_cost_matches_independent_bounded_allocations` compares the selector with exhaustive allocations across eight cost/capacity models, seven budgets, both orderings and both scalar widths, including zero capacity, ties, exact maxima and overflow. The independent embedding's linear-price `pipeline_quotes_share_the_global_retry_budget_across_steps` checks one/two/three-Step bodies and local ceilings two/ten against the shared global cutoff two. All six sources use public creation, stored/API quote checks and ordinary close before reusing the owner slot; no balance, process or configured-capacity reset supplies the result.

| Available input or method | Information retained | Missing economic proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `StepControlWeightContext` plus Step maximum | Cursor, fragment/Opening-tail/predicate geometry; one physical maximum across phases/outcomes | No declared Opening-only/retry/completion split; repeating it cannot identify the chargeable path |
| `StepControlExecution` plus actual provider | Observed phase, disposition, placement, invocation and collection branch | A post-execution resource bound may retain the whole maximum; it is not a declared speculative branch-price contract |
| Current Pipeline producer arguments | Steps, class, funding disposition, resource maxima, zero-Step and policy inspection | Uses the configured shared global cutoff; no policy expression/state path or phase-priced branch matrix is supplied |
| Authenticated Contract and host authority | Complete authored semantics and configured bounds for a uniform cached envelope | Qualified branch costs and bounded path composition still need an explicit owner; refundable holds and current state cannot manufacture that evidence |

`adapters.rs::PipelineControlWeightProvider<Contract>` now exports the separate economic declaration: non-optional catalog identity plus complete-Contract `Option<Weight>`. `Config::PipelineControlWeight` requires a host binding; `()` always returns unavailable with a deterministic identity. No live compiler, authority composer or scheduler calls it. The current producer, stored Balance, physical Step/effect ports and two-input authority identity remain unchanged. [Embedding §1A](./embedding.md#1a-pipeline-tariff-owner-proposal) owns the complete promise and future routing rather than another public phase/exit ontology.

The independent consumer's `pipeline_tariff` tests execute synthetic declarations without externalities, retain encoded inputs, reject unsupported domains and vary catalog/model/converter identities while selected Weights remain equal. Its unit-bound baseline proves current physical authority and System completion remain available with zero System fees, while current User creation still caches its positive legacy envelope. This preparation-only baseline is not a fallback contract for the later consumer switch. Models do not qualify physical units, backing or production tariffs.

A phase-aware economic declaration must exclude Task effects and final destruction and compose only selected successors. Existing physical maximum/actual behavior remains usable for admission and conservative metering; manufacturing `StepControlExecution` at installation cannot qualify the prepared tariff. Numerical/caller qualification and coordinated Result/identity/cache/artifact switching remain later gates.

`Pallet::benchmark_pipeline_tariff_candidate<Provider, Converter>` is test/benchmark-only. It reuses canonical-source/kernel syntax checks, rejects AwaitFunding and returns plain Rust `tariff` diagnostics. A supported User quote uses one complete declaration and one conversion, with no added physical/component owners. System bypasses all User pricing callbacks and gets economic zero while retaining physical authority. The separate candidate identity hashes class/mutability, full Contract semantics, current authority, catalog and quoted values; it never writes authority, headers or process state. Account/host admission and public policy availability remain outside this diagnostic path.

Independent `pipeline_tariff_candidate` tests cover zero/nonempty/policy syntax, both price and source refusal, full-input unsupported domains, exact one-conversion ownership, model/converter/header/mutability identity changes and unchanged output after ordinary ledger/block changes. Package `pipeline_tariff` tests cover every physical-authority field plus pre-query retry/Trigger/canonical-source refusal. The infallible converter's internal numerical behavior is not introspectable: exact extrema are accepted without invented overflow checks, and converter-domain soundness remains a qualification obligation. These model observations provide no tariff coefficient or physical/caller bound.

Main `manual_trigger` and `address_event_trigger_occurrence` now retain maximum Steps, signed funding, balance watches and a live window through public Mutable User admission. Header assertions retain every legal payload and exclude the nonce payload incompatible with watches; Manual keeps its own Trigger width. AddressEvent matches the last entries of both canonical allowlists, attaining the full linear filter search. Verification requires exactly one useful-readiness fee, B+1 Pending residence, nonce zero and no Run. Signed Manual keeps FRAME's signer allowance while its sovereign and Fee Sink remain measured; automatic AddressEvent excludes that allowance. Thin main fixtures and duplicate full-header extras are removed. These selected success paths do not certify complete class/residence/failure, policy/wait, placement or host physical maxima; source publication and later Pipeline/Actions remain independent.

`creation_payment_survives_activation_replacement_and_unfunded_removal` follows Dormant creation, activation, expansion into a tail chunk, deactivation and reactivation. Only the original Creation Fee is collected through reconfiguration; holds expand and release independently. Funding exactly one useful Manual occurrence but no Pipeline causes automatic removal with no additional Actors fee, no Task effect, retained native/foreign custody, released slot/holds and nonzero current-block Control accounting. This proves the payment/lifecycle boundary, not a worst-case cleanup tariff or Weight bound.

The generic pallet collects creation, successfully processed Manual, AddressEvent, AtTime, and Cadenced Trigger occurrences, per-admitted-cycle Pipeline Machine, and per-invoked-Action User fees through one runtime-supplied `FeeCollector`; terminal cleanup charges no Actors fee. Both User creation calls collect `ActorCreationFee` inside the creation transaction. Dormant creation collects before slot allocation; Active creation allocates the slot provisionally before collecting. Failed collection rolls back both branches; neither allocation nor payment survives refusal. System creation remains exempt.

Actors invokes the runtime-supplied collector exactly once only when one Trigger changes `pending_signal` from false to true. Manual, matching AddressEvent, AtTime, and Cadenced use their generated family owner and emit `TriggerOccurrenceProcessed` only for that useful transition. An already-latched occurrence performs no Actor-specific fee, event, activation, or causal-history update. Trigger underfunding rolls back readiness publication without closing the process, except that a consumed one-shot User AtTime closes through prepaid fee-free minimal apoptosis with `TriggerAdmissionInsufficient`. Collector rejection rolls back the complete occurrence and restores exact source authority. Collection is ledger-only: it performs no Actors ingress preflight, readiness mutation, or scheduler placement. Zero collection is a no-op.

`manual_trigger()` is both the signed dispatch Weight and one useful User Manual Trigger fee basis. A paid maximum-Step signed-funding/watch User latch exceeds the retained short-header dispatch coefficient in complete RefTime and even in measured PoV bytes versus retained charged ProofSize. This selected result does not qualify all Manual placements or justify charging later Pipeline/Action effects at Trigger time.

A completed Cycle retains Idle semantics with `pending_signal` false and no Run, and leaves Service until useful readiness. Manual completion without a live window uses Protocol-disabled Idle with no residence; a live window retains only its expiry Deadline. Parked completion retains its bounded review, and independent temporal Trigger authority survives. A later occurrence re-latches through the common transactional publisher. B+1 eligibility becomes Pending Service; later eligibility becomes one primary Block Deadline. Duplicate latched occurrences coalesce before fee admission.

The native scheduling cohort now checks passive completion after ordinary, retry and Percent traces, one paid occurrence per Cycle and no free Actor scan after completion. Closed-round and defensive-attempt discovery fixtures retain a real multi-Step Running Actor instead of reading a nonexistent completed member; their full-root and no-replay checks remain. Direct/pass-owned accounting compares the same final Empty discovery. The independent linear-price zero-Step test additionally requires exact equality to its declared admitted Control. These are state/payment declaration checks, not physical prices or public policy admission.

`scheduler.rs::latched_idle_deadline` uses the existing eligibility/window arithmetic without storage reads. After bounded Pending publication, `commit_canonical_trigger_occurrence_with_authority` transfers that transient Service member to the selected Deadline in the same occurrence transaction. The paid latch survives; due extraction obeys B+1. Native public User/System cooldown and expiry cases cover retained-Service sources, duplicate coalescence and late collector rollback. A selected zero-Step User window diagnostic covers replacement of an initial expiry Deadline by window-start readiness. Transient writes and both carrier mutations remain real occurrence costs; no complete physical maximum or production tariff follows. This excludes known-delay polling for this publisher, not unrelated Idle no-work, capacity, collector or invariant refusals.

- Predicate and Task preparation run read-only before collection determines the Step outcome.
- Idle readiness consumption first charges the fixed-size certified Pipeline Machine fee and emits `PipelineFeeCharged`; collection failure rolls back latch/residence mutation and preserves the Service head. Current-Step admission still meters control/effect Weight component-wise but reserves only the Action-effect maximum economically. Before a Suspended User retry, the scheduler proves the current maximum Action liability above `MinUserBalance`; insufficiency closes through fee-free minimal apoptosis before invocation, while the enclosing Step transaction settles valid actual cost and releases the remainder. False Precondition, skipped resolution, `FundingUnavailable`, and `StopCycle` charge no Action fee; an invoked success or typed failure settles valid actual effect Weight and appends `ActionFeeCharged` after semantic boundary events as the attempt's final economic receipt.
- Collection failure rolls back the complete scheduler transaction, including provisional Task dispatch, residence, close, and every fee, event, counter, nonce, cursor, or Run mutation. Missing or excessive actual evidence fails before collection and rolls back the same atom. Simulation reports interface-local `FeeCollectionFailed`.
- An invoked adapter failure reports `Invoked` effect evidence and retains its valid actual effect fee when the enclosing attempt and collection commit; `ContinueNextStep` and `AbortCycle` never alter that charge or trigger another collection. Zero actual total fee produces no collector call.
- `TaskEffectWeightProvider` owns maximum admission and one closed post-dispatch branch: `NotInvoked` returns zero, while `Invoked` returns the canonical generated Task-family Weight whether the operation commits or returns typed failure. Scheduler service rejects absent or component-wise greater-than-reserved evidence and rolls back the complete process/Step/effect transaction. Successful non-invocation consumes control only and releases the effect reservation inside the pass budget.
- `StepControlWeightProvider` receives the starting phase, committed outcome, canonical post-placement class (`None`, `Queue`, or `Wakeup`, where the retained code names map to no residence, Service, or Deadline), typed Task invocation evidence, and independently whether nonzero Action fee collection is required for the attempt to commit. The runtime keeps invocation-receipt Control separate from fee collection. A receipt-free estimate adds the generated receipt owner for general-path invocation, including System zero-fee attempts; a direct estimate already containing receipt deposition adds nothing. Maximum composition compares complete envelopes, and conservative maximum fallback retains its included receipt allowance. `StopCycle` emits no receipt, with or without predicates; a host quote's conservative receipt allowance does not prove emission. These additions are staged components; complete generated Weight rebinding remains required.
