Joined Park/Tick fixtures install an unconsumed AtTime after actual Manual completion, with a checked future due tick aligned to public guards. Guard staging surrounds replacement, without clock rewind or fabricated Trigger authority. Public pause removes physical detector membership and preserves mathematical Trigger clocks in a Disabled process. `system_temporal_pause_preserves_clocks_without_detector_membership` covers AtTime/Cadenced pause and resume; try-state requires detector membership only for unpaused, unlatched temporal Actors and rejects a paused pointer.

Corners 20/21 join interior Tick geometry with that full-length Transfer plan, wide funding and host-configured Park watches. The fixture checks completed Q1 Steps, namespace detachment, peer preservation and late rollback. Historical four-watch/fanout measurements have no current-domain authority.

`park_review_namespace_late_cleanup_restores_original_episode_and_pending` covers User timed/causal negative and direct/mixed entry. One missing native ActorState hold unit permits strict original semantic loading but makes terminal release fail after detachment. Exact root, episode/anchors, Pending, overdue deadline, committed credit, custody and fees restore; the complete admitted maximum remains charged and no Close event survives. Repairing the hold and advancing one block closes from the original overdue head against another exhausted public C32 future destination, without a new deposit, replay, compensation or fee. Mock non-native balances are asserted separately from trie roots. This diagnostic does not establish arbitrary-host currency or ordinary-block physical containment.

`causal_and_address_ingress_namespace_owner_partition` uses public User/System committed-Cycle sources for pre-due negative, due positive, certified AddressEvent and due negative. Public peers retain full C32 epochs with synthetic exhausted counters. Pre-due causal negative retains its existing review without append; due positive and AddressEvent remove Park authority and wake B+1 Service without future append. Due negative reaches the future carrier and exact namespace closes from original Park authority, consuming Pending without replay or payment. The causal publisher/scan only creates bounded Pending authority. Surviving retained counters and future peers remain unchanged; the mock non-native ledger is checked separately from trie roots. All successful shapes pass canonical state validation.

`commit_canonical_trigger_occurrence_with_authority` passes actual `now` to `wake_parked_member_to_service`; the shared `insert_service_member` alone derives B+1. `parked_occurrence_uses_current_round_once_and_restores_late_refusal` covers public User/System Manual/AddressEvent occurrence after a committed Cycle with another live Service peer. New members retain current-round metadata, join behind the peer and become eligible exactly B+1; opening the current round succeeds. Provisional Park detachment followed by a malformed peer or late User Trigger collector failure restores exact root and episode. Repaired retry collects only one committed User Trigger fee, no System fee or new Pipeline/Action fee; the nontransactional collector spy distinguishes attempt from payment.

`process_parked_dependency_review` carries its supplied future target as internal `due_successor` through the weighted causal consumer. After due extraction, negative classification consumes Pending and replaces its timed review using that target before publishing the complete successor; ordinary pre-due service supplies `None` and retains its existing future review. Positive classification keeps its existing wake. The existing whole-review transaction restores old deadline/Pending/registration authority on late carrier failure. No queue, fee, storage/call/event schema or generated Weight method is added. This new control-flow/placement branch still requires fresh physical evidence; model/native conformance does not establish tariff or coefficient containment.

`parked_due_pending_handoff_preserves_anchor_and_rearms_without_new_deposit` covers User/System healthy and malformed future C32 destinations after two committed Transfers and one subthreshold causal mint. Either-dimensional short capacity performs no worker mutation. Complete current review admission consumes Pending and publishes one future review while retaining generation, fixed anchors, ownership, committed recipient credit and the fee ledger. Late malformed-carrier rejection restores exact original root/Pending; repair permits successor publication without an additional deposit, replay or compensation. Independent cleanup admission and original-authority namespace Close cover both timed and causal negative future rearm; physical qualification remains open.

`deadline_destination_search_is_bounded_by_reachable_active_population` directly constructs C32 pages/process handles, not a public Actor population. It checks the representation limit, forced append refusal, member saturation and malformed header. Retain that evidence without treating restored occupancy, an ignored vacancy or missing semantic identities as a reachable creation benchmark.

`user_creation_admission_clock_targets_separate_block_exhaustion_from_tick_range` admits exact `now+cooldown` and window `end+1` at the native Block maximum, and rejects the next unrepresentable target without fees/state changes. Temporal creation uses the native mock's saturating millisecond clock; it does not confuse its Block maximum with `SchedulerTick::MAX`. If positive `CadenceTickMillis` and the configured delay satisfy `ceil(u64::MAX/tick_millis)+MaxTemporalDelayTicks <= u64::MAX`, initial AtTime/Cadenced target overflow is excluded for every admitted timestamp. The test pins this inequality for the native host and separately preserves checked rejection at 1 ms helper extrema; neither is reference Full Executive or arbitrary-host evidence.

Creation never invokes a Task adapter or `ObservationProvider` and allocates no funding-asset inventory. Different authored assets affect encoded operands, validation, locator policy or host resource-provider callbacks; any extra asset-ledger proof must identify an actual reached callback. Native creation payment/hold remains a real currency owner. `MaxFundingTrackedAssets` supplies no production creation ceiling. Signed transaction payment, eventual destruction tariffs and later Trigger/Pipeline/Action costs remain distinct from these creation-body owners.

### Lifecycle Control

Mutable Actors may replace Contract, deactivate, cancel, or close through their authorized control surface. Individual pause/resume additionally requires System class. `ensure_system_pause_origin` preserves owner/`SystemOrigin` authority and rejects an authorized User owner with `BadOrigin` before expiry substitution or exact no-op handling. `class_control` tests preserve the complete storage root, holds, custody and Running/Suspended progress across User refusals; existing System lifecycle/retry/deadline tests cover retained pause/resume behavior. Immutable User Actors reject owner destruction/mutation but still execute authored triggers and mandatory terminal semantics. Immutable System Actors reject Manual admission and external lifecycle mutation. User Contracts cannot contain `Mint`.

A Cycle derives `run.cycle_nonce = identity.cycle_nonce + 1`; termination commits that nonce once. Running/Suspended state retains one coherent Run, while Idle retains none. Nonce exhaustion, window expiry, retry/global failure bounds, productive completion, auto-close, Pipeline apoptosis, and sweep use their typed terminal owner without granting arbitrary close authority.

Manual and AddressEvent retain block cooldown/window gates. AtTime/Cadenced use independent Tick Trigger deadlines and admit neither block cooldown nor windows. A busy Cadenced Actor keeps its current Service or retry Deadline while the Trigger worker installs one future aligned Tick; it creates no deferred Cycle or fee. New Service publication in block `B` remains ineligible until `B + 1`.

Package and runtime regressions cover activation/deactivation, pause/resume, Contract replacement, cooldown/window ordering, busy cadence, exact-slot custody reuse, Park/Pending cleanup, synchronous close, and actor-to-actor B+1 ingress.

Canonical `do_deactivate_actor` detaches publication once but preserves stored Active/Run authority through full Contract/monitor geometry re-authentication. It advances only the local Dormant successor, retires geometry and the stored Run, then replaces Active directly with Dormant. No intermediate Idle semantic or Service successor is published. This lets a busy clean monitor authenticate against its still-present Run until that epoch is removed. `behavior_runtime_clean_run_deactivation_preserves_custody_and_retires_policy` uses an unaged admitted native constructor, real block advancement and a genuine first Transfer; four Idle/Running clean/non-clean cases preserve custody/slot/generation, cancel the open nonce exactly once, reconcile only released hold geometry and restore the complete prefix under outer rollback.

### Close cost partition

`lib.rs::close_actor` calls read-only `load_close_authority` to classify state and check origin/mutability before selecting Active finalization or Dormant destruction. Active `finalize_actor` calls read-only `load_finalization_authority` to reload/compare the instance and resolve canonical generation; canonical `scheduler.rs::remove_actor_publication_and_finalize` transactionally detaches publication and invokes the consumed-state finalizer. Internal terminal callers may enter with authority already loaded, so a shared finalizer measurement alone does not cover the public-call wrapper.

| Segment | Source owner | Cost-relevant alternatives |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Authority loading | `load_close_authority`, `close_actor`, `load_finalization_authority`, `detach_actor_publication_inner` | Public origin/mutability and machine-freeze checks, repeated authority/body loading, full admission-certificate rebuilding, Run-byte comparisons and legacy-absence reads |
| Independent Trigger | `detach_actor_publication_inner` | Absent or Tick source; exact page removal and key/index repair can coexist with Block retry/review cleanup |
| Exclusive residence | `detach_actor_publication_inner` | Service Pending/Live unlink, Block retry or Tick financial-expiry Deadline removal, Parked release, or Disabled with no residence; then process removal |
| Parked release | `release_parked_dependency_authority`, `remove_dependency_registration` | Watch count, linked C32 page width, bounded hole reclamation, source-ring retirement and optional Block review removal |
| Publication epilogue | `detach_actor_publication_inner` | Process deletion plus semantic reload/write after exclusive residence and independent Trigger removal |
| Cancellation | `execution.rs::cancel_run_internal_loaded` | Idle has no Run; Running/Suspended authenticate optional policy, remove the Run and emit cancellation/summary events. Clean tracking additionally clears the monitor and publishes closing Idle semantic state before geometry removal; no Task or successor publication on Close |
| Active destruction | `finalize_actor_loaded_inner`, `remove_active_actor_with_admission` | Admitted tail chunks, semantic removal, counters, User slot/hold release versus System locator vacancy |
| Loaded Contract removal | `remove_loaded_contract_geometry` | Head/tail deletion, optional policy lookup and required monitor removal, and financial companion removal; source loading and other destruction owners remain separate |
| Dormant destruction | `close_inactive_actor` | Identity/counter and class-specific slot/locator/hold cleanup, without Active Contract or process teardown |

The standard no-policy `close_actor` benchmark records the complete public Pallet call on an ordinarily created User with Manual Trigger and a live retry Run. Setup retains the configured full body with a reachable first Task and wide unvisited successors; it does not union Parked, temporal or maximum Deadline/index/hold geometries. Existing `close_public_wide_header_prefix` and `close_public_wide_idle` add categorical class/owner-slot and Running/Suspended/Idle alternatives; their late-hold counterparts prove whole-call rollback under explicit host-currency inconsistency. Source preparation, economic prefix and verification stay outside timing. These four categorical extras require exact raw/extra selection and cannot enter regression, candidate merge or bulk generation. None is a universal ordinary Close upper or signed-Executive witness.

For canonical Active Close-only policy paths, the following source-derived occurrences use `G` for geometry optional authentication, `C` for Contract context through Close-price selection, `K` for Closing reset and `D` for optional delete/drop. `L` is one when the supplied state has a live Run, otherwise zero; no-clean `K` returns before monitor writes. Counts assume preceding identity/host checks pass, exclude earlier caller preparation where stated and are not measured full-path maxima or proof summands. AwaitFunding, malformed/legacy state and host-callback internals remain outside this table.

| Entry boundary | G | C | K | D |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Public canonical Active `close_actor` | 4 | 2 | L | 1 |
| Selected Service close branch, starting at its `close_envelope` quote | 3 | 3 | L | 1 |
| `remove_actor_publication_and_finalize`, supplied full state | 2 | 2 | L | 1 |
| Step `finalize_actor_from_consumed_state` boundary | 1 | 1 | L | 1 |
| Raw `close_finalize_policy` detached cases `c0..8` | 1 | 0 | L | 1 |

Public Close loads full authority in `load_close_authority` and again in `load_finalization_authority`; detachment loads full admitted geometry once more, then retirement reconstructs it again. `detach_actor_publication_inner` and `finalize_actor_loaded_inner` each perform their own Close-price context query. Selected Service terminal cleanup adds its explicit quote and terminal-only full reload before the shared detachment/finalization path. Failed checks stop at their actual prefix: successful-suffix counts must not be assigned to a refused call. The branch table does not include earlier Service inspection, control/candidate publication or resource queries.

Normal Step and zero-Step terminal control instead call `finalize_actor_from_consumed_state` before `retire_service_member`; they do not pass through ordinary detachment. Candidate/clean-state publication remains Step control. Zero-Step has no Run and skips `K`. Intrinsic `finalize_actor_loaded_state` includes `K`, retirement `G`, `D`, ordinary state/hold/slot/event work and its transaction; the detached raw wrapper adds view construction and the explicit benchmark commit/rollback guard but skips the outer Close-price gate. It is not disjoint from authentication/reset/delete components. Retained-Service `c9` instead records intrinsic finalization plus caller retirement atomically; that joint boundary differs from the historical detached one.

Retirement `reconstruct_contract_geometry` also rebuilds Steps and checks full semantic/body commitments; the isolated `G` block and pure `C` profile do not jointly measure all that surrounding work. A successful live cancellation removes Run, and `remove_active_actor_with_admission` executes another Run removal; both executed operations belong to ordinary retirement even though they share one key. Public dispatch, sweep-member and producer wrappers retain their own complete bounds; a returned optional price cannot update their annotations or supply monetary backing.

A live policy-bearing Run reaches `clear_behavior_cycle_for_cancellation` before its Run is removed. Clean tracking reads and authenticates policy/monitor/source, writes `clean_cycle = None` and, for Closing, publishes Idle identity/Hot into the semantic record. Subsequent `remove_active_actor_with_admission` reloads full geometry; `reconstruct_contract_geometry` again authenticates policy/monitor and required Run coherence. Idle/None then remains valid without the removed Run. The closing reset is therefore not proven redundant by the later monitor deletion. Contract-side Close lookup separately derives policy context, including `semantic_contract_id` hashing the full authored Steps. These are real caller/finalizer work, not part of the raw delete/drop fragment or automatically covered by its I/O count.

`load_authenticated_geometry_close_when` owns exactly the optional reconstruction occurrence: load/verify the cold policy, conditionally authenticate its monitor/episode/clean Run, then move the expression to the reconstructed Contract. `reconstruct_contract_geometry` keeps financial/count/chunk prerequisites before this region and ordinary Step/body reconstruction after it. `Some(None)` means no authored policy; missing or incoherent required state returns `None`. The monitor temporary and remaining policy plan drop at the original points; the expression remains caller-owned. No ActorRef reload, clone, transaction, price lookup or mutation is added. The forwarding facade is test/runtime-benchmarks-only, not a public runtime API.

`behavior_runtime_geometry_policy_authentication_preserves_source_and_prefix` reuses the existing five policy shapes over six native sources. User custody is funded before installation; natural blocks and real service produce four paid Transfer prefixes. Missing-policy/monitor faults live only inside rollback; a supplied header mismatch never changes stored authority. Fragment/full-loader refusals and restored roots preserve custody, holds, process, slots, nonce and paid prefix. Recording executes only the shared kernel; the table lists unique keys, not read multiplicity, optimized cost or universal maxima.

| Native source | Unique recorded keys | Records |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Absent policy, Running | 0 | None |
| CurrentOutcome/no monitor, Idle | 2 | Policy, Semantic |
| Zero-counter clean, Idle | 3 | Policy, Semantic, Monitor |
| Zero-counter clean, Running | 4 | Policy, Semantic, Monitor, Run |
| Accumulated/no clean, Running | 3 | Policy, Semantic, Monitor |
| Maximum accumulated clean, Running | 4 | Policy, Semantic, Monitor, Run |

All six kernel sources produce zero overlay writes with optional prices unavailable. The native recorder does not prove access multiplicity or compiled timing equivalence. This isolates one additional occurrence only; Closing cancellation/reset, repeated reconstruction, full-body context, enclosing finalization and caller declarations remain separate qualification obligations.

The generic `prepare_behavior_source_snapshot_in_domain` constructs an ordinary source before selecting its component policy. For Running/Suspended, `stage_behavior_source_snapshot` then rebinds the persisted Run's Contract/admission/service identities, updates Semantic/head/tail bindings and stages monitor facts from the executed ordinary prefix. Existing component profiles explicitly disclose this construction; it is not a pre-authored paid policy Run or public-admission witness. Reusing this staged constructor must not silently upgrade its provenance to that of the separate pre-authored native sources.

Extra raw-only `geometry_behavior_authentication(c)` uses the shared kernel with a preloaded header and retains its returned expression outside recording. Its finite User sources are absent Idle (`0`), CurrentOutcome/no-monitor Idle (`1`), zero-counter clean Idle/Running (`2`/`3`), accumulated/no-clean Running (`4`) and maximum accumulated-clean Running (`5`). Policy staging occurs only while Idle; the last three execute a genuine paid first Transfer through ordinary retained envelopes. Head/tails/policy remain unchanged across that prefix. Source binding, collection, full-body reconstruction, caller transaction and returned-expression destruction are outside the kernel block; this staged component source is not public policy admission or a qualified tariff.

Native profile checks exercise verification on/off, exact extra metadata, supplied-header and missing-required-record refusals, whole-root rollback and the exact unique-key sets above (absent is Idle here). Every kernel records zero overlay writes with optional price ports unavailable. Script self-tests exclude the profile from bulk selection and refuse missing extras, Weight generation and candidate merge. These establish finite source integrity and isolation, not reference-host execution, physical maxima, complete caller costs or economic backing.

`load_behavior_cleanup_source` reads the policy only for a present authored marker and refuses absence before any write. `remove_loaded_behavior_state` consumes that loaded policy, deletes it and conditionally deletes the monitor for Accumulated or clean-Cycle tracking. `remove_loaded_contract_geometry` keeps chunk-count validation and ordinary head removal between those fragments, then financial removal and ordinary tail removal afterward. The helpers add no clone or transaction; the loaded policy is dropped before financial/tail writes. Together the fragments exclude ordinary geometry, financial state and enclosing destruction/caller work.

`behavior_runtime_policy_only_cleanup_preserves_collateral_and_prefix` checks five native classes: absent, CurrentOutcome without monitor, zero-counter clean tracking, Accumulated without clean tracking and maximum four-by-four policy/full monitor. Four sources retain a real first-Transfer prefix; the first-success policy remains Idle. Recorded reads and overlay writes contain only applicable policy/monitor keys. Root oracles at matching FRAME depth verify collateral preservation, missing-policy refusal and enclosing rollback. The test/benchmark facade is not a public transition or qualified physical price.

Extra raw-only `behavior_closing_reset(c)` isolates `clear_behavior_cycle_for_cancellation`, including its own head/policy authentication, conditional monitor/Run authentication and Closing monitor/Semantic writes. Three genuine pre-Opening User paid sources select accumulated/no-clean, zero-counter clean and maximum accumulated-clean. Setup invokes real inner Closing cancellation inside full rollback to capture the clean successor instead of synthesizing Idle authority; the paid source is restored exactly. The source retains its process and Run: it is not detached-finalizer topology or a public Close witness. Trial cancellation, caller transaction, Run removal, events and later retirement stay outside recording.

The reset kernel ends at an intermediate caller-owned Closing phase, not a valid standalone terminal publication. Matching-depth expected-root oracles allow only the observed monitor/Semantic changes; no-clean returns without writes and ignores successor arguments. Native profile checks cover verification on/off, supplied-admission mismatch, missing policy/monitor and incoherent Run refusals, outer rollback of the paid prefix and exact key/write sets. Clean sources read Head/Policy/Semantic/Monitor/Run and write only Monitor/Semantic; no-clean reads only Head/Policy/Semantic. Script guards keep this diagnostic extra/raw-only/JSON, excluded from generation, merge and bulk use. No production helper, price port, state or API was added.

Raw-only extra `behavior_policy_cleanup(c)` records only the shared optional read/delete/drop fragments; it contains no transaction or ordinary head/tail/financial destruction. Its five categorical classes are absent (`0`), CurrentOutcome/no monitor (`1`), CurrentOutcome/zero-counter clean (`2`), Accumulated/no clean (`3`) and maximum four-by-four/full monitor (`4`). The first four are Idle component sources. The maximum reuses the temporal Running source with a real Transfer/failure prefix. Existing source-snapshot staging binds the optional records, including the pre-authored zero-clean expression, without public policy admission or a new authority mechanism.

`policy_cleanup_profile` native tests exercise verification on/off, metadata exclusion without extras and the exact selector range. A recorder and overlay delta check only optional keys for all five sources; matching-depth whole-root oracles prove preservation and outer rollback, while missing policy refuses before writes. Setup, policy binding, prefix execution, root scans and oracle transactions stay outside recording. Codec ceilings are fixture assertions, not physical maxima; neither this profile nor the broader `contract_optional_cleanup` qualifies the additional Close price or complete destruction, and their timings/proofs must not be subtracted.

These are implementation partitions, not independent generated coefficients. Adding recorded trie sizes is not a certified physical-proof model; adding separately justified proof upper bounds can conservatively bound their union without subtracting overlap. Service, Deadline, Parked and Disabled are alternative residences; Parked has no Running/Suspended Run. A temporal Trigger can coexist with Block residence/review authority. A fixture that fabricates Parked plus a Run cannot establish a reachable maximum.

Internal terminal entry belongs to its enclosing effect owner. One Transfer can synchronously finalize its certified expired recipient, including complete detachment and late refusal rollback. Multi-recipient profiles are removed; the reference integration retains their historical observations separately from current qualification.

The reference runtime selects one `MaxParkedBalanceWatches`, independently of twelve Contract Steps and sixteen allowlist entries. The dedicated bound owns activation/episode vectors, complete dependency plans, scan capacity and FRAME current-population metadata. `reference_parked_watch_bound_is_one_and_two_watch_encoding_is_rejected` pins one-watch construction, rejects two-watch construction/decoding and checks the reference storage ceilings. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#current-distribution-and-evidence-domain) owns current native custody/Close and distribution evidence. Earlier four-/twelve-watch observations retain their exact historical domains and do not qualify current physical maxima or generated binding.

`close_load_wide_idle` supplies the missing public no-policy wide-Idle loading input for both User and System. Ordinary creation admits twelve unthinned RemoveLiquidity Steps with maximum canonical Observation predicate clauses, full AddressEvent/funding allowlists, a window and host-configured balance-watch rules. Creation atomically arms a host-bounded balance episode, so Idle has Parked residence and no Run; it is not an unarmed Service fixture. Setup checks the family-compatible header width and current geometry; it does not maximize compact resource envelopes. Measurement invokes public Close authority loading plus finalization reload, without mutation. Root comparison and complete Close occur only in verification, outside measurement. Its class selector is categorical and raw-only; it does not qualify class dominance, optional-policy admission or complete physical Close.

`prepare_temporal_close` now retains a wide unvisited suffix in its existing shapes 2–5: Idle Cadenced, Idle AtTime, Running Cadenced and Suspended Cadenced. The first two Steps preserve the real committed Transfer and early return or temporary Transfer refusal; each remaining Step has a maximum-width RemoveLiquidity task and canonical Observation predicates from `maximum_temporal_control_steps`. Its asset comes from the wide header fixture, since the mock native asset has a shorter encoding. Ordinary admission remains effective, and a host that would thin any unvisited Step refuses this diagnostic.

Under the reference twelve-Step bound, ten wide Steps and all three tail chunks survive until Close. Loading, detachment, finalization and late-hold refusal profiles share this constructor; neither record-width coverage nor successful raw execution certifies maximum resource envelopes, physical Weight or tariffs. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) owns current collection identities.

`prepare_retry_close_header_with_currency_peer` applies the same unthinned suffix requirement to Manual and AddressEvent shapes 0–1. Step 0 remains the funded AddLiquidity attempt whose output bound causes a real temporary failure; each of the eleven unvisited Steps is a wide RemoveLiquidity with canonical Observation predicates. Ordinary admission refuses suffix thinning. The full family-compatible header, balance-activation rules, suspended Block residence, custody and optional shared native hold are retained. Step 0 in this reachable retry shape is not the widest Task; the independently admitted wide Idle/Close case attains its complete Step and no-policy resource codec ceilings. That encoding evidence does not replace the reached phase/profile or physical cost owners. Public Close and Dormant/shared-hold preparation also consume this constructor; deactivation removes the wide body before Dormant measurement. The reference integration owns collection/codec identity and temporal evidence.

`contract_optional_cleanup` invokes the actual loaded Contract-removal owner through a benchmark-only seam. Its categorical cases are ordinary (`0`), policy/monitor (`1`), financial companion (`2`) and both (`3`). Each retains all twelve wide Steps: maximum-width RemoveLiquidity tasks, four singleton clauses of maximum-width Observation predicates and RetryLater. The header uses full AddressEvent/funding allowlists, window and nonce; only the forbidden ParkedBalance payload is absent. Assertions refuse thinned tails. Geometry carries a synthetic no-policy System certificate, not public optional-policy admission or maximum resource envelopes.

Policy cases require four-by-four bounds and use all ten metrics, full-width policy/monitor records and clean-Cycle state; the combined financial record reaches its codec ceiling. No semantic Actor or process is published. Removal receives the loaded Contract; it does not load the stored header, tails or financial record. Authentication, loading, residence/Trigger detachment, Run cancellation, currency hold, events and enclosing transaction work remain separate. The script excludes this categorical diagnostic from generation and refuses regression or candidate merge. Raw samples do not certify complete Close or production coefficients.

`financial_wait_record_binding` isolates the maximum-width financial companion read, original episode/origin/debt/clock binding and expired-insolvency classification. It installs one optional record without an Actor, Contract head or process; the expected source, time and spendability are caller-provided inputs. Canonical source authentication, current timestamp/currency reads, full body loading, admission, fees, publication and destruction remain separate. Setup establishes FRAME's minimum block number before taking the verification root; verification preserves the complete root and record. The native fixture checks zero/nonzero initial blocks with verification on/off. The extra requires raw-only samples and is excluded from generation and candidate merge; its fixed storage/codec point does not certify complete financial inspection or physical maxima.

Financial repark is not a pure topology-only publication owner. `repark_financial_service` plans the original expiry destination, calls `remove_service_member`, replaces process residence and inserts Deadline membership inside the owning transaction. Removing an AwaitingFunding Service process calls `process_has_service_authority` and `financial_wait_matches_process`: it reads the canonical semantic record, financial episode, Contract head and Run (including absence), re-authenticates the original source/clock and liability, and for Running/Suspended loads the current Step under current authority or complete retained geometry under stale authority.

These checks remain inside the publication suffix even when the outer caller already loaded the episode. The native `financial_wait_runtime_repark_reauthenticates_source_inside_atomic_publication` owner records only the actual suffix over all four unpaid sources with commit/rollback, outside setup and verification; it preserves semantic/head/Run/episode, fees and events, with a complete-root rollback guard.

`financial_wait_runtime_stale_repark_authenticates_complete_retained_body` adds configured-length twelve-Step Running/Suspended sources with an actual paid prefix and all three retained tail chunks. After a funded return, current authority reads only the cursor-bearing chunk; stale authority authenticates all three chunks against the original certificate. A recorder encloses only repark and its transaction. Commit retains original debt, episode, Run, custody, fee/backing traces and events while restoring the original expiry; explicit rollback restores the whole root. A missing final cold chunk under stale authority returns a Service-source error and preserves the corrupted input root; repair stays outside measurement and precedes try-state. These are native model source/integrity witnesses, not maximum-width header/policy/destination, public admission, reference Wasm or physical tariff evidence.

Its singleton/fresh-destination prepared sources use mock financial authority/backing; public admission, maximum headers/tails/policy/destination geometry, failures and physical prices remain separate. A raw fixture with no canonical Actor cannot qualify this AwaitingFunding branch; ordinary Serving ring samples omit its authentication and cannot substitute for it. Test and benchmark forwarding methods are separately gated; the production suffix is unchanged.

`financial_service_repark_unqualified(c)` measures the actual rejected suffix for configured-length Running (`0`) and Suspended (`1`) sources when either financial entry or additional-close resource port is unavailable. It reuses ordinary temporal preparation with a paid Transfer prefix, real temporary failure and every unvisited wide Step. Setup removes the independent Trigger Deadline, stages a valid but deliberately different semantic certificate, rebinds the complete body and Run, and installs a no-policy wait episode. Existing quotes are retained component inputs, not financial backing or evidence of public waiting history.

The stale source loads every retained tail before `reconstruct_contract_geometry` refuses unqualified AwaitFunding geometry; successful full-body authentication and expiry placement are not reached. Measurement includes repark and the enclosing rollback. Verification preserves the whole root, Service, semantic/head/tails/Run/episode, custody, Fee Sink and events; verification roots and setup stay outside the timer. Native fixtures cover verification on/off, then enable model resource ports and repark the same source successfully under whole-root rollback. Qualified hosts skip this extra. It requires raw-only samples and is excluded from generation and candidate merge. Fresh-destination/no-policy refusal is a separate cost domain from successful repark, public admission, populated placement, policy/header maxima and certified tariffs.

`admission_certificate_authority` isolates one call to the configured host's `AdmissionCertificateAuthority::current`, retaining its result through `black_box`. Expected authority is constructed outside measurement; an unavailable provider stops the profile. No Actor body, certificate validation, admission, Task, detachment or destruction is invoked. `admission_authority_profile_measures_one_call_without_actor_state` checks setup plus measured call counts and unchanged whole storage roots with verification enabled and disabled. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#admission-certificate-authority) owns concrete provider composition and storage independence. This raw-only extra remains excluded from generation and candidate merge: it measures construction CPU, not the validity of referenced Weight values, caller multiplicity or complete Close. Changed host configuration or generated bindings require fresh measurement.

`scheduler.rs::close_cleanup_weight_upper` currently returns only `WeightInfo::close_actor()`. The nearby APIs are not certified cleanup components: `service_member_retire_*` covers ring retirement alone, `contract_geometry_close` removes body storage, `run_cancel` preserves the Actor, and `return_due_block_deadline_to_service*` removes a due source while publishing Service. `process_pending_parked_balance_event` measures positive wake, not terminal destruction. Their sum is not an established close bound, and `WeightInfo` has no dedicated pure Trigger/Deadline removal or complete Parked-release owner. This is a missing cost contract, not permission to infer a coefficient by subtraction.

`financial_wait_runtime_joint_optional_records_attain_codec_ceilings_before_close` reaches all optional-record ceilings in one prepared User with four-by-four policy bounds and `u128` balance: cold policy 177 bytes, monitor 116, financial wait 331 (624 combined). The sixteen-atom expression references all ten metrics. Ordinary canonical Steps commit one temporary Action failure; its unpaid retry enters SuspendedAction waiting without another observation. Small counters already occupy every fixed-width slot, so high counter values or long outcome history are unnecessary for this byte corner. Clean flags and the behavior-bound financial scope are authenticated by the read-only API, and the installed hold remains unchanged from before the Cycle because clean capacity and financial record capacity were reserved.

The fixture retains a configured-length Contract: a real retry Swap followed by widest remaining Task operands and bounded predicates. The funding allowlist and authored terminal window remain separate header owners. Earlier exact head/tail bytes belong to the previous contract and do not bound this tree.

A deliberately removed currency hold makes owner Close refuse with the whole root, head, all tails and all three optional records preserved. Restoring that backing allows common Close to remove policy, monitor, wait, Run, process, expiry and hold, while native and task-asset custody remain unchanged. Native try-runtime and runtime-benchmarks/try-runtime modes both pass. This is a legal prepared semantic/codec and state-hold corner under model prices, not public creation/reference-adapter conformance, an aggregate trie/RefTime maximum, sufficient destruction tariffs or production artifact acceptance. It extends the optional `F` record domain without replacing the existing `D/L/T/S/B/P/F` physical partition.

The canonical call graph requires coverage equivalent to `max(D, L + T + max(S, B, P, 0) + F)`, with componentwise Weight maximum and checked or saturating addition. `D` covers complete public Dormant close for both classes; `L` covers Active wrapper/loading, detachment revalidation and its publication epilogue; `T` covers independent Tick removal; `S/B/P` cover Service, primary Deadline and Parked release. `F` covers loaded Active finalization, including optional Run cancellation, body/class destruction, hold reconciliation and events, and must bound both classes and legal record shapes.

Here `B` includes Block retry and Tick financial expiry: both use `DeadlineHandles`, while the independent temporal Trigger uses `TriggerDeadlineHandles`. AwaitFunding disarms that independent Trigger and may tentatively return to Service after a funded hint. Its companion authentication belongs to the encountered loading/removal owner, and companion deletion belongs to `F`. Summing independent maxima is deliberately conservative, not a claim that Parked plus Run or waiting plus an armed Trigger is reachable. This is a coverage obligation derived from the implementation, not an implemented Weight formula or permission to add existing unrelated methods.

A complete qualification must include transaction setup, committed-path merge and failed-path rollback work. Pure-removal profiles do not cover certificate rebuilding, process deletion or the final semantic write in `detach_actor_publication_inner`. `cancel_run_internal_loaded` skips successor publication and hold reconciliation for `Closing`, but retains Run removal and cancellation/summary events; ordinary `run_cancel` cannot be substituted by subtraction. Destruction itself reconciles the hold. A failed outer Close restores its transaction-local prefix, not Task effects from earlier committed Steps.

The pinned FRAME call macro (`frame-support-procedural` 40.0.0, `pallet/expand/call.rs`) wraps public dispatchables in `with_storage_layer`, including direct native calls to `close_actor`. `with_control_transaction` reuses that layer for Dormant Close. Canonical Active Close additionally opens `remove_actor_publication_and_finalize` and `finalize_actor_loaded_inner` transactions. The following counts cover those entry owners, excluding enclosing callers and host callbacks; they are not timing or proof bounds.

| Entry | Owned transaction layers |
| --- | --- |
| Public Dormant Close | 1: FRAME dispatch layer; control helper reuses it |
| Public canonical Active Close | 3: FRAME dispatch, publication removal, loaded finalization |
| Internal canonical publication removal plus finalization | 2; the caller's Step/effect transaction is additional |
| Already consumed Active finalization | 1; caller detachment and any enclosing transaction are separate |

The pinned `frame-support` 48.0.0 transaction owner reads/increments `:transaction_level:` before starting the host transaction, commits or rolls back into its parent, then restores the level through its guard. The increment and guard restoration therefore write in the parent context even when the started transaction rolls back. `OverlayedMap::close_transaction` directly traverses the popped current-layer dirty set, not all earlier parent keys. Source loading extras use a measured block around read-only helpers, so they do not include the generated public dispatch layer. Coverage of `L` must include that wrapper and its merge/rollback work; `F` includes the loaded finalizer's own layer. Do not infer these costs from the number of layers or an empty-transaction sample.

Local traversal does not make transaction work independent of the caller. The native SDK uses `HashSet` dirty sets: commit inserts current keys into the parent's set, whose capacity can grow, while entry lookup uses the shared overlay `BTreeMap`. Committed provenance uses `Extrinsics(Vec<u32>)::extend` when collection is enabled. Host transaction start/commit/rollback also visits every existing child changeset and the offchain changeset; `Ext` then delegates to every registered extension's transaction callback. These owners require a qualified host context, not just a Close-local write count. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#benchmark-host) retains the source inventory and a native collection-growth observation without claiming reference reachability or physical bounds.

| Transaction owner | Required context input |
| --- | --- |
| Current-layer traversal and entry mutation | Dirty-set capacity, distinct keys, key/value widths and shared overlay population |
| Parent merge and provenance | Parent dirty-set capacity; enabled extrinsic collection and parent/child provenance lengths |
| Host transaction coordination | Existing child-overlay count, offchain changes, nesting depth and extension callback domain |

For a non-genesis block with available event-count capacity, loaded destruction emits the following Actor events. The currency provider's events and any Step/effect receipts emitted before entry remain separate host/caller owners.

| Finalization branch | Actor event suffix |
| --- | --- |
| Dormant or Active without Run, successful | `ActorClosed` |
| Active with Run, successful | `CycleCancelled`, `CycleSummary(Cancelled)`, `ActorClosed` |
| Active with Run, late currency refusal | Provisional `CycleCancelled`, `CycleSummary(Cancelled)`, then rollback |
| Without Run, late currency refusal | No Actor event before rollback |

These are fixed Actor payload shapes: cancellation retains the existing nonce and Close reason, and summary retains six scalar outcome totals without loading Task history. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) owns native currency events and its conditional two-key proof input; last-hold release can add `Endowed` before `Released` without deleting the System account. Generated pallet event deposition supplies no topics. `frame-system` 48.0.0 reads block number, execution phase and event count, increments the count and appends an `EventRecord` to System Events. These SDK-whitelisted owners still incur encoding, append and transaction work. No Actor runtime path enumerates the unbounded event vector. Host event materialization and overlay work need complete physical qualification.

With at most five destruction records, the `u32` compact count crosses at most one of 64, 16,384 or 1,073,741,824. Its largest width increase is two bytes, so a valid encoded event vector grows by at most 208 bytes including the 206-byte record suffix. An unmaterialized carried Append buffer instead inserts its complete first count prefix, up to five bytes, giving a separate 211-byte added-data bound. These are logical/source byte inputs; prefix length, capacity, shifting, allocation and full root/transaction RefTime remain open.

The pinned `sp-state-machine` 0.53.0 host distinguishes event-buffer authority from transaction depth. `Ext::storage_append` loads a backend value only when the overlay has no entry. On the first write in a transaction, `OverlayedValue::append` starts from an empty buffer for `Remove`, clones an existing `Set` buffer, or moves an existing `Append` buffer while recording its parent length. Consecutive append commits retain that buffer; rollback restores it and truncates the provisional suffix. Counting transaction layers therefore does not justify one complete event-prefix copy per layer. `frame-executive` 48.0.0 resets prior-block Events before runtime-upgrade hooks and initialization; ordinary event publication then uses append authority. Raw replacement or an embedding caller that omits reset does not inherit that path.

`close_dormant_committed_root_proof_preserves_event_prefix_without_old_payload` performs public User and System Dormant Close after that reset and a 63-event native prefix. Recording includes the host `storage_root` call. The witness excludes the previous block's external Events payload, preserves the current prefix across the compact-count boundary, and reconstructs the exact post-root from all net changes using only the recorded proof. State snapshots and assertions run outside recording. This covers a native reset/prefix/Close fragment, not a complete Executive block, maximum proof or RefTime. Buffer growth and final materialization remain host costs: `StorageAppend::replace_length` splices the compact count, so a wider count can move the retained suffix even when transaction entry avoids cloning it.

The reference integration additionally imports a complete native block with a legal signed event prefix before User Dormant Close on both presets; [the integration evidence](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md) owns that runtime composition. It verifies prefix retention through finalization/import without supplying a maximum preceding-prefix, buffer-capacity or physical-cost bound.

The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) qualifies a shared conditional key-proof input for the four System event/context values and FRAME transaction depth. Native exact hold release now also retains the reset/63-event prefix and reconstructs its committed root without the previous external payload. Scalar/key proofs are shared across emitted events and layers; encoding, buffer growth/materialization and overlay merge/rollback CPU remain separate. The same reference owner now supplies the exact currency release call/loop inventory separately from its two-key proof union; repeated AccountData mutation and Holds processing retain CPU ownership. This reference input does not replace full event/transaction Weight or qualify an embedding host that omits reset or replaces the append buffer.

The same reference owner pins fixed destruction-record shapes, class/Run suffix byte bounds and logical vector growth; these do not cap the preceding event buffer. The native Rust 1.94.1 and associated runner Rust 1.93.0 sources qualify a combined append/materialization argument. Let `P` and `C >= P` be the carried Append buffer's initial byte length and capacity. One qualified Close suffix adds at most 206 record bytes and at most five count-prefix bytes, so every required length is at most `P + 211`. If `P >= 211`, successful doubling on the first growth gives `2C >= P + 211`; no second growth is needed for that suffix and its count materialization. If growth occurs, its old capacity is below `P + 211`, and the selected new logical capacity is below `2(P + 211)`.

For smaller initial buffers, the same successful `grow_amortized` recurrence selects at least `max(2C, required_length, 8)` for bytes. Initial `P` intervals `0..=7`, `8..=14`, `15..=30`, `31..=70` and `71..=210` require at most six, five, four, three and two growths respectively. In the first interval, one growth reaches eight and six reach at least 256, exceeding `P + 211`. For the other intervals, `2^k P >= P + 211` at the lower endpoint proves the stated bound. Every growth still selects capacity below `2(P + 211)`, since its old capacity is below the required length and the eight-byte minimum is smaller than the suffix bound.

These conditional inequalities concern one moved Append vector with the qualified suffix/count insertion and no intervening shrink or replacement. They do not cover a Set clone, unrelated producers, materialization across rollback/replacement or a global buffer limit. An existing capacity that needs no growth has no upper from this argument. The [reference benchmark host](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#benchmark-host) owns exact runner source identities; other hosts require separate qualification.

The locked native host's production `OverlayedEntry::value` reaches `StorageEntry::materialize_in_place` through `as_option`. It repairs the compact count in the carried vector and caches the current count; further requests without an intervening count change perform no repair. `OverlayedChanges::storage_root` uses this mutable path before `backend.full_storage_root`.

`Ext::next_storage_key` also calls `value` on visited overlay entries to check their presence, so an indirect enumeration can materialize Events even without a direct event read. Consecutive Append commit moves the buffer without repairing its count; rollback forwards the child's encoded count, adjusts the parent byte boundary and truncates the suffix, leaving any count correction for a later request. The stale-value cloning helper `materialize(&self)` is `cfg(test)`, not this production path. A storage read still copies returned bytes, and hashing/root construction retain their own costs; cache reuse does not remove those owners. The reference integration pins these SDK sources and a native public Close/cache/rollback witness separately from runner and production-node acceptance.

For one carried Append entry without rollback, Set/Remove replacement or unrelated appends, the at-most-five destruction records create at most five stale-count intervals. Even if the host requests materialization after every record, there are at most five count repairs from an initially matching cache, or six including an initially stale cache repaired before the first record. Within five monotone count increments there is at most one compact-width widening, since adjacent `u32` width thresholds are more than five counts apart; an initially absent count has its separate prefix insertion. This narrows repeated count repair only in that conditional suffix. Rollback, intervening producers, total host calls, preceding bytes/capacity, return copying, hashing, root/allocator costs and physical RefTime remain separate gates.

`StorageAppend::replace_length` supplies an owned count-byte vector to `Vec::splice`, whose iterator has an exact remaining length. With a nonempty tail, widening the count performs one `move_tail`: it reserves for the complete new length and shifts the retained tail once. The exact iterator leaves no second collected suffix to move. Equal-width replacement does not shift the tail; shrinking a prefix during restoration shifts it left through `Drain::drop`. A cached count already matching the current length skips materialization. These source cases separate tail movement from capacity growth; they do not bound the number of host materializations, preceding length/capacity, allocator work, physical memory or complete root/transaction RefTime.

That reference owner also qualifies FRAME's raw pre-measure commit and whitelist priming. Pure Close raw fixtures therefore begin with backend-loaded setup Events, rather than the ordinary reset/current-block Append authority. Their proof delta excludes the primed Events proof, and post-measure commit work is outside both reported timers. Native `test_instance` boundary checks and reset/prefix root witnesses retain their respective semantic/proof scope; neither certifies the raw event-buffer path or its complete CPU cost. This measurement distinction requires complete event/transaction ownership before physical composition, without inventing a coefficient from either path.

`close_host_authority_calls_belong_to_detachment` now checks exact successful suffixes, retained preceding block events, phase, empty topics and count across its existing User/System public/consumed and Dormant fixtures. `close_destruction_late_hold_failure_restores_public_and_consumed_state` checks a failed inner Close preserves the caller's earlier write and event prefix, then verifies an enclosing rollback restores successful destruction, its currency release and all events before a committed retry. Both cover actual native currency, custody and state authority; they do not certify event/overlay RefTime or trie-proof bounds. Test-only event-vector reads are verification, never measured or production execution.

The extras `close_load_user` and `close_load_system` invoke the same `load_close_authority` and `load_finalization_authority` functions as public Close, without duplicating their checks in benchmark code. They include the public load, Signed/Root and mutability checks, second full load, instance comparison, legacy-absence checks and canonical generation lookup. `CheckedCloseAuthorityOf` is a crate-private read result, not storage, public ABI or an additional Actor class. The extraction preserves error order and leaves mutation in the existing transactional owners.

Storage-root equality verifies both loading profiles are read-only; full Close occurs only in verification. Native tests retain process/Deadline/Run/semantic authority with verification disabled and cover Active/Dormant classification, wrong-owner/User-Root rejection, stale-instance refusal and missing/corrupt state before origin failure. Existing immutable-close and mandatory-terminal regressions remain part of the full Actors suite. These loading witnesses do not certify every admitted body/Run width, residence-validation geometry or finalizer cost; internal terminal callers that already hold authority are not charged by this measurement automatically.

The extras `close_finalize_user` and `close_finalize_system` isolate `F` through `finalize_actor_from_consumed_state`. The retry shapes use `prepare_retry_close_header` for an admitted body, wide Manual/AddressEvent header and suspended Run through ordinary creation and a real temporary attempt; temporal shapes add completed, Running and Suspended alternatives. The Manual wrapper `prepare_full_header_retry_close` also supplies the complete public `close_actor` witness. `detach_close_destruction` loads full authority and calls ordinary publication detachment outside measurement, checking byte-identical optional Run state. Only loaded finalization is timed; no Service, Deadline or Parked removal is included. The System case does not borrow User slot/hold costs or assume class dominance.

The extras `close_dormant_user` and `close_dormant_system` isolate `D` through complete public Close, including authority loading and Signed/Root checks. Setup ordinarily deactivates the same funded retry Actor; cancellation and Active body removal stay outside measurement. Shared verification checks identity/body/Run absence, exact counters, retained peer slot bitmap, User hold release or System locator vacancy, unchanged custody/Fee Sink and global try-state. Native tests execute all four profiles with verification enabled and disabled; the measured call itself completes destruction. These witnesses are not a maximum over every legal header/Run shape or complete Close.

The raw-only `close_dormant_event_root` instead includes reset, 63 ordinary remark calls with Signed origin, prefix materialization through `storage_root`, public Dormant Close and its resulting `storage_root` in one measured block. Its categorical class selects last-slot User or System authority; no class dominance is assumed. First-root materialization fixes count 63, and the Close suffix crosses the one-byte compact-count boundary on the second root. Verification preserves all remark records, count/phase/topics and terminal event, checks root stability and performs the shared destruction/custody checks. The native fixture checks both classes with verification enabled and disabled. This is a whole-fragment diagnostic, not pure `D`: prefix production and the first root have their own costs, native setup overlay differs from the raw pre-commit boundary, and backend commit stays outside measurement. The fixed prefix does not qualify every block buffer or complete RefTime.

The extra `close_public_user_late_hold(a, r)` measures complete failed public Close across the same six Active shapes and owner-slot branches; `close_dormant_user_late_hold(r)` owns the separate Dormant path. Setup retains a real currency Holds entry exactly one unit below the target's required release. With peer holds, it removes the peer aggregate plus one unit without changing peer records; last-slot setup removes one unit. This deliberately inconsistent host ledger permits canonical loading and causes actual `StateHoldInvariant` after the Run/body/count/slot prefix. Root, custody, committed credit and Fee Sink checks precede unmeasured hold repair and ordinary successful Close. It is a fault witness, not admitted malformed state or a maximum over unrelated hold reasons and currency/account configurations.

The optional extra `close_actor_user_shared_hold(r)` and `close_dormant_user_shared_hold(r)` reuse the Manual retry and ordinarily deactivated Dormant fixtures with both owner-slot branches. A public host producer installs an unrelated native hold before the Actor reason, making Actor lookup traverse the preceding entry. Last-slot Close removes only the Actor reason and retains the currency key; retained-peer Close keeps both reasons. Setup and producer-preservation checks remain outside the complete public Close measurement. Unsupported producer domains explicitly skip; host errors stop. These two shapes add a concrete currency branch, not coverage of all six Active shapes, Parked residences, failures, host producers or whole-caller maxima. Concrete producer reachability and the refreshed four-case raw collection belong to the runtime integration document.

Current-source Wasm passes both owner-slot corners for Active and Dormant, with complete public Close and unrelated-producer preservation. These full-call observations must not be subtracted into pure currency coefficients; source call/loop bounds and complete host physical costs retain separate authority.

Current-source raw Wasm now also passes the existing Manual-retry and Dormant late-hold refusal profiles for both owner-slot branches. The runtime integration owner retains complete-call timings, committed-write counts, exact source/artifact identity and duplicate fixed-component sampling. Zero committed writes do not erase provisional events/destruction or measured nested rollback. These four fixed faults do not establish a maximum or replace internal-terminal, ordinary event-buffer and complete host qualification.

`close_public_late_hold_profiles_preserve_failed_authority_and_measurement_boundary` executes all twelve Active and both Dormant combinations with SDK verification enabled/disabled. Disabled verification leaves the failed Actor, body/Run/process authority and nonzero currency hold; enabled verification checks exact-root rollback and performs repair/destruction afterward. The maximum AddressEvent retry shape now has this late-currency witness. Measured Active calls include public loading, detachment and nested finalization rollback; Dormant has no body/carrier/certificate work. Repaired success and its terminal event are outside timing, so zero committed writes cannot imply no provisional cancellation/event/transaction work. These are complete failure-call observations, not pure component coefficients or final aggregate bounds.

For successful public Dormant Close, the following package-source inventory starts at `load_close_authority` and follows `close_inactive_actor`, `mutate_actor_semantic_state`, class cleanup and `reconcile_actor_state_hold_with_authority`. Counts are getter/contains/mutation calls, not unique DB operations, trie nodes or coefficients. Origin policy, currency internals, FRAME event handling and transaction machinery are excluded.

| Dormant stage | User reads/writes | System reads/writes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Classification and absence checks | 7 / 0 | 7 / 0 |
| Public machine-freeze guard | 1 / 0 | 1 / 0 |
| Identity, count, sovereign and class preflight | 4 / 0 | 4 / 0 |
| Generation lookup and expected-semantic removal | 2 / 1 | 2 / 1 |
| Dormant identity deletion and identity-count update | 1 / 2 | 1 / 2 |
| Owner bitmap/sovereign unlink or System locator vacancy | 1 / 2 | 0 / 2 |
| Post-removal hold reconciliation, excluding currency | 4 / 1 | 4 / 0 |
| Package total | 20 / 6 | 19 / 5 |

Both classes touch at most twelve package keys. Common owners are semantic state, dormant identity, identity count, sovereign reverse index, hold record and `GlobalCircuitBreaker`, plus five absence probes: Contract head, Run, Process, control locator and unsignaled control cell. The class-specific twelfth key is the User owner bitmap or System sovereign locator. Valid User state starts with its hold record present; valid System adds an absence witness for that record. The breaker may independently be absent under `ValueQuery`. Semantic absence after removal observes a transaction-local deletion, not another pre-state proof. The native Dormant root witness records this exact twelve-key package set before root computation; no Contract tails, Service/Deadline carrier or custody asset namespace is enumerated.

`remove_owner_slot_binding` reads one fixed bitmap and either writes the retained peers or deletes the empty key. The System path writes `Vacant`, not locator deletion. After semantic removal, hold reconciliation makes four package reads; a User's record selects exact currency release and record deletion, whereas a valid hold-exempt System returns without invoking currency. These branch counts do not bound the host currency implementation or prove one branch cheaper than another. `with_control_transaction` reuses an existing transaction or opens one; caller/FRAME transaction and event costs remain separate owners.

`close_destruction_late_hold_failure_restores_public_and_consumed_state` includes ordinarily deactivated Dormant User Actors with retained peers and last-slot cleanup. A forced late currency-release refusal restores the exact pre-call storage root, and repaired retries complete with exact counters, slots, holds, custody and Fee Sink checks. Both failed and successful Dormant attempts invoke admission authority zero times. This extends rollback coverage without fabricating Dormant Contract/Run state or measuring setup deactivation.

For canonical consumed Active finalization (`F`), publication and both wakeup pointers are already detached. Let `m = ceil(max(n - 1, 0) / 4)` and `r = 1` when the supplied authority retains a Run, otherwise zero; this `r` is not the owner-slot benchmark selector. The following successful-path inventory includes `finalize_actor_from_consumed_state` but excludes caller detachment, host configuration providers, currency, FRAME events and transaction machinery; it is not a bound for legacy retained-frame entry.

| Consumed Active stage | User reads/writes | System reads/writes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Entry legacy-marker checks | 2 / 0 | 2 / 0 |
| Counts, sovereign and class preflight | 4 / 0 | 4 / 0 |
| Independent Tick absence probe | 1 / 0 | 1 / 0 |
| Closing Run cancellation | 3r / r | 3r / r |
| Primary-marker check, body reload/removal, unconditional Run deletion | 2 + m / 2 + m | 2 + m / 2 + m |
| Semantic lookup/expected removal and both count updates | 4 / 3 | 4 / 3 |
| Class cleanup | 1 / 2 | 0 / 2 |
| Post-removal hold reconciliation | 4 / 1 | 4 / 0 |
| Package-source total | 18 + m + 3r / 8 + m + r | 17 + m + 3r / 7 + m + r |

`F` touches at most `11 + m` package keys: two legacy markers, two counts, sovereign reverse index, one class key, independent Tick handle, Contract head and `m` tails, Run, semantic state and hold record. Run deletion occurs twice when `r=1`, but that is one key; absence probes and post-deletion reads must likewise not be counted as independent pre-state proofs. `cancel_run_internal_loaded` skips successor publication for Closing, performs no Step-resource lookup and emits two cancellation/summary events only for a retained Run. Finalization additionally emits `ActorClosed`. These source-event counts are not the FRAME event implementation's I/O cost.

Coherent committed package state admits a read/net-root proof bound for these key inventories under H256 `LayoutV1`, package keys no wider than 81 bytes and foreign keys no wider than 712. For each fixed 32-byte namespace, let `g` bound matching global nodes; the generic ceiling is 65. Each ordinary point key of length `l` adds at most `2(l - 32)` matching nodes and one divergent node. Existing matching nodes cost at most 650 SCALE bytes; a divergent foreign node costs at most 1,284. Each net point deletion adds at most one collapse sibling by the pinned repair argument in [Storage Topology](#storage-topology). Insertion and replacement preserve foreign value/child references and need no additional off-path descent. External values read by the operation add their complete bytes and SCALE length prefix; foreign payloads are not fetched solely for root repair.

Contract tails refine the point model. The actual two Blake2_128Concat hashers and `u64/u32` fields give every Actor a complete 56-byte tail prefix, with 76-byte keys. Its `t` retained chunks form a subtree with at most `2t - 1` nodes; loading and removing all chunks needs 48 source ancestors, that complete subtree and at most one outside collapse sibling. Repeated body loads add no pre-state proof bytes. With `G` summing each namespace's `g` once, `A` summing ordinary point-key suffix nodes, `q/d` counting ordinary queried/deleted keys and `V` bounding encoded external payloads, the union is `(G + A + [t>0](48 + 2t - 1))*650 + (q + d + [t>0])*1284 + V + 5`. This applies to coherent complete body prefixes; arbitrary orphan tails do not inherit it.

The [reference key/namespace and codec guard](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) supplies the following conditional inputs at twelve Steps / three tails. `M` is the matching-node sum, and `X` is the divergent/collapse allowance. Whole-value maxima are deliberately conservative, including class-wide identity and complete head/chunk type bounds. Optional User `F` includes policy, monitor and financial companions; optional System `F` includes only policy/monitor because System AwaitFunding is forbidden. Prepared optional state remains physically gated.

| Package branch | `M` | `X` | Encoded payload ceiling `V` | Encoded read/net-root bound |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| User `D` | 640 | 17 | 299 | 438,132 |
| System `D` | 593 | 15 | 185 | 404,900 |
| User `F` | 645 | 18 | 13,116 | 455,483 |
| System `F` | 598 | 16 | 13,002 | 422,251 |
| User `F`, optional companions | 805 | 24 | 13,746 | 567,817 |
| System `F`, optional companions | 705 | 20 | 13,299 | 497,234 |

These are package proof-union bounds under the declared foreign-key, namespace, typed-value and complete-tail contracts. They exclude host configuration/callbacks, currency, FRAME events/transaction bookkeeping, loading/detachment and all residence owners outside `F`; they are not complete `D/F` Weight or public Close maxima. The linked reference integration qualifies ordinary key producers from its named fresh presets; other host/raw-state domains and complete RefTime remain separate obligations. Package value bytes, read/net-root witnesses and full host commit work retain separate owners; no generated binding, budget increase or public policy/wait enablement follows from this table.

The same point-proof argument bounds the package portion of `L`. Its common inventory is twelve namespaces/point keys plus complete body tails: semantic, control locator, dormant-identity absence, head, unsignaled-cell absence, process, own Service node, primary Deadline handle, Pending owner, Run, independent Trigger handle and the public breaker. A primary Deadline adds its one validation page. Parked validation adds timed review, episode and at most one Deadline page; its inner review fallback is not read when the outer timed review is present. Optional policy/monitor authentication rereads existing semantic/Run owners, adding only the two companions. Financial authentication adds its episode, using the existing head/Run and at most one current-Step tail already covered by the complete body prefix. Source provider and fee-conversion work remain host obligations.

`L` deletes one process key and replaces the semantic value after residence removal; it does not delete body tails. The tail repair term therefore applies only when that owner actually deletes the complete prefix. For read-only complete tails it is zero. With `z` indicating complete-tail deletion, the point union generalizes to `(G + A + [t>0](48 + 2t - 1))*650 + (q + d + z)*1284 + V + 5`, where `d` counts only ordinary point deletions and `z = 0` for `L` and `S`. This separates a bounded read prefix from a removed prefix without charging unrelated recorded samples.

Pure `S` removal has seven package point keys: both legacy-marker absences, process, ring header and at most three Service nodes (target and two distinct neighbors). It deletes only the target node; the header and retained neighbors are replacements. Singleton and two-member rings are subsets of this inventory. AwaitingFunding adds semantic, financial episode, head, Run, independent Trigger absence and optional policy/monitor; the proof upper bound conservatively authenticates all coherent tails even though the financial source reads at most one current-Step tail. It does not claim complete body decoding by Service removal.

`service_removal_committed_root_proof_has_exact_three_node_inventory` creates and ordinarily triggers three User or System Manual Actors, commits setup and records only transaction-wrapped Service removal plus host root calculation. It checks the exact seven-key package read set before root calculation, the four-key net change (target, neighbors, header), and exact post-root reconstruction from the recorded proof. Process/body/class authority is outside this pure removal boundary; the reconstructed root excludes mutation of any other owner. This is a native component witness, not a complete Close, waiting witness, maximum host proof or RefTime measurement.

The current native input guard supplies these additional package proof ceilings. Payloads conservatively charge full typed values, including some absent or mutually exclusive records. The policy/monitor superset adds companions to the largest nonfinancial `L` inventory; it bounds the union of alternatives without declaring that every field is simultaneously reachable. Waiting rows are User-only and do not include Parked residence. Financial waiting remains publicly gated; a policy-bearing Contract requires its configured complete lifecycle/control resources.

The following Deadline inputs cover index repair; Parked source/registration cleanup retains its own proof owner. These input guards alone do not qualify the aggregate or a generated coefficient.

Pure `T/B` removal has eight common point keys: the two Actor legacy-marker absences, the owning handle, semantic (`T`) or process (`B`) authority, bucket header, two legacy bucket guards and clock-local index length. A surviving bucket adds at most seven member pages, four index pages and one reverse position. The seven pages cover target, four physical/vacancy neighbors and two boundary checks; aliases only reduce the union. At most two keys disappear: handle and empty member page. Full-page retention uses a subset of these inputs.

Last-member removal instead has one member page, at most eighteen index pages and sixteen reverse positions at the reference cap of 10,000 Actors. It can delete five keys: handle, member page, header, removed reverse position and singleton index-tail page. A retained tail or direct-tail return has fewer changes. This uses the existing index address bound below, including root, interior, ascending and stopping paths; it does not combine deep heap repair with the seven-page surviving-bucket alternative. Both inventories exclude the caller's process/semantic detachment and final destruction.

Member pages within one bucket share the complete first-key prefix, at most 57 bytes (53 for Block); index pages within one clock share 49 bytes. Both double maps then have 24-byte page suffixes. For selected pages, charge shared ancestors once per group and at most 48 matching nodes per selected page: `50 + 48r` for `r` member pages and `34 + 48i` for `i` index pages. The native guard checks actual Block/Tick hashes, prefixes and typed value ceilings. Selected pages do not constitute a complete removed prefix, so each deleted key retains its own divergent collapse allowance.

With `M` adding these grouped paths, all other ordinary point paths and each namespace's `g` once, the conditional package read/net-root bound is `M*650 + (q+d)*1284 + V + 5`. Here `q/d` count queried/deleted ordinary keys. Encoded payload ceilings include full typed authority, handle, header, member/index pages, reverse positions and index length; both legacy families must be absent. The guard pins the reference cap and existing eighteen-page/sixteen-position geometry. A changed capacity, schema or host key domain requires requalification.
