Direct `B` applies to Block retry and Tick financial expiry. Its removal checks the process/handle residence; financial episode and current-Step authentication belong to the preceding `L` owner. Parked `B` conservatively adds Pending owner, timed review and pending-review fallback, including full review payloads even when canonical Close short-circuits the fallback. It does not add episode, source, registration or scan cleanup: those remain `L/P`. Waiting has no armed independent `T`, and this table does not assert simultaneous attainment of its alternatives.

These are source-qualified package proof inputs, not complete physical Weight or observed worst-case proof. They use existing legal authority/page/index fixtures without a new occupancy campaign. Valid heap/page geometry is required; arbitrary raw corruption does not inherit the address bound. Repeated decoding/comparison, host configuration and callbacks, currency, events, overlay/root commit CPU and complete `P` composition remain open. No production coefficient, budget change, public policy/wait enablement or baseline acceptance follows from the numeric guard.

`P` includes optional primary Deadline removal through `B`. The remaining suffix has eight common point keys: process, Pending owner, episode, complete dependency plan, primary handle, timed review, pending event and pending review. Its epilogue removes six of these; process deletion belongs to `L` and handle deletion to `B`. Every registration adds its exact handle, position, source header, asset/source pair and target page. Repeated validation/removal reads share their pre-state keys. Complete plan/episode payloads and both pending marker alternatives are conservatively charged without asserting simultaneous presence.

If the page retains another registration, only the handle/position disappear; two new hole keys are absent in pre-state and are inserted. If it empties while the source remains, at most two page neighbors and one revision add reads; handle, position and page disappear. Last-source removal has no page neighbors, removes header/revision and both asset/source maps, and can retire one scan node with at most two ring neighbors plus the shared list header. Across mixed sources, scan-neighbor aliasing only reduces the union; each removed source contributes at most eight ordinary net deletions. No source allocator, semantic/Contract, currency, event or custody mutation belongs to this suffix.

Empty-page reclamation processes exactly 32 local positions, including the newly inserted hole. At most 31 other pre-state tail positions are relocated: the new hole is either popped directly or first encountered as the tail. Each hole map therefore queries at most 32 local/append keys plus 31 old tail keys, with at most 31 pre-state net deletions. The new key is absent both before and after empty-page reclamation. The retained-source interior fixture already exercises 63 distinct keys per table/source; no additional occupancy enumeration is needed. Slot/order aliases cannot increase this distinct pre-state key bound; it is not a bound on repeated CPU or overlay work.

`remove_dependency_free_position` is constant-work dense-stack swap-last, not a heap: a tail removal uses two gets/two removes, while a non-tail removal adds two gets/two inserts. Empty-page retirement checks only32 slots and invokes at most32 removals; neither the source's hole count nor its monotone append frontier adds a search/sift/history walk. `parked_public_close_free_stack_slot_order_preserves_custody_and_caller_root` uses128 public System creations and63 peer Closes with one watch. Its slot0/slot31 boundary pair replays a pre-call diagnostic sequence against the actual public Close result:31non-tail plus one tail versus32non-tail removals, while at most31 old foreign tails move in either case.

The new slot31 hole first appears as the appended tail donor, moves into an earlier dense offset and is removed later. Hence equal63-address unions do not imply equal branch execution. The test covers both commit and outer rollback, exact remaining stack/inverse/page links,64 peer full states, active scan revision/frontier, allocated System slots and native custody77. Asset ingress uses a real mock mint to a distinct non-Actor followed by exactly one typed producer publication. It adds no measurement counter or storage injection; diagnostic branch counts are not measured CPU. This one-Step System witness is not a wide-body/User/root-clock/SDK/reference Weight upper.

For partial source prefixes, the ordinary point argument shares 48 matching source ancestors, then charges each selected key's remaining suffix and divergent/collapse allowance. With `r=63`, `d=31`, actual key width `l=80/81` and inline 9/8-byte values, each table is bounded by `(G + w*(48 + 2r*(l-56)))*650 + w*(r+d)*1284 + 5`. This remains valid with unqueried surviving holes; it does not use the complete-subtree node bound. Last-source removal instead inherits the independently proved complete-prefix read/deletion bound in [Storage Topology](#storage-topology). Retained-page insertion uses two ordinary absence proofs per source, without a deletion term.

The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) supplies a narrower conditional forward-table input from the complete dense-offset hash domain. Every smaller coherent stack has a branch topology contained in that domain; maximized shared branch/fanout costs replace the per-key suffix charge. The read term includes inline leaves and partial keys. Typed deletion siblings remain inside a surviving source prefix; one foreign sibling conservatively covers complete-prefix loss. No embedding or successor composition inherits the reference corpus automatically.

Coherent committed registration topology further bounds the source's hole population. With `N` registrations and `P` retained C32 pages, every page is nonempty, hence `P <= N` and tracked holes are at most `32P - N <= 31N`. Unallocated tail slots can only reduce that count. Empty-page retirement inserts its newly vacant slot before reclaiming the page, so the transient dense-offset domain also includes offset `31N`. At the reference cap `N <= 10,000`, offsets `0..=310,000` cover all committed reads and that one provisional insertion. The append guard's broader 320,000-offset input remains valid for its declared domain.

The reference hash-image guard exhausts this narrower 310,001-offset domain and maximizes the shared branch union over up to 63 queried paths. It gives 104,430 branch bytes, a 106,887-byte source read input including leaves/partial keys, and 156,669 bytes per source including typed deletion siblings and prefix-loss allowance. The following four-source arithmetic and four-watch composition tables retain their historical input domain; they are not current one-watch ceilings. Shared global ancestors give a four-source forward-table input of 629,931 bytes; the broader input remains 632,159. This is a conditional source/trie bound, not measured physical cost, history/repetition proof or a complete Parked Close certificate. The reverse table retains its separate arbitrary page-ID domain.

The reverse table cannot inherit that dense-offset domain, but its complete source prefix contains only fixed 81-byte keys and inline 8-byte values. Its 25-byte suffix permits at most 50 branch nodes per selected path, with no branch values. Each branch contributes at most `7 + 16*33 = 535` bytes before partial-key bytes; that allowance includes header, bitmap, SCALE-vector overhead and odd-nibble rounding. Partial bytes sum to at most 25 per path, and one inline terminal leaf adds at most 14 other bytes, including rounding. Thus 63 paths have a source read term bounded by `63*(50*535 + 25 + 14) = 1,687,707` bytes, without assumptions about page-ID density or hash distribution.

At most 31 deletion-collapse siblings remain inside that typed prefix, each bounded by `2 + 25 + 2 + 16*33 + 2 = 559` encoded bytes. Prefix loss can add one foreign node of at most 1,284 bytes; an absent pre-state source prefix also needs only one such mismatch. The guard composes shared source ancestors once: `(G + 48w)*650 + w*(1,687,707 + 31*559 + 1,284) + 5`. With native `G=5`, `w=4`, this gives `6,953,335` bytes for the partial reverse table. The native paired-map trie primitive preserves dense forward/reverse agreement through 31 tail relocations and replays the exact net root from recorded nodes; it is not full Actor topology or a maximum-proof witness. Complete-prefix and retained-page alternatives retain their separate bounds.

The native guard composes these hole inputs with full typed plan, episode, registration/page/header, asset/source, revision and scan-ring values at four watches. Ordinary `M/X/V` use the established point proof model; the separate hole term includes its own conservative collection overhead. `B` is excluded from this table and must be added once when a review handle exists. Rows cover categorical alternatives and do not claim joint attainment of every charged typed maximum.

The typed-key guard further qualifies single-query fixed-length map paths below their own 32-byte storage namespace. Native metadata proves constant key length recursively for primitives, fixed arrays/composites/tuples and equal-width enum alternatives; Compact, sequence, bit-sequence, recursive and unequal-width enum keys are excluded unless the hasher's complete output has constant length independently. In particular, variable Block/Tick `WakeupKey` is excluded. Only namespaces with exactly one queried role and no shared-prefix group are refined; grouped member/index pages and complete tail prefixes keep their existing owners.

Equal-length stored keys forbid branch values below that namespace. For a suffix of `L` bytes, at most `2L` branch positions cost `7 + 16*33 = 535` bytes each; partial bytes along the path sum to at most `L`, and terminal framing/hash costs at most 37. The old `2L*650` matching-path term is therefore replaced by `2L*535 + L + 37`. The guard retains the complete earlier namespace paths, foreign/absence witnesses, typed payload and every net-deletion/collapse allowance. Actual V1 codec checks cover suffix widths 17/24/48, value lengths 0/32/33/64 and the inline/hash transition. A full fixed-width fork encodes to 556 bytes with its 23-byte partial; adding a shorter complete key creates a branch value and raises it to 589, rejecting the no-value bound. The fixed-length premise is essential.

Scalar composition combines these disjoint point-read reductions with the finite Deadline index-page corollary, preserving all earlier coarse rows and source multiplicities. It covers the 32 active source rows and both Dormant classes. Every row narrows; complete body, policy/wait payloads, grouped pages, holes, class/currency/FRAME and deletion work remain charged. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) binds each metadata-qualified role list, arithmetic and validation.

`finalize_actor_loaded_inner` opens its own `with_transaction`, including when its caller already owns a transaction; unlike Dormant's reused transaction helper, this can introduce a nested commit/rollback boundary. `close_finalize_body_refusal_restores_run_and_event_prefix` covers User/System and retained-Run/Idle inputs. Reconstruction independently checks that limit and the exact expected chunk count before allocating or iterating. They do not certify arbitrary raw-storage corruption, RefTime or trie proof.

The User `close_finalize_user(a, r)` and `close_dormant_user(r)` profiles use a binary peer-retirement selector: `r=0` retains the admitted owner peers, while `r=1` ordinarily closes them before measurement. The latter leaves the target in its original highest owner slot and exercises actual bitmap-key removal and release of the owner's last ActorState hold. Shared verification checks physical bitmap presence as well as its value, so a stored zero bitmap cannot masquerade as deletion. Native tests cover both selector endpoints with verification enabled/disabled. Earlier benchmark-Wasm samples predate System-only pause and do not qualify the revised User consumed-AtTime shape. This is branch coverage, not a continuous economic scaling variable or permission to assume either branch dominates the other's cost.

The load, detach and finalization profiles share categorical authority selector `a` through `prepare_close_shape`. Every shape runs for both classes; these labels are not continuous cost components.

| `a` | Trigger | Cycle | Process authority | Run |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Manual | Suspended | Block Deadline | First-attempt retry |
| 1 | Maximum AddressEvent | Suspended | Block Deadline | First-attempt retry |
| 2 | Cadenced | Idle | Service plus future Tick | Absent |
| 3 | Consumed AtTime | Idle | User: Live Service; System: owner-paused Disabled; neither retains Tick | Absent |
| 4 | Cadenced | Running | Live Service plus future Tick | Committed Transfer and continued failure |
| 5 | Cadenced | Suspended | Live Service plus future Tick | Committed Transfer and retryable failure |

`large_header_fields` supplies full canonical source/asset allowlists and funding policy. Retry header-width assertions adjust the selected Trigger, host activation shape and excluded auto-close nonce. `open_reachable_retry` selects a matching ingress asset, funds its sender and uses `BenchmarkHelper::transfer_signed`; fabricated notification or native-only movement cannot stand in for the maximum asset filter. Setup verifies sender debit and non-native recipient credit, and destruction checks include that ingress asset.

`prepare_temporal_close` executes two ordinary Service opportunities. Idle uses Transfer → StopCycle. Consumed System AtTime is publicly paused into typed `Disabled(OwnerPaused)` status with no residence; consumed User AtTime remains unpaused on Live Service. Native fixture tests cover this class-specific shape. Earlier User-disabled AtTime measurements do not qualify the current reachable User shape.

Completed history retains its real anchor and last-cycle block; only Cadenced retains a future Tick. Funding uses the maximum allowlist, a legal future auto-close nonce and no temporal window/cooldown. Semantic-width assertions account for class encoding, consumed-source pointer absence and host-fixed lifecycle Weight.

Busy temporal shapes execute Transfer followed by one funded Transfer rejected by its deposit minimum. ContinueNextStep retains Running at cursor 2; RetryLater retains Suspended at cursor 1. Identity nonce stays zero while Run nonce is one. Ordinary fixed-amount shapes exclude the absent snapshot and forbidden Running suspension from their width guard. Every maximum-prefix shape instead commits a capture Transfer on the maximum-width non-fee asset, retaining the original snapshot after the later Temporary failure. Its Suspended Run reaches the complete codec ceiling; Running subtracts only its forbidden suspension.

The separate `prepare_busy_temporal_rearm` constructor commits a skipped prefix, then attempts a capture Transfer on the helper's maximum-width non-fee asset. It requires half the preservable volume to equal the funded below-minimum transfer amount; incompatible host setup stops explicitly. A real Temporary failure retains that snapshot. ContinueNextStep reaches Running cursor 2 with no suspension; one retry retains Suspended Service, and two retries retain Suspended Block residence. Width checks include the complete snapshot and subtract only the forbidden Running suspension. All three rearm profiles preserve the encoded Run, identity, residence, hold, native custody, Fee Sink and event prefix. Their shared Tick classifier, pure retry removal and two-clock public Close inputs inherit this constructor. Native verification-on/off and try-state pass the rearm profiles and public Close cases; complete physical maxima and generated binding remain separate.

The extra `close_public_cycle_snapshot` uses the same wide AddressEvent source with a real first-Step capture of a maximum-width non-fee asset, followed by the temporary Transfer failure. Capture excludes the asset's preserved minimum; its 50% share commits to a pre-endowed recipient. The remaining Steps retain the wide inert suffix, funding/window/watch header and ordinary Suspended Block residence. Before measurement, the complete Run must equal its encoded schema ceiling with the original snapshot intact. The measured block contains only public User Close. Native verification-on/off checks destruction and committed custody; a separate late currency-hold refusal restores the exact root and Run before unmeasured repair/Close. A fresh reference benchmark-Wasm smoke also passes with one watch. The reference integration owns raw artifact identity and observation scope.

`assert_contract_geometry_encoding` checks reconstructed Step count, head option presence, contiguous tail indices, exact final-chunk occupancy, paired resource lengths and actual fixture encodings against component byte ceilings. `assert_max_contract_geometry` additionally requires exactly `MaxContractSteps`, not merely the same number of tail keys. `contract_encoding_bounds_cover_zero_head_partial_and_full_tails` covers empty/head-only/chunk-boundary bodies and an ordinarily admitted shorter body whose chunk count equals the maximum's; the latter must fail the maximum-geometry assertion. The helper is read-only and runs outside measured Close owners.

For admitted `n` Steps, let `K = MAX_STEPS_PER_TAIL_CHUNK = 4`, `m = ceil(max(n-1, 0)/K)`, and let `H`, `S`, `R`, `A` be valid encoded-length upper bounds for the Contract header, Step, resource envelope and body authority. With `E = S + R` and `F = A + 4 + 2 × encoded_len(Compact(K))`, the following decomposition yields conservative value bounds only when those component ceilings are sound. The helper uses `R = 4 × Compact<u64>::max_encoded_len() = 36`, independently of the SDK Weight trait. It uses `MaxEncodedLen` for `H`, `S` and `A`: current body types contain Weight only in the two resource fields and declare no compact field attributes. Host-supplied scalar types must still satisfy their encoding-bound contracts. The separate agreement check against derived head/chunk metadata is a schema check, not the byte ceiling.

An embedding may qualify a narrower coded-value envelope from its admitted domain without changing this generic schema bound. `ensure_contract_steps_fits_idle_budget` admits bare no-policy Steps only when the complete coded maximum, including each control/effect sum, is no greater than guaranteed idle service. `derive_step_resource_envelopes` adds the host's fixed Parked completion owner to actual no-policy control; effect is unchanged. A finite reserve and completion bound therefore constrain the four compact coordinates. The [reference integration](../../../../docs/deos/actors.integration.en.md#actor-state-holds-and-capacity) qualifies a conditional 20-byte ceiling. Earlier attainment by twelve wide Steps belongs to the historical fanout/watch domain; the current remaining-Task fixture checks containment without claiming attainment. These are encoding inputs, independent of physical Weight soundness, optional-policy admission, generic 36-byte capacity and hold pricing.

An admitted Step ceiling can likewise use the canonical total-predicate budget rather than the larger raw DNF schema. Public canonicalization/shape validation rejects empty clauses and a total predicate count above `MaxPredicatesPerStep`. When the total-predicate and clause bounds agree and stay below the first compact-length boundary, singleton clauses attain the maximum prefix contribution and predicate count together; add the Option tag, maximum Task and error policy. The reference integration records attainment with its actual configured limits. Generic host/scalar encoding contracts and admission semantics remain prerequisites, and a raw schema ceiling remains appropriate without that narrower qualification.

| Body surface | Keys | Encoded-value bound |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Head | 1 | `H + 2 + (n > 0 ? E : 0)` |
| Tail with `k` Steps | 1 | `F + k × E`, with `1 <= k <= 4` |
| Complete head and tails | `1 + m` | `H + 2 + n × E + m × F` |

The package's [resource encoding metadata](#resource-encoding-metadata) supplies compact-aware resource/certificate bounds. `admission_identity_hash_input_has_bounded_encoding` retains the SDK counterexample and verifies exact package maxima. `contract_encoding_bounds_cover_compact_weight_extremes` attains corrected metadata bounds in codec-only head and full-tail records and checks partial tails; ordinary admitted fixtures remain separate. No maximum Weight is injected into an Actor, and reference admission limits remain distinct from representable type maxima.

The two head bytes are Option tags; each tail has two compact vector lengths. `hashed_key_for` checks the current Blake2_128Concat key geometry: head `32 + 16 + encoded_len(ActorId)` bytes, tail `32 + 32 + encoded_len(ActorId) + 4`. One successful `load_contract_geometry_with_admission` decodes one head and `m` tails containing `n` Steps and `n` resource envelopes. Reconstruction additionally clones tail Steps and verifies semantic/body commitments. Repeated loads, hashing, certificate rebuilding, rejected/corrupt paths and host adapter work require separate accounting. Key/value byte ceilings omit trie structure and absence witnesses; they are neither ProofSize nor RefTime bounds.

For successful public canonical Active Close without policy and with `OnUnfunded::Close`, the following source-derived inventory attributes complete body work. It excludes metadata probes, residence validation/removal, Run encoding, class/hold cleanup and transaction machinery. Let `t = max(n-1, 0)`; a commitment pair means one `semantic_contract_id` and one `body_commitment` invocation.

| Stage | Complete geometry loads | Commitment pairs | Step clones in source | Host authority calls |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `load_close_authority` → `load_active_actor_state` | 1 | 1 during reconstruction | `t` tail Steps | 0 |
| `load_finalization_authority` → `load_active_actor_state` | 1 | 1 during reconstruction | `t + n`; the complete view clone is compared with the supplied instance | 0 |
| `detach_actor_publication_inner` | 0 | 1 through `build_admission_certificate` | 0 | 1 |
| `finalize_actor_from_consumed_state` → loaded finalizer | 1 in `remove_admitted_contract_geometry_with_admission` | 1 during removal reconstruction | `t + n`, plus `n` when cancelling a retained Run | 0 |

Thus this path reconstructs three complete bodies and computes four commitment pairs. It decodes `3n` Steps and `3n` resource envelopes through `3(1+m)` full body-record loads; those are not unique DB reads, and existence probes are additional. Source clone work is `3t + (2+r)n` Steps, where `r=1` for a retained Run. `cancel_run_internal_loaded` clones `source = state.clone()` even on Closing before bypassing successor publication. This is a source-work inventory, not a claim about optimized machine allocations or cycle counts. The two strict Active loading stages also each invoke canonical semantic/residence validation twice; their cost is not covered by body byte ceilings.

`semantic_contract_id` hashes the 19-byte domain, authored header fields and encoded Step vector. `body_commitment` builds an indexed-reference vector and hashes its 15-byte domain plus compact count and each `(u32 index, Step)` pair. With `h` authored-field bytes and `c(n)` compact count bytes, message sizes are `19 + h + c(n) + sum(step_bytes)` and `15 + c(n) + 4n + sum(step_bytes)`. Four pairs therefore encode Steps eight times on the successful public path. `contract_commitment_hashing_visits_each_step_once` checks both actual generic methods with an encoding counter, exact input bytes and compact-count boundaries; it tests the codec primitive, not the aggregate call graph or admission.

Admission identity hashes `221 + encoded_len(maximum_lifecycle_weight)` bytes: a 20-byte domain, four 32-byte commitments, two `u32` versions and a 65-byte wake qualification. A sound representable Weight ceiling is twice `Compact<u64>::max_encoded_len()` (18), giving at most 239 input bytes, not a reachable-host assertion. Wake qualification separately hashes `24 + encoded_len(trigger)` and `24 + encoded_len(window)` bytes. The codec regression checks the actual hashes, compact transitions and maximum encoding; the domain-field regression rejects independent tampering. On the successful public path, source calls comprise two identity validations per strict load, one certificate construction at detachment and one identity validation at removal; wake qualification is rebuilt in the two strict loads and detachment. These counts exclude other identities and storage hashing, and are not CPU bounds.

The generic `build_admission_certificate` recomputes commitments, invokes host `AdmissionCertificateAuthority::current()` once and constructs the certificate; it does not derive per-Step resource envelopes. Host authority work, certificate identity checks and wake qualification remain distinct costs. Pure `close_finalize_*` includes the third body reload despite receiving loaded state. Internal terminal entries may omit public loading, while failed/corrupt paths may stop earlier or have different owners; neither inherits the successful public inventory as a certified resource bound.

Policy/AwaitFunding detachment selects `load_admitted_contract_geometry` instead of certificate rebuilding, so it performs an additional complete geometry load with policy/monitor verification, followed by current-authority and Close-price checks. AwaitFunding additionally invokes canonical carrier validation; each waiting validation authenticates the financial episode and may read its current Step. The no-policy three-body/four-commitment/one-host-call inventory above cannot be applied unchanged to these optional branches. Their distinct proof keys are covered by the `L/S` supersets, while exact hashing, cloning, provider multiplicity and CPU composition remain open.

`close_host_authority_calls_belong_to_detachment` counts the actual mock provider boundary outside storage. Across both classes and all six shapes, successful public Active Close calls it once, solely during detachment; body reloads do not multiply this owner. Wrong-owner loading refusal and public Dormant Close call it zero times. The existing late-hold rollback regression additionally observes one call on each failed public Close and repaired retry, but zero on consumed finalization: rolling back state does not erase already-performed host work. These are call-count witnesses, not host-cost measurements or coverage of every corrupt/terminal path.

`load_canonical_actor_semantic_state` has a separate bounded carrier-validation owner. Each successful branch performs seven base storage calls: two legacy-marker absence probes, semantic/process loads and Service-node/Deadline-handle/Pending-owner loads. These are source calls, not unique backend reads or proof nodes. The conditional branch inventory below does not itself establish reachability for every configuration.

| Successful branch | Additional calls | Total calls | At most distinct keys |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Service or Disabled/no residence | None | 7 | 7 |
| Deadline | One Deadline page | 8 | 8 |
| Parked without timed review | Timed-review absence and episode lookup | 9 | 9 |
| Parked with timed review | Timed review, repeated Pending owner and timed review, Deadline page, episode | 12 | 10 |

AwaitingFunding adds `financial_wait_matches_process` to the Service/Deadline carrier branch. It reads the episode and independent Trigger absence, authenticates head/Run and optional policy/monitor, and may read one current-Step tail. Those calls and host authority/fee pricing are additional to the ordinary seven/eight-call rows; the `L/S` proof inventory includes their package keys without treating call counts as physical prices.

The repeated Parked reads occur inside `deadline_handle_matches_process`. In this caller, an outer `Some(DependencyTimedReviews)` guarantees its inner reread is also present, so the helper's `PendingDependencyReviews` fallback is not read. Each page fetch decodes its bounded C32 value before the O(1) slot check; validation does not traverse ring neighbours or the Deadline heap. Parked review and episode checks are computed before their final Boolean conjunction, so a negative owner check does not imply that every later lookup was skipped.

Each strict healthy Active load first performs five outer classification calls in `load_actor_semantic_state`; Contract-head presence short-circuits the Run-existence probe. It then invokes canonical carrier validation twice. Thus public Close's two strict loads contribute four canonical passes: 28/32/36/48 calls for the rows above, reusing the same carrier keys. Those counts exclude the ten outer classification calls, body/Run loads, finalization's generation lookup, origin policy, removal and transaction machinery. Dormant/missing classification can instead perform seven calls and does not invoke the Active carrier validator.

`close_loading_rejects_carrier_drift_without_mutation` reuses the six-shape User/System fixtures to reject stale Service generations, missing Deadline slots and Disabled residence drift through canonical loading, Close loading and public Close. An existing ordinarily reached Parked fixture separately loses its Pending owner, timed review or episode. Every rejection preserves the storage root; restoring each carrier restores valid loading, and repaired six-shape cases close with custody/hold/committed-prefix checks. These are typed corruption probes, not arbitrary raw-storage corruption coverage or measured RefTime.

User finalization retains both authority and owner-slot selectors; native tests cover all twelve combinations. FRAME component sweeps are not Cartesian, and categorical storage owners may appear at only one selector value. Such data cannot support per-owner slope fits. The root benchmark script's `--raw-only --json-file` mode collects successful fixed-corner or branch samples without regression or Weight generation. Raw Wasm samples cover all twelve User combinations through ordinary and fixed-retained-slot runs; neither selector interpolation nor class dominance nor whole-Close maxima follows.

The extras `close_detach_user` and `close_detach_system` time actual `detach_actor_publication` across the shared authority shapes. They cover admission rebuilding, authority/Run revalidation, Block retry or Tick/Service removal, Disabled without residence, process deletion and semantic reload/write. Full authority loading precedes measurement; finalization follows verification. Thus these are detachment witnesses including a simple residence cost, not pure `L` coefficients or a bound for arbitrary residence geometry; do not subtract the Deadline diagnostic to manufacture an overhead coefficient.

`retained_close_destruction` compares semantic bytes excluding the detached placement pointers, plus exact Contract head/tails, optional Run, hold, counters and events. Verification separately requires both actual semantic pointers to be absent; normalizing a snapshot cannot hide a retained pointer. Only those fields change in the pre-captured state used for unmeasured finalization. Custody is checked separately. `close_detachment_profiles_exclude_finalization` checks both classes and verification modes: disabling verification retains Active semantic/body and optional Run after publication removal. The read-only loading profiles own the preceding public-wrapper work; combining their bound with detachment and finalization still requires qualified legal shapes and failed-path costs.

`close_destruction_late_hold_failure_restores_public_and_consumed_state` injects a missing currency hold after reachable User setup, covering both retained-peer and last-owner-slot cases. Both public Close and loaded finalization fail at hold reconciliation, after transaction-local Run/body deletion, counter changes and slot release. Exact storage-root equality proves rollback of those changes; restoring the hold permits the same Close to succeed with common custody/peer checks. All four temporal last-slot public cases preserve the earlier committed Transfer through failed Close and successful retry. This negative fixture is not an admitted malformed-ledger benchmark, a failed-path timing bound or compensation of previously committed Action effects.

The extra `close_parked_dependency_release` measures transaction-wrapped `release_parked_dependency_authority` alone. A real completed User Parked episode carries the host maximum watch count; 31 completed peers fill every shared registration page's 32 handle slots. Its absent page links do not attain the linked-page codec ceiling. Ordinary future-window guards fill the admitted population to the last singleton Block-index tail page. Release removes the target's watches and root review, repairs the deep index and reclaims its tail page while preserving every peer Deadline handle, process/semantic/Contract authority, independent Tick Trigger, holds, Service topology and custody. Verification completes remaining Close outside measurement.

The Parked-release fixture includes a durable `PendingDependencyEvents` row produced by a real third-party watched-asset mint and bounded source-dispatch work; source publication and scanning stay outside measurement. The target remains below its balance threshold with its timed review retained. By contrast, the sole live `publish_due_dependency_review` caller is the transactional `process_due_parked_balance_review`: review publication and interpretation commit together, so no persistent `PendingDependencyReviews` row is fabricated for Close. `parked_balance_episode_publishes_atomically_and_positive_review_reclaims_it` covers marker absence after resource refusal, interpretation failure, negative rearm and positive wake.

Two native regressions prove the Parked-release measurement boundary and exact-root rollback after a late registration-header refusal; the pending-event/deep-index profile also runs in isolated benchmark Wasm. This witness does not certify every interior/index-path extremum or the complete Close maximum; it publishes no production coefficient.

The extras `close_deadline_page_unlink` and `close_trigger_page_unlink` measure transaction-wrapped process or independent Trigger removal alone. Ordinary future-window or Idle Cadenced System Actors fill nine Block or Tick pages; Root close of guards leaves one interior target with four distinct physical/vacancy neighbors. Extra vacancies make both vacancy neighbors internal, filling all four link fields. Separate full first/last pages maximize boundary reads in `validate_deadline_index_header`. Shared postconditions preserve all 252 peer handles, repair each neighbor, and retain process, semantic and Service authority. Verification completes the remaining teardown and checks custody/global try-state. Native `pure_deadline_page_unlink_preserves_four_neighbors_and_close_boundary` and fresh benchmark-Wasm runs cover both sources.



`prepare_temporal_close` uses the real single-recipient module-refusal producer after a committed Transfer prefix. Both attempted Steps are narrower than the inert RemoveLiquidity suffix; unvisited Steps retain maximum Task/predicate operand width. Ordinary admission and fresh Observation checks remain effective. This constructor does not claim an all-maximum-width body.

Current maximum-prefix native qualification covers public peer/last-slot Close, late hold refusal, pure loading/finalization, the joint Running body/Run constraint and Deadline pages. The fixed Deadline-key inventory keeps 24 verification-on and nine verification-off cases as independent named tests: Root/RootWide/InteriorWide/Upward, User peers/last slot or System, and tail occupancy 1/32. Each case invokes the unchanged benchmark and global try-state in fresh externalities. Exact source/parameter equivalence reuses 32 completed sequential cases; the final System Upward/tail-32 verification-off case passes separately. Both monolithic timeouts remain historical. These shapes retain a real snapshot; ordinary fixed-amount Native-prefix observations keep their earlier narrower scope. Fresh reference Wasm samples only the selected System Suspended wide-header public Close. The reference integration owns exact identities and observation limits; complete physical Close remains open.

`prepare_temporal_close_with_prefix(..., true)` retains wide predicates around a successful capture Transfer and a real single-recipient failure. The first Step commits 50% of the captured preservable volume; the later failure leaves that original snapshot intact. Native fee/custody funding uses the Native minimum independently of the prefix asset. Unvisited Steps stay wide; committed custody and prefix credit survive Close. Earlier fixed-prefix measurements do not qualify these changed body/Run inputs.

Raw extras `close_load_maximum_prefix` and `close_finalize_maximum_prefix` select User Running/Suspended then System Running/Suspended at categorical `a=0..3`. Loading measures signed-owner authority and finalization reload only, preserves the root, and verifies complete Close afterward. Finalization measures consumed-state destruction after unmeasured loading/detachment. User owner-slot peers remain. `maximum_prefix_close_profiles_preserve_body_credit_and_boundaries` checks both verification modes and global try-state; the five original Close families retain the Native-prefix scenarios. Optional/public policy, joint resource maxima and complete physical Close remain separate gates. The wide-header composition below qualifies the additional no-policy header branch.

Canonical Running width is conditional on the committed outcome. Opening and current-Step execution retain a Failed outcome in Running only through ContinueNextStep; RetryLater failure retains Suspended, and deadline return changes residence without clearing suspension. Successful/skipped retry overwrites the outcome before returning to Running. Contract replacement cancels the Run and rotates its body authority. Therefore a maximal Failed Running Run requires one shorter policy in its retained body. Other outcome encodings save more Run bytes than that policy costs. `running_close_body_and_run_widths_obey_committed_outcome` checks this joint bound for both classes, including a real repaired retry with all twelve Steps still wide, resource-refusal preservation and custody-neutral Close. The integration owner gives reference codec substitutions; headers, resources, trie/host costs and physical Close remain independent.

`prepare_prefix_close` shares the temporal constructor with a wide AddressEvent mode. `prepare_wide_header_prefix_close` retains complete source/asset filters, signed funding, a ScheduleWindow and the host-configured activation plan. A real signed-origin host transfer matches the last source and asset; two actual Service opportunities commit the Transfer prefix and retain the real module failure. Running remains on Live Service; nonzero cooldown puts Suspended on an ordinary Block Deadline. Neither phase owns an independent Tick Trigger or a busy Parked registration/episode. The header guard substitutes the actual maximum watch plan into the schema ceiling and excludes the eight-byte nonce payload forbidden alongside that plan. The existing conditional Step/Run guards remain.

Raw extras `close_load_wide_header_prefix` and `close_finalize_wide_header_prefix` use the same categorical class/phase mapping and pure L/F boundaries as the temporal maximum-prefix extras. Source publication, admission, prefix/failure execution and finalizer detachment are outside measurement. `wide_header_prefix_close_preserves_admitted_run_and_custody` checks both verification modes, exact residence/authority, read-only loading, complete teardown, preserved custody/committed credit and global try-state. Current native qualification covers these snapshot-bearing loading/finalization inputs; earlier fixed-prefix Wasm profiles retain their original source identities and narrower Run scope. The reference codec join below qualifies the no-policy resource/header/body/Run composition. Certificate, optional-policy/financial-wait public admission, complete detachment geometry and physical Close retain separate qualification owners in the reference integration.

Nonempty Step execution shares `load_effectful_step_source`, `prepare_effectful_step_control` and `execute_effectful_step_control`. The production transition retains Close qualification before preparation and maximum policy-control containment before mutation; the mutating body retains the real Task effect owner and performed-work accounting. A benchmark-only StopCycle facade checks the intrinsic path without optional policy prices, retaining real predicate execution and the Task effect provider, then leaves publication or destruction/retirement to the encompassing transaction. `prepare_step_policy_source` preserves the complete Opening fixtures at the configured Step count; the StopCycle wrapper retains its existing source families. `stop_cycle_policy_terminal` joins control/destruction/retirement; `stop_cycle_policy_publication` uses a wide false policy and joins control/surviving Idle publication. Neither composition supplies qualified prices or late actual-Weight settlement.

`transfer_policy_terminal` adds a genuine positive Transfer, clean-Cycle authority publication before Close, destruction/retirement and Action-fee suffix. `transfer_policy_progression` retains the Run and advances Service instead; it verifies B+1/Q1, unchanged prepaid holds, Contract/identity retention, exact custody/recipient/fee deltas and explicit asset/root rollback. `expected_step_balances` shares setup-only economic expectations without merging the distinct measured owners. Both Transfer profiles remain raw-only and exclude actual-control price settlement.

Native Running predicate conformance derives its paid Run from a policy-bearing Transfer Opening with a protected AddLiquidity successor. Benchmark-only `benchmark_policy_step_source` loads authenticated stored geometry without qualifying host prices; `benchmark_running_predicate_control` requires Running, genuine predicate rejection, no Task invocation and an unsatisfied policy before publication. Raw-only `running_policy_predicate` measures that control/publication/Service composition for User/System and absent/present balance records; source preparation and presence queries remain outside timing. The witness checks prefix/fee/hold/custody preservation and rollback.

The benchmark-only `benchmark_policy_step_source` accepts genuine Running/Suspended states without supplying policy prices, including a Suspended cursor-zero head while refusing Running cursor zero. It shares bounded Run/current-chunk loading and `materialize_actor_service_state` with production: the internal control Contract-view repeats the current Step to the header count, rather than loading or representing the full authored body. Production price/containment gates remain before materialization. Full authored-body verification is a separate fixture operation outside measurement. A benchmark-only liquidity retry facade constrains the shared core to invoked Temporary/Suspended failure or funded Executed/Continued recovery, preserving the existing production guards; native economic checks remain subordinate to the mock adapter's scope.

Benchmark-only late/exhausted liquidity facades constrain genuine Suspended failures separately from the raw recovery facade. Their caller composes canonical Deadline transfer or clean-authority publication plus consumed destruction/Service retirement, followed by the actual Action fee; native rollback preserves committed due-return boundaries. Authored retry limits are set before creation without changing configured ceilings. Shared `execute_liquidity_policy_late_attempt` composes both branches for the native witness and raw-only `liquidity_policy_late_control`; shared preparation returns due Deadline members and selects B+1 outside timing. The raw owner requires real host snapshots, preserves failed inputs except the Action fee and checks post-return rollback; it includes Task effects and supplies no standalone control tariff.

Raw-only `liquidity_policy_control` requires supported host snapshots for input debits, LP/reserves and deposits; unsupported mocks skip explicitly while the independent native semantic witness remains active. Its measured region joins Task effects with control/fees, not a standalone control price.

`opening_policy_pre_effect_control` reuses the reachable empty-staking-receipt-loss source with policy authenticated before Opening. Its failure facade requires permanent Unstake preparation failure, `NotInvoked`, zero effect/no Action observation and unsatisfied policy. The existing raw Abort source publishes surviving Failed Idle; the shared native/raw `opening_policy_pre_effect_continue` source retains Run/Service progression with the exact preparation error and no Action receipt. Its custody/share/hold/Contract/certificate and root checks remain outside timing; it does not include Task effects or qualify other domains.

Both pre-effect raw profiles use `(r,c)` to select commit/rollback and User/System. The User source exercises the same bodies with one nonzero Pipeline receipt/debit/sink credit and no Action receipt. Fee-aware custody, share, hold and certificate checks remain outside timing; this is not actual-control-price or independent-backing qualification. The native witness excludes Action receipts and verifies custody/hold/body/certificate rollback. This System/minimal-header sample excludes Task effects, destruction and actual host-price settlement; it is not an invoked-control or configured-domain upper.

The benchmark-only Opening no-Action facade distinguishes genuine `ResolutionSkipped` from `FundingUnavailable(TaskInput)` and requires `NotInvoked`, zero effect and no Action observation. Its User/System native witness composes retained Service or canonical cooldown Deadline placement, preserving live Run versus completed-identity nonces, custody/holds and exact rollback without optional policy prices. User pays one nonzero Pipeline fee with no Action receipt; TaskInput is distinct from the ServiceFee/AwaitFunding branch. `execute_opening_policy_no_action` shares that composition with raw-only `opening_policy_no_action_control(f,r,c)`, including User/System selection and Pipeline-aware custody/sink/receipt verification outside timing. User financial waiting, configured-domain maxima and host-price settlement remain separate.

Benchmark-only funding transition composition distinguishes advancing Task-input skip, repeated head suspension and local/global-bound Close, always without Task invocation or Action receipt. Sources author the error policy before creation and retain configured retry/failure bounds; terminal cleanup uses existing consumed-state finalization, with required clean authority publication and committed due-return kept outside rollback. Raw-only `funding_policy_control(t,r,c)` selects User/System and shares this composition and records advancing skip or the actual configured-bound terminal attempt, leaving prior retries/due-return outside timing. It validates local/global precedence without changing limits. Native/reference User coverage separates one Pipeline payment from owner state-hold release and slot cleanup, preserving sovereign custody and post-return rollback. Owner hold/free-balance and slot observations stay outside timing.

`prepare_service_fee_policy_source` authors a native-spend prefix and funded non-native successor before creation under Close-only policy. `execute_running_service_fee_policy_attempt` shares the first Running ServiceFee control/publication between native and raw `running_policy_service_fee(f,r)`. Its facade requires User/Running/cursor 1, real ServiceFee/NotInvoked, zero effect and no Action observation/financial wait. Setup commits the paid prefix; all economic/source checks stay outside timing. This source never substitutes for the following Suspended retry's insolvency admission.

The terminal join includes creation-backed destruction and supplies neither a standalone Pipeline tariff nor qualified prices.

`prepare_liquidity_policy_source` authors each selected error policy before creation; retry-specific wrappers retain their original inputs. Its input-specific helper distinguishes derived recovery bounds from preserved authored Fixed inputs/minimum LP. The source constructor derives a liquidity bound only when explicitly requested for an authored successor, never after creation. An optional authored prefix is likewise installed before creation, enabling native-spend source construction without later balance/lifecycle rewriting.

A benchmark-only invoked-failure facade checks actual Invoked execution and expected failure class through the shared real Task/control body: Temporary selects ContinueNextStep/AbortCycle, while Permanent selects ContinueNextStep/RetryLater without retry or Close. Its caller retains Run/Service or publishes surviving Failed Idle before collecting the actual Action fee. The native outcome witness checks both classes, prefix/custody/hold preservation and rollback. Raw-only `liquidity_failure_policy_control(c,a,r,p)` shares that body, selects Temporary/Permanent source with `p`, requires host pool/input/native-balance snapshots outside timing, and verifies full Contract/certificate and surviving Run versus passive Idle. Unsupported mocks explicitly skip; the joined Task/control/Action cost is not a standalone control tariff.

The shared fixture installer checks the supplied full-body commitment against the existing admission certificate before staging policy; bounded current-Step state is not a full-body comparator. Authored retry bounds are fixed before creation, never rewritten into an admitted body. These helpers do not change production admission. The shared evaluator visits every atom without short-circuiting; physical bounds still depend on predicate kinds and accessed storage geometry.

In production, only `finalize_actor_loaded_inner` calls `finalize_actor_loaded_state`, after checking policy and AwaitFunding resource availability. Benchmark-only facades expose loaded-only and consumed-source measurement. `benchmark_finalize_actor_from_consumed_state` shares `ensure_consumed_finalization_source` with the production consumed-state wrapper and preserves the supplied Close reason; it does not supply resource prices. The terminal zero-Step profile joins intrinsic control, destruction and Service retirement inside one rollback-capable transaction, without adding component prices or changing public admission.

Raw-only extra `close_finalize_policy(c,r)` retains detached `c=0..2` for User peers, `3..5` for last-slot User and `6..8` for System, each ordered Idle/Running/Suspended. Setup performs ordinary admitted detachment before staging maximum policy/monitor records and reconciling holds. Measurement includes view derivation, loaded cancellation/destruction, geometry removal, counts, owner/sovereign cleanup, hold release, events and transaction completion; `r=1` adds outer rollback. The shared destruction verifier checks custody, committed credit, aggregate holds, slot/locator cleanup and global try-state. It excludes source loading, resource qualification, detachment, public dispatch, late-currency refusal and complete topology maxima. Prepared sources do not establish public policy reachability or a production tariff.

`c9` adds one pre-Opening maximum-clean User source with a genuine paid Transfer prefix and retained Service/process/Run. Existing consumed-state intrinsic forwarding finalizes it; ordinary `retire_service_member` follows in the same caller transaction. Retirement is included in this case's recorded joint, never committed as orphaned intermediate state merely to exclude it from timing. `c0..8` keep their detached source and measured boundary. Caller loading, public/resource qualification and terminal Step control remain excluded; no price port, production function or authority rewrite is added.

The retained-Service native falsifier runs actual Closing cancellation under rollback, then authenticates the resulting accumulated-clean Idle/None Contract through the full geometry loader. It separately finalizes the original paid source, observes retained process/Service until ordinary retirement, and checks complete destruction/custody/credit/holds/slots/counters on commit or exact-root restoration on rollback. The profile wrapper now exercises all40 source/outcome/verification contexts. These prove finite topology/transaction conformance, not public policy admission, whole Step terminal control, configured-domain maxima, a tariff or independent backing.

`authenticate_zero_step_service_source` owns the read-only caller prelude: generation/kind-bound semantic loading, identity/hot/admission equality and complete Contract equality. `execute_zero_step_on_service` calls it inside the original transaction and retains the returned semantic authority for financial recovery. Shape/policy qualification, prices and mutation remain downstream; this helper alone is not zero-Step admission. `behavior_runtime_zero_step_source_authentication_is_read_only` rejects seven supplied-argument mismatch classes through both the helper and production wrapper, preserves exact roots, then completes the unchanged readiness.

`execute_zero_step_transition` authenticates empty-Cycle shape and the optional policy source, then qualifies resources before calling shared `execute_zero_step_control`. The semantic transition carries no reserved Weight; its production wrapper returns that reservation separately for unchanged late settlement inside the Service transaction. The benchmark-only facade reuses source authentication, Opening/rearm, actual stored-envelope Pipeline collection, empty-cycle completion and candidate publication. It returns an unfinished residence intent, not a committed Service attempt.

Raw-only extra `zero_step_policy_source_authentication(c)` measures only `authenticate_zero_step_service_source` on eight wide-policy User/System × Manual, AddressEvent, AtTime and Cadenced full-header sources. Setup constructs and loads caller arguments; snapshots and verification stay outside timing. Exact root/returned semantic authority and native/non-native custody remain unchanged. The native wrapper checks verification enabled/disabled with control/Close/inspection prices unavailable. This profile excludes downstream shape/policy qualification, price selection, transition, publication, placement, destruction and caller transaction completion; it is not complete admission or an additive surcharge on a complete wrapper sample.

`zero_step_policy_control(c,q,r)` retains its wide accumulated-clean false/late-completion categories at `q=0/1`. Categories `2/3` supply current-outcome no-monitor false/true, `4/5` current-outcome zero-slot clean false/true, and `6/7` accumulated-without-clean false/true. Expressions are selected before shared source construction and installed before Opening. The common staging helper allocates monitors from actual tracking/clean requirements, not the old case-number assumption. Source authentication checks expression, binding, mode and monitor shape before timing; candidate checks retain typed counter/no-history semantics rather than requiring ten metrics and a monitor for every class.

Raw-only extra `zero_step_policy_control(c,q,r)` uses User then System across Manual, AddressEvent, AtTime and Cadenced sources. Ordinary readiness precedes staged policy installation. Its shared no-policy base now funds the complete prospective Contract rather than a windowless creation estimate or empty-plan Action budget, and asserts capacity after readiness. This does not qualify complete optional-policy fee backing.

`prefund_zero_step_creation` serves the existing minimal and full-header factories. It projects the complete future Contract/id/class through the existing admission-certificate and geometry producers without installing either result. The future actor and sovereign must be unpublished; User native funding covers MinUserBalance, the complete Pipeline fee and initial Trigger fee. System remains fee-exempt, preserving only each source's existing custody witness. Public creation independently produces its Head; `assert_zero_step_creation_prefund` compares every encoded Head field and exact sovereign native balance, then readiness uses a rechecked Trigger quote. Signed non-native AddressEvent ingress still follows creation.

`zero_step_fixtures_prefund_complete_contract_before_creation` covers persistent User/System minimal Manual and full AddressEvent. The real User Creation Fee callback observes the native budget before publication; both classes then execute complete inner workers through existing fee/hold/custody/placement verifiers. The old preparation fails the same pre-publication budget assertion. Minimal User no longer carries a redundant second Pipeline buffer. These are native fixture-method checks, not physical class containment, a production correction or a before-creation guarantee for later staged policy.

The episode begins at zero; `q=0` is a wide false policy and `q=1` an independent late completion clause. New class pairs preserve false/true outcome selection without inventing Action success, clean economic activity or a Running carrier. Accumulated-without-clean `q=7` has an unreachable Action-threshold disjunct and Completed-at-one, so it validly selects accumulated mode while completing on the first genuine empty Cycle. Verification checks exact retained-envelope payment, custody, authenticated completion-only counters and whole-root rollback; fixture destruction/Service retirement is outside measurement. The three optional host price ports may remain unavailable. Public admission, Service publication/placement, destruction, late actual-Weight settlement and complete policy fee backing are excluded; native production-path tests retain those transactional obligations.

`publish_idle_cycle_completion` is the shared nonterminal semantic-publication/passive-placement owner for zero-Step and completed nonempty Idle cycles inside the caller's attempt transaction. Non-Idle retry/continuation still publishes through its existing branch. The StopCycle native witness covers User/System, Close/continue and commit/rollback; a surviving Actor retains identity, policy and holds while publishing its monitor and retiring current Service.
